/**
 * Module: recentCommits.ts
 * Fetches recent commit history and identifies volatile (frequently touched) files.
 * Formats structured XML metadata embedded into Part 1 of the RepoMix snapshot.
 */

export interface CommitFileChange {
  path: string;
  status: 'modified' | 'added' | 'removed' | 'renamed' | string;
}

export interface RecentCommitInfo {
  hash: string;
  date: string;
  author: string;
  title: string;
  files: CommitFileChange[];
}

export interface VolatileFileInfo {
  path: string;
  touchCount: number;
  latestStatus: string;
}

export interface RecentCommitsResult {
  commits: RecentCommitInfo[];
  volatileFiles: VolatileFileInfo[];
  xmlContent: string;
}

/**
 * Escapes characters for XML attribute and text compatibility.
 */
export function escapeXml(str: string): string {
  if (!str) return '';
  return str
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&apos;');
}

/**
 * Formats recent commits and volatile files into a structured XML block.
 */
export function formatRecentCommitsXml(
  commits: RecentCommitInfo[],
  volatileFiles: VolatileFileInfo[]
): string {
  if (!commits || commits.length === 0) {
    return '';
  }

  const sections: string[] = ['<recent_commits>'];
  for (const c of commits) {
    const authorAttr = escapeXml(c.author);
    const dateAttr = escapeXml(c.date);
    const hashAttr = escapeXml(c.hash);
    const titleText = escapeXml(c.title);

    sections.push(`  <commit hash="${hashAttr}" date="${dateAttr}" author="${authorAttr}">`);
    sections.push(`    <title>${titleText}</title>`);
    if (c.files.length > 0) {
      sections.push('    <files>');
      for (const f of c.files) {
        sections.push(`      <file path="${escapeXml(f.path)}" status="${escapeXml(f.status)}" />`);
      }
      sections.push('    </files>');
    }
    sections.push('  </commit>');
  }
  sections.push('</recent_commits>');

  if (volatileFiles && volatileFiles.length > 0) {
    sections.push('<volatile_files>');
    for (const v of volatileFiles) {
      sections.push(
        `  <file path="${escapeXml(v.path)}" touchCount="${v.touchCount}" latestStatus="${escapeXml(v.latestStatus)}" />`
      );
    }
    sections.push('</volatile_files>');
  }

  return sections.join('\n');
}

/**
 * Fetches recent commit metadata and file changes from GitHub REST API in parallel.
 * Aggregates touch frequencies to discover volatile files.
 * Always catches errors gracefully and never throws.
 */
export async function fetchRecentCommitsAndVolatileFiles(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  token: string,
  count = 3
): Promise<RecentCommitsResult> {
  const safeCount = Math.min(5, Math.max(1, count));
  const fallback: RecentCommitsResult = { commits: [], volatileFiles: [], xmlContent: '' };

  try {
    const listUrl = `https://api.github.com/repos/${encodeURIComponent(owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo)}/commits?sha=${encodeURIComponent(branch)}&per_page=${safeCount}`;
    const listRes = await fetch(listUrl, {
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'Cloudflare-Worker-gemini-git-bridge',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
        'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.v3+json',
      },
    });

    if (!listRes.ok) {
      return fallback;
    }

    const listData: any = await listRes.json();
    if (!Array.isArray(listData) || listData.length === 0) {
      return fallback;
    }

    const detailPromises = listData.slice(0, safeCount).map(async (c: any): Promise<RecentCommitInfo> => {
      const sha: string = c.sha || '';
      const rawMsg: string = c.commit?.message || '';
      const title = rawMsg.split('\n')[0] || '';
      const author: string = c.commit?.author?.name || c.author?.login || 'unknown';
      const date: string = c.commit?.author?.date || c.commit?.committer?.date || '';

      try {
        const detailUrl = `https://api.github.com/repos/${encodeURIComponent(owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo)}/commits/${encodeURIComponent(sha)}`;
        const detailRes = await fetch(detailUrl, {
          headers: {
            'User-Agent': 'Cloudflare-Worker-gemini-git-bridge',
            'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
            'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.v3+json',
          },
        });

        if (detailRes.ok) {
          const detailData: any = await detailRes.json();
          const detailMsg: string = detailData.commit?.message || rawMsg;
          const detailTitle = detailMsg.split('\n')[0] || title;
          const detailAuthor: string = detailData.commit?.author?.name || detailData.author?.login || author;
          const detailDate: string = detailData.commit?.author?.date || detailData.commit?.committer?.date || date;
          const files: CommitFileChange[] = Array.isArray(detailData.files)
            ? detailData.files.map((f: any) => ({
                path: String(f.filename || ''),
                status: String(f.status || 'modified'),
              })).filter((f: CommitFileChange) => f.path.length > 0)
            : [];

          return {
            hash: sha,
            date: detailDate,
            author: detailAuthor,
            title: detailTitle,
            files,
          };
        }
      } catch {
        // Fall back to commit list metadata
      }

      return {
        hash: sha,
        date,
        author,
        title,
        files: [],
      };
    });

    const commits = await Promise.all(detailPromises);

    // Aggregate volatile files across all commits (sorted latest to oldest)
    const fileMap = new Map<string, { path: string; touchCount: number; latestStatus: string; latestCommitIndex: number }>();
    for (let cIdx = 0; cIdx < commits.length; cIdx++) {
      const commit = commits[cIdx];
      for (const f of commit.files) {
        if (!f.path) continue;
        const existing = fileMap.get(f.path);
        if (!existing) {
          fileMap.set(f.path, {
            path: f.path,
            touchCount: 1,
            latestStatus: f.status,
            latestCommitIndex: cIdx,
          });
        } else {
          existing.touchCount++;
        }
      }
    }

    const volatileFiles: VolatileFileInfo[] = Array.from(fileMap.values())
      .sort((a, b) => {
        if (b.touchCount !== a.touchCount) {
          return b.touchCount - a.touchCount;
        }
        return a.latestCommitIndex - b.latestCommitIndex;
      })
      .map(({ path, touchCount, latestStatus }) => ({ path, touchCount, latestStatus }));

    const xmlContent = formatRecentCommitsXml(commits, volatileFiles);

    return {
      commits,
      volatileFiles,
      xmlContent,
    };
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

# How-To Guide: Gemini-to-GitHub Bridge (.info)

Praktische Schritt-für-Schritt-Anleitung für den täglichen Workflow: Vom Repomix-Kontext über das Prompting in Gemini Web / AI Studio bis zum 1-Click Git-Commit via Userscript & Cloudflare Edge Worker.

---

## 1. Voraussetzungen & Einmaliges Setup

1. **Userscript im Browser:**
   - [`gemini-bridge.user.js`](file:///Y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/gemini-bridge.user.js) in Tampermonkey oder Violentmonkey installieren / aktualisieren.
   - `gemini.google.com` oder `aistudio.google.com` im Browser öffnen (oder neu laden mit `F5`).
2. **HUD-Konfiguration (Zahnrad / Bridge Drawer im Browser):**
   - **Worker URL:** z. B. `https://gemini-git-bridge.<deine-subdomain>.workers.dev`
   - **Bridge Secret:** Dein gesetztes Shared Secret (aus Cloudflare)
   - **Default Repo:** `owner/repo` (z. B. `meinUser/meinProjekt`)
   - **Default Branch:** z. B. `main` oder `preview`

---

## 2. Der Standard-Workflow (In 3 Schritten)

### Schritt 1: Codebase als Kontext bereitstellen (Repomix / Mix Repo)
1. Im HUD-Drawer auf **"📦 Mix Repo"** klicken (oder lokal Repomix ausführen).
2. Das Userscript lädt das Repository über den Worker herunter, filtert Binärdateien & Node-Modules, zerlegt den Code atomar in Chunks ($\le$ 85k Zeichen als `.txt`) und fügt ihn direkt in den Chat ein.
3. Gemini besitzt nun den vollständigen Dateibaum und den aktuellen Code im Kontext.

---

### Schritt 2: Gemini nach Code-Änderungen fragen (Prompting)

Damit das Userscript und der Worker die Änderungen automatisch erkennen und auf GitHub committen können, muss Gemini die Antwort im passenden Format ausgeben.

#### Variante A: V2 Patch-JSON (Empfohlen – Atomar & Kompakt)
Ideal für Änderungen über mehrere Dateien hinweg in einem einzigen zusammenhängenden Commit.

> **Minimaler Prompt für Gemini (ohne Kontext-Bloat):**
> *"Gib die Änderungen ausschließlich als Patch-JSON mit branch, message und files (search/replace) aus."*

Gemini antwortet dann in diesem Schema:
```json
{
  "branch": "main",
  "message": "feat: button styling and layout fix",
  "files": [
    {
      "path": "src/components/Button.tsx",
      "type": "modify",
      "search": "const color = 'red';",
      "replace": "const color = 'blue';"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/utils/helpers.ts",
      "type": "modify",
      "search": "export const MAX_ITEMS = 10;",
      "replace": "export const MAX_ITEMS = 25;"
    }
  ]
}
```

*Unterstützte `type`-Aktionen im JSON:*
- `modify`: Teiländerung einer bestehenden Datei via `search` und `replace`
- `create`: Neue Datei erzeugen via `content` (vollständiger Dateiinhalt)
- `delete`: Datei im GitHub-Repository löschen

#### Variante B: SEARCH / REPLACE Blöcke (Klassisch / Markdown)
Ideal für schnelle Einzeländerungen oder wenn Gemini Markdown-Blöcke bevorzugt.

> **Minimaler Prompt:**
> *"Gib die Änderung als SEARCH/REPLACE Block mit filepath an."*

Gemini antwortet:
```typescript
// filepath: src/components/Button.tsx
<<<<<<< SEARCH
const color = 'red';
=======
const color = 'blue';
>>>>>>> REPLACE
```

---

### Schritt 3: Patches anwenden (Harvested Patches Drawer)

Sobald Gemini die Antwort im Chat generiert, erkennt das Userscript die Patches automatisch im Hintergrund ("Harvested Patches"):

1. **Option A – Alle nacheinander ausführen (`⚡ Apply All (N)`):**
   - Klicke im Queue-Header auf **`⚡ Apply All`**.
   - Das Skript wendet alle gesammelten Patches **chronologisch von alt nach neu (FIFO)** an.
   - **Fail-Fast:** Tritt bei Patch 2 ein Fehler auf (z. B. Merge-Konflikt oder Linter-Fehler), **stoppt die Ausführung sofort**. Alle Folge-Patches bleiben unangetastet in der Queue.
   - Jeder erfolgreich angewendete Patch wird sofort aus der Queue entfernt.

2. **Option B – Einzeln anwenden (`Apply`):**
   - Jeder Patch besitzt eine Diff-Vorschau und einen eigenen **`Apply`**-Button.
   - **Reihenfolge bei manuellem Klick:** Immer von **alt nach neu (chronologisch)** anwenden, da spätere Patches auf den Änderungen früherer Patches aufbauen können.

3. **Queue leeren (`🗑️ Clear`):**
   - Mit einem Klick auf **`Clear`** verwirfst du alle aufgelaufenen Patches auf einmal.

---

## 3. Wichtige Regeln & Hard Guardrails

### ❌ Niemals Platzhalter verwenden!
- Der Cloudflare Worker besitzt eine strikte **Anti-Placeholder Guardrail**.
- Code mit Kommentaren wie:
  - `// ... existing code ...`
  - `/* ... rest of code ... */`
  - `# ... remaining code ...`
  wird vom Worker **sofort mit `400 Bad Request` abgelehnt**!
- **Hintergrund:** Dies schützt das Repository davor, dass versehentlich unvollständiger KI-Code bestehende Funktionen löscht.

### 🔄 Optimistic Concurrency & Head-OID
- Vor jedem Commit verifiziert der Worker die aktuelle Git HEAD-OID des Branches.
- Bei `Apply All` wird die resultierende neue OID des vorherigen Commits direkt an den nächsten Commit übergeben.
- Dadurch sind Race-Conditions und Versionskonflikte ausgeschlossen.

---

## 4. Schnelle Fehlerbehebung (Troubleshooting)

| Problem | Ursache | Lösung |
|---|---|---|
| `400 Bad Request: Placeholder detected` | Gemini hat `// ... existing code ...` verwendet | Gemini auffordern: *"Schreibe den Code vollständig ohne Auslassungs-Kommentare oder nutze SEARCH/REPLACE"* |
| `PREFLIGHT_FAILED / Conflict` | Such-Block (`search`) stimmt nicht exakt mit dem Dateiinhalt im Branch überein | Branch-Stand prüfen oder Gemini den aktuellen Codeausschnitt erneut vorgeben |
| Kein `Apply`-Button / Queue bleibt leer | Kein relativer Dateipfad oder ungültiges JSON | Sicherstellen, dass die erste Zeile `// filepath: ...` lautet oder valides JSON vorliegt |
| `401 Unauthorized` | Bridge-Secret im HUD ist falsch oder fehlt | Im HUD-Drawer das `Bridge Secret` mit dem im Cloudflare Worker hinterlegten Secret abgleichen |
| Branch existiert nicht | Zielbranch auf GitHub nicht vorhanden | Branch auf GitHub anlegen oder im HUD auf `main` bzw. `preview` umstellen |

================
File: REPOMIX_INSTRUCTIONS.md
================
# RepoMix & Bridge Instruktionen für Gemini

> **Status:** Diese Instruktionen werden vom Edge-Worker automatisch an den Anfang jedes RepoMixes (`<instructions>...</instructions>`) eingebettet. Dadurch entsteht **kein zusätzlicher Datei-Upload** (wichtig für das 10-Dateien-Limit in Gemini Web) und das Modell liest die Regeln noch vor dem Dateibaum.

---

## 1. Universelles 2-Modi-Verhalten (Keine Prompt-Verwirrung)

Damit die eingebetteten Regeln niemals mit deinem eigentlichen Benutzer-Prompt kollidieren, unterscheidet Gemini strikt zwischen zwei Aufgabenarten:

* **Modus 1: Reine Lese- & Analyse-Aufgaben (Read-Only):**
  Wenn du Fragen stellst, Code-Erklärungen wünschst, Architekturen besprichst oder ein Review anforderst:
  * Gemini antwortet ganz normal und flüssig in natürlicher Sprache (z. B. auf Deutsch).
  * Es werden **keine** Patches oder leere JSON-Blöcke erzeugt.
* **Modus 2: Codeänderungen & Implementierungen (Modification):**
  **Nur wenn** du explizit Codeänderungen, Fehlerbehebungen, Refactorings oder neue Dateien verlangst:
  * Gemini gibt niemals ganze bestehende Dateien neu aus.
  * Gemini verwendet **keine Platzhalter** (`// ... existing code ...`).
  * Gemini formatiert Änderungen strikt nach Format A (JSON) oder Format B (Search/Replace).

---

## 2. Struktur des RepoMix-Snapshots

Jede generierte Textdatei enthält eine strukturierte XML-Momentaufnahme:
1. `<repository repo="..." branch="..." headOid="..." part="1" totalParts="N">`: Basis-Metadaten.
2. `<instructions>`: Die universellen Systemleitplanken direkt am Dokumentanfang.
3. `<file_tree>`: Vollständiger Dateibaum des Projekts (in Part 1).
4. `<files><file path="...">...<![CDATA[...]]></file></files>`: Reale Dateiinhalte.
5. **Multi-Part Splitting:** Bei großen Repositories teilen sich die Dateien auf Parts 1 bis N auf; alle Parts gehören zusammen.

---

## 3. Ausgabe-Formate bei Codeänderungen (GitHub-Bridge)

### Format A: V2 Patch-JSON (Standard für atomare Multi-File Commits)
```json
{
  "branch": "main",
  "expectedHeadOid": "40_STELLIGE_HEAD_OID_AUS_DEM_REPOSITORY_TAG",
  "message": "feat: präzise beschreibung der änderung",
  "files": [
    {
      "path": "relativer/pfad/zur/datei.ts",
      "type": "modify",
      "search": "// exakter bisheriger Codeausschnitt aus dem RepoMix",
      "replace": "// neuer ersetzter Code"
    },
    {
      "path": "relativer/pfad/zu/neuer_datei.ts",
      "type": "create",
      "content": "// vollständiger Inhalt der neuen Datei"
    }
  ]
}
```

### Format B: Search/Replace Blöcke (Für Einzelfiles)
```text
// filepath: relativer/pfad/zur/datei.ts
<<<<<<< SEARCH
exakter bisheriger Codeausschnitt aus dem RepoMix
=======
neuer ersetzter Code
>>>>>>> REPLACE
```

---

## 4. Strikte Guardrails
* **Verbot von Auslassungen:** Niemals `// ... existing code ...`, `/* rest unchanged */` oder `# ...` verwenden.
* **Exakte Übereinstimmung:** Der `search`-Block muss exakt Zeichen für Zeichen mit dem RepoMix übereinstimmen (inkl. Einrückung).
* **Single Source of Truth:** Nur vorhandene Pfade und Signaturen aus dem `<file_tree>` verwenden.

/**
 * Chunk-based UTF-8 to Base64 encoding.
 * Processes bytes in chunks (32KB) to prevent "Maximum call stack size exceeded" errors
 * that occur with large files when using String.fromCharCode.apply on large arrays.
 */
export function utf8ToBase64(str: string): string {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
  const CHUNK_SIZE = 0x8000; // 32768 bytes
  let binary = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += CHUNK_SIZE) {
    const chunk = bytes.subarray(i, i + CHUNK_SIZE);
    binary += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, chunk as unknown as number[]);
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

/**
 * Decodes Base64 string back to UTF-8 string safely.
 */
export function base64ToUtf8(base64: string): string {
  const binary = atob(base64);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) {
    bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
  }
  return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
}

import type { GitHubCommitResult } from './types';

const GITHUB_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT = 'https://api.github.com/graphql';

/**
 * Generic GitHub GraphQL executor.
 */
export async function executeGraphQL<T = any>(
  query: string,
  variables: Record<string, any>,
  token: string
): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(GITHUB_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'User-Agent': 'Cloudflare-Worker-gemini-git-bridge',
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json'
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables })
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    throw new Error(`GitHub GraphQL HTTP error ${res.status} (${res.statusText}): ${text}`);
  }

  const json: any = await res.json();
  if (json.errors && json.errors.length > 0) {
    const msg = json.errors.map((e: any) => e.message).join(' | ');
    throw new Error(`GitHub GraphQL error: ${msg}`);
  }

  return json.data as T;
}

/**
 * Retrieves the current HEAD commit OID for a branch.
 */
export async function getBranchHeadOid(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  token: string
): Promise<{ headOid: string; resolvedBranch: string }> {
  const qualifiedBranch = branch.startsWith('refs/heads/') ? branch : `refs/heads/${branch}`;

  const query = `
    query GetBranchHead($owner: String!, $repo: String!, $branch: String!) {
      repository(owner: $owner, name: $repo) {
        ref(qualifiedName: $branch) {
          name
          target {
            oid
          }
        }
        defaultBranchRef {
          name
          target {
            oid
          }
        }
      }
    }
  `;

  const data = await executeGraphQL<any>(query, { owner, repo, branch: qualifiedBranch }, token);
  const repository = data?.repository;

  if (!repository) {
    throw new Error(`Repository "${owner}/${repo}" was not found or is not accessible with the provided token.`);
  }

  if (repository.ref?.target?.oid) {
    return {
      headOid: repository.ref.target.oid,
      resolvedBranch: repository.ref.name
    };
  }

  // If specific branch not found, but branch was default/empty or fallback
  if (!branch && repository.defaultBranchRef?.target?.oid) {
    return {
      headOid: repository.defaultBranchRef.target.oid,
      resolvedBranch: repository.defaultBranchRef.name
    };
  }

  throw new Error(`Branch "${branch}" (or "${qualifiedBranch}") not found in repository "${owner}/${repo}".`);
}

/**
 * Fetches existing file content from GitHub using GraphQL Blob or REST raw content fallback.
 */
export async function fetchFileContent(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  path: string,
  token: string
): Promise<string> {
  // 1. Try GraphQL Blob object expression
  const expression = `${branch}:${path}`;
  const query = `
    query GetFileBlob($owner: String!, $repo: String!, $expression: String!) {
      repository(owner: $owner, name: $repo) {
        object(expression: $expression) {
          ... on Blob {
            text
            isTruncated
          }
        }
      }
    }
  `;

  try {
    const data = await executeGraphQL<any>(query, { owner, repo, expression }, token);
    const blob = data?.repository?.object;
    if (blob && typeof blob.text === 'string' && !blob.isTruncated) {
      return blob.text;
    }
  } catch {
    // If GraphQL blob query failed, proceed to REST fallback
  }

  // 2. REST raw contents fallback
  const encodedPath = path.split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/');
  const restUrl = `https://api.github.com/repos/${encodeURIComponent(owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo)}/contents/${encodedPath}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`;
  const res = await fetch(restUrl, {
    headers: {
      'User-Agent': 'Cloudflare-Worker-gemini-git-bridge',
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
      'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.raw+json'
    }
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Failed to fetch file "${path}" from "${owner}/${repo}" on branch "${branch}": HTTP ${res.status}`);
  }

  return await res.text();
}

/**
 * Executes createCommitOnBranch mutation.
 */
export async function createCommitOnBranch(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  expectedHeadOid: string,
  message: string,
  additions: { path: string; contents: string }[],
  deletions: { path: string }[],
  token: string
): Promise<GitHubCommitResult> {
  const mutation = `
    mutation CreateCommitOnBranch($input: CreateCommitOnBranchInput!) {
      createCommitOnBranch(input: $input) {
        commit {
          oid
          url
          messageHeadline
        }
      }
    }
  `;

  const headline = message.split('\n')[0].trim();
  const body = message.includes('\n') ? message.split('\n').slice(1).join('\n').trim() : undefined;

  const fileChanges: Record<string, any> = {};
  if (additions.length > 0) {
    fileChanges.additions = additions;
  }
  if (deletions.length > 0) {
    fileChanges.deletions = deletions;
  }

  const variables = {
    input: {
      branch: {
        repositoryNameWithOwner: `${owner}/${repo}`,
        branchName: branch
      },
      expectedHeadOid,
      message: {
        headline,
        body: body || null
      },
      fileChanges
    }
  };

  const data = await executeGraphQL<any>(mutation, variables, token);
  const commit = data?.createCommitOnBranch?.commit;

  if (!commit?.oid) {
    throw new Error('createCommitOnBranch did not return commit information.');
  }

  return {
    oid: commit.oid,
    url: commit.url,
    messageHeadline: commit.messageHeadline
  };
}

File: _v1_backup/cf-gemini-git-bridge/src/index.ts

import type { Env, CommitRequest } from './types';
import { utf8ToBase64 } from './encoding';
import { resolveFileContent } from './patch';
import { getBranchHeadOid, fetchFileContent, createCommitOnBranch, executeGraphQL } from './github';

const CORS_HEADERS: Record<string, string> = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type, Authorization, x-bridge-secret',
  'Access-Control-Max-Age': '86400',
};

function jsonResponse(data: any, status = 200): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), {
    status,
    headers: {
      ...CORS_HEADERS,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json'
    }
  });
}

function errorResponse(message: string, status = 400, details?: any): Response {
  return jsonResponse({
    success: false,
    error: message,
    ...(details ? { details } : {})
  }, status);
}

function verifyAuth(request: Request, env: Env): boolean {
  if (!env.BRIDGE_SECRET) {
    return false;
  }

  const customHeader = request.headers.get('x-bridge-secret');
  if (customHeader && customHeader === env.BRIDGE_SECRET) {
    return true;
  }

  const authHeader = request.headers.get('Authorization');
  if (authHeader) {
    const token = authHeader.replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, '').trim();
    if (token === env.BRIDGE_SECRET) {
      return true;
    }
  }

  return false;
}

export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    // 1. Handle CORS Preflight
    if (request.method === 'OPTIONS') {
      return new Response(null, {
        status: 204,
        headers: CORS_HEADERS
      });
    }

    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const path = url.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';

    // 2. Health check endpoint (public)
    if (request.method === 'GET' && (path === '/' || path === '/health')) {
      return jsonResponse({
        status: 'ok',
        service: 'gemini-git-bridge',
        version: '1.0.0',
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        hasBridgeSecret: Boolean(env.BRIDGE_SECRET),
        hasGithubToken: Boolean(env.GITHUB_TOKEN)
      });
    }

    // 3. Authenticate all protected endpoints
    if (!verifyAuth(request, env)) {
      return errorResponse('Unauthorized: Invalid or missing BRIDGE_SECRET in Authorization or x-bridge-secret header', 401);
    }

    if (!env.GITHUB_TOKEN) {
      return errorResponse('Server configuration error: GITHUB_TOKEN secret is not set in Worker environment.', 500);
    }

    try {
      // 4. Commit / Push Relay Endpoint (Supports POST /push, /commit, /api/commit)
      if (request.method === 'POST' && (path === '/push' || path === '/commit' || path === '/api/commit' || path === '/')) {
        const body = (await request.json().catch(() => {
          throw new Error('Malformed JSON payload');
        })) as CommitRequest;

        // Parse owner and repo
        let owner = body.owner || '';
        let repo = body.repo || '';

        if (!owner && repo.includes('/')) {
          const parts = repo.split('/');
          owner = parts[0];
          repo = parts.slice(1).join('/');
        }

        if (!owner || !repo) {
          return errorResponse('Missing required fields: Repository must be specified as "owner/repo" or separate "owner" and "repo".');
        }

        const commitMessage = body.commitMessage || body.message;
        if (!commitMessage) {
          return errorResponse('Missing required field: "commitMessage" (or "message") must be specified.');
        }

        const targetBranch = body.targetBranch || body.branch || 'main';
        const fileChanges = body.changes || body.files || [];
        const deletions = body.deletions || [];

        if (fileChanges.length === 0 && deletions.length === 0) {
          return errorResponse('Commit must include at least one entry in "changes" or "files" or "deletions".');
        }

        // 4a. Resolve HEAD Commit OID for target branch to prevent race conditions
        const { headOid, resolvedBranch } = await getBranchHeadOid(owner, repo, targetBranch, env.GITHUB_TOKEN);

        // 4b. Resolve File Changes (Apply Search/Replace, Hard Guardrail, & encode to Base64)
        const additions: { path: string; contents: string }[] = [];

        for (const fileInput of fileChanges) {
          if (!fileInput.path) {
            throw new Error('Every file entry must contain a "path" property.');
          }

          const contentFetcher = async (filePath: string) => {
            return await fetchFileContent(owner, repo, resolvedBranch, filePath, env.GITHUB_TOKEN);
          };

          const resolvedText = await resolveFileContent(fileInput, contentFetcher);
          const encodedBase64 = utf8ToBase64(resolvedText);

          additions.push({
            path: fileInput.path,
            contents: encodedBase64
          });
        }

        // 4c. Format Deletions
        const formattedDeletions: { path: string }[] = deletions.map((del) => {
          return typeof del === 'string' ? { path: del } : { path: del.path };
        });

        // 4d. Execute createCommitOnBranch via GitHub GraphQL
        const commitResult = await createCommitOnBranch(
          owner,
          repo,
          resolvedBranch,
          headOid,
          commitMessage,
          additions,
          formattedDeletions,
          env.GITHUB_TOKEN
        );

        return jsonResponse({
          success: true,
          commit: commitResult,
          branch: resolvedBranch,
          repository: `${owner}/${repo}`,
          filesChanged: additions.length + formattedDeletions.length
        });
      }

      // 5. Raw GraphQL Proxy Endpoint
      if (request.method === 'POST' && path === '/graphql') {
        const body: any = await request.json().catch(() => {
          throw new Error('Malformed JSON payload');
        });

        if (!body.query) {
          return errorResponse('Missing required field: "query" in GraphQL request.');
        }

        const result = await executeGraphQL(body.query, body.variables || {}, env.GITHUB_TOKEN);
        return jsonResponse({ success: true, data: result });
      }

      return errorResponse(`Endpoint not found: ${request.method} ${path}`, 404);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Request processing error:', err);
      return errorResponse(err.message || 'Internal server error', 500, {
        stack: err.stack
      });
    }
  }
};

import type { FileChangeInput, PatchItem } from './types';

/**
 * Hard Guardrail: Patterns indicating AI placeholder text that would truncate code.
 */
const LLM_PLACEHOLDER_PATTERNS = [
  /\/\/\s*\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of|remaining|other)?\s*code\s*\.{0,3}/i,
  /#\s*\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of|remaining|other)?\s*code\s*\.{0,3}/i,
  /\/\*\s*\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of|remaining|other)?\s*code\s*\.{0,3}\s*\*\//i,
  /<!--\s*\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of|remaining|other)?\s*code\s*\.{0,3}\s*-->/i,
  /\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of)\s*code\s*\.{3,}/i,
  /\/\/\s*\.{3,}\s*$/m,
];

/**
 * Validates that file content does NOT contain LLM placeholder comments.
 * Throws an error immediately if detected.
 */
export function validateNoPlaceholders(content: string, filePath: string): void {
  for (const pattern of LLM_PLACEHOLDER_PATTERNS) {
    if (pattern.test(content)) {
      throw new Error(
        `Hard Guardrail Rejection: File "${filePath}" contains forbidden LLM placeholder text (e.g. "// ... existing code ..."). Commit rejected to prevent accidental code truncation.`
      );
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Extracts <<<<<<< SEARCH ... ======= ... >>>>>>> REPLACE blocks from text.
 */
export function parseSearchReplaceBlocks(text: string): PatchItem[] {
  const blocks: PatchItem[] = [];
  const regex = /<{5,9}\s*(?:SEARCH|ORIGINAL|HEAD)?\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n={5,9}\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n>{5,9}\s*(?:REPLACE|MODIFIED|UPDATED)?/g;
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;

  while ((match = regex.exec(text)) !== null) {
    blocks.push({
      search: match[1],
      replace: match[2]
    });
  }

  return blocks;
}

/**
 * Applies a single search & replace transformation to a file content string.
 * Handles exact matches, CRLF vs LF differences, and trimmed line matches.
 */
export function applySearchReplace(original: string, search: string, replace: string, filePath: string = ''): string {
  // 1. Direct match
  if (original.includes(search)) {
    return original.replace(search, replace);
  }

  // 2. Normalized newline match (CRLF -> LF)
  const normOriginal = original.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
  const normSearch = search.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
  const normReplace = replace.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');

  if (normOriginal.includes(normSearch)) {
    const updated = normOriginal.replace(normSearch, normReplace);
    if (original.includes('\r\n')) {
      return updated.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n');
    }
    return updated;
  }

  // 3. Relaxed line-by-line trimmed match
  const origLines = normOriginal.split('\n');
  const searchLines = normSearch.split('\n');

  let matchStart = -1;
  for (let i = 0; i <= origLines.length - searchLines.length; i++) {
    let matches = true;
    for (let j = 0; j < searchLines.length; j++) {
      if (origLines[i + j].trim() !== searchLines[j].trim()) {
        matches = false;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (matches) {
      matchStart = i;
      break;
    }
  }

  if (matchStart !== -1) {
    const before = origLines.slice(0, matchStart).join('\n');
    const after = origLines.slice(matchStart + searchLines.length).join('\n');
    const updated = (before ? before + '\n' : '') + normReplace + (after ? '\n' + after : '');
    if (original.includes('\r\n')) {
      return updated.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n');
    }
    return updated;
  }

  const snippet = search.length > 200 ? `${search.slice(0, 200)}...` : search;
  throw new Error(`Search block could not be resolved for file "${filePath}". Target snippet:\n${snippet}`);
}

/**
 * Resolves a FileChangeInput into full updated file content.
 * If the input specifies full content without patches, it validates against placeholders and returns it directly.
 * If search/replace patches or blocks are present, it fetches existing file content, applies them, and validates the result.
 */
export async function resolveFileContent(
  fileInput: FileChangeInput,
  existingContentFetcher: (path: string) => Promise<string>
): Promise<string> {
  const patchesToApply: PatchItem[] = [];

  // Check explicit single search/replace
  if (typeof fileInput.search === 'string' && typeof fileInput.replace === 'string') {
    patchesToApply.push({ search: fileInput.search, replace: fileInput.replace });
  }

  // Check array of patches
  if (Array.isArray(fileInput.patches)) {
    for (const p of fileInput.patches) {
      if (typeof p.search === 'string' && typeof p.replace === 'string') {
        patchesToApply.push(p);
      }
    }
  }

  // Check diff or content for <<<<<<< SEARCH blocks
  const diffToCheck = fileInput.diff || fileInput.content || '';
  if (diffToCheck.includes('<<<<<<<') || diffToCheck.includes('=====')) {
    const parsed = parseSearchReplaceBlocks(diffToCheck);
    patchesToApply.push(...parsed);
  }

  // If no search/replace patches found, treat as full file content replacement/creation
  if (patchesToApply.length === 0) {
    if (typeof fileInput.content === 'string') {
      validateNoPlaceholders(fileInput.content, fileInput.path);
      return fileInput.content;
    }
    throw new Error(`File change for "${fileInput.path}" must provide either "content" or search/replace patches.`);
  }

  // Validate that replace blocks do not contain placeholders
  for (const patch of patchesToApply) {
    validateNoPlaceholders(patch.replace, fileInput.path);
  }

  // Fetch existing content
  const existingContent = await existingContentFetcher(fileInput.path);
  let resolved = existingContent;

  for (const patch of patchesToApply) {
    resolved = applySearchReplace(resolved, patch.search, patch.replace, fileInput.path);
  }

  // Validate the final resolved file
  validateNoPlaceholders(resolved, fileInput.path);

  return resolved;
}

export interface Env {
  BRIDGE_SECRET: string;
  GITHUB_TOKEN: string;
}

export interface PatchItem {
  search: string;
  replace: string;
}

export interface FileChangeInput {
  path: string;
  content?: string;
  search?: string;
  replace?: string;
  patches?: PatchItem[];
  diff?: string;
}

export interface FileDeletionInput {
  path: string;
}

export interface CommitRequest {
  owner?: string;
  repo: string; // e.g. "alexanderpeschkaGit/Portfolio_VJ_Brave_V2_0" or "Portfolio_VJ_Brave_V2_0"
  branch?: string;
  baseBranch?: string;
  targetBranch?: string;
  message?: string;
  commitMessage?: string;
  files?: FileChangeInput[];
  changes?: FileChangeInput[];
  deletions?: (string | FileDeletionInput)[];
}

export interface GitHubCommitResult {
  oid: string;
  url: string;
  messageHeadline: string;
}

# AGENTS.md — Gemini-to-GitHub Bridge

## Project Context
Gemini-to-GitHub Bridge is a two-tier developer utility enabling 1-click commits and code patch synchronization from browser-based LLM chats (Google Gemini and Google AI Studio) directly into GitHub repositories:
1. **Frontend Layer (`gemini-bridge.user.js`)**: A browser userscript (Tampermonkey / Violentmonkey) running on `gemini.google.com` and `aistudio.google.com`. It scans LLM code blocks, detects file paths & diff blocks, provides an in-chat commit modal/button, and dispatches JSON payloads to the Cloudflare Worker.
2. **Backend Edge Worker (`cf-gemini-git-bridge/`)**: A serverless Cloudflare Worker written in TypeScript. It handles authentication, resolves target branch HEAD OIDs to prevent race conditions, applies in-memory Search/Replace patches against current GitHub blobs, enforces strict guardrails against LLM placeholder text, and performs atomic multi-file commits via GitHub GraphQL API v4 (`createCommitOnBranch`).

## Codebase Structure
- `gemini-bridge.user.js`: Browser userscript (DOM scanners, diff parser, configuration modal, API dispatch).
- `README.md`: Central documentation for installation, configuration, and API specifications.
- `cf-gemini-git-bridge/`: Cloudflare Worker directory.
  - `wrangler.jsonc`: Cloudflare Worker configuration (`compatibility_date >= 2026-08-04`).
  - `package.json` / `tsconfig.json`: Dependencies and TypeScript config.
  - `src/index.ts`: Worker entry point, routing (`/health`, `/push`, `/commit`, `/graphql`), CORS handling, and authentication.
  - `src/github.ts`: GitHub GraphQL API operations (HEAD resolution, blob fetching, atomic commits).
  - `src/patch.ts`: Search/replace block parser, multi-strategy line matching (exact, normalized CRLF/LF, relaxed trimmed), and LLM placeholder guardrails.
  - `src/encoding.ts`: UTF-8 to Base64 encoding utilities.
  - `src/types.ts`: TypeScript interfaces for payloads, environments, and commit structures.

## Endpoints & Authentication
- **Health Check (`GET /` or `GET /health`)**:
  - Public endpoint returning `{ status: "ok", service: "gemini-git-bridge", version: "1.0.0", hasBridgeSecret: boolean, hasGithubToken: boolean }`.
- **Commit Relay (`POST /push`, `POST /commit`, `POST /api/commit`, `POST /`)**:
  - Authenticated via `Authorization: Bearer <BRIDGE_SECRET>` or header `x-bridge-secret: <BRIDGE_SECRET>`.
  - Body parameters: `repo` (`"owner/repo"`), `targetBranch` (default: `"main"`), `commitMessage`, `changes[]` (supports `search`/`replace`, `patches[]`, or full `content`), and `deletions[]`.
  - Verifies target branch HEAD commit OID (`expectedHeadOid`).
  - Applies patches in-memory and pushes atomically via GitHub GraphQL `createCommitOnBranch`.
- **GraphQL Proxy (`POST /graphql`)**:
  - Authenticated proxy for arbitrary GitHub GraphQL queries/mutations.

## Developer Rules for AI Agents
- **Path Awareness**: The Cloudflare Worker source code and config reside in `cf-gemini-git-bridge/`. All worker commands (e.g. `npx wrangler deploy`, `npm install`) must be run inside `cf-gemini-git-bridge/`.
- **No Local State**: The worker runs at the Cloudflare Edge. Do not introduce local file-system dependencies, SQLite databases, or stateful worker memory.
- **Security First**: Never log, expose, or echo `GITHUB_TOKEN` or `BRIDGE_SECRET` in API responses, console logs, or client-side UI error messages.
- **Hard Guardrail**: Strictly maintain the validation in `patch.ts` that rejects any payload or file content containing LLM placeholder comments (e.g., `// ... existing code ...`, `// ... rest of code ...`, `<!-- ... existing code ... -->`).
- **Patch Preference**: For modifying existing files, always prefer targeted `search`/`replace` patches over full-file overwrites to prevent accidental code truncation.

# ==============================================================================
# GEMINI PROMPT TEMPLATE - GEMINI-TO-GITHUB BRIDGE
# Datei: gemini_prompt_instructions.txt
# Hinweis: Diese Datei dient als Kopiervorlage für Google Gemini / AI Studio
#          und wird von AI-Assistenten nicht automatisch in den Kontext geladen.
# ==============================================================================

--- [DEUTSCHE VERSION (Copy & Paste in Gemini System Instructions / Prompt)] ---

Wenn du Code änderst oder neue Dateien erstellst, beachte strikt folgende Regeln für die GitHub-Bridge:

1. DATEIPFAD:
   Gib in der allerersten Zeile jedes Code-Blocks immer den relativen Dateipfad an:
   // filepath: pfad/zur/datei.ts (oder # filepath: pfad/script.py für Python)

2. TEILÄNDERUNGEN (Search/Replace):
   Wenn du nur Teile einer bestehenden Datei änderst, verwende exakt dieses Format:
   // filepath: pfad/zur/datei.ts
   <<<<<<< SEARCH
   exakter_alter_code_aus_dem_repo
   =======
   neuer_ersetzter_code
   >>>>>>> REPLACE

3. NEUE ODER VOLLSTÄNDIGE DATEIEN:
   Gib den kompletten Dateiinhalt an, beginnend mit der Dateipfad-Zeile:
   // filepath: pfad/zur/neuen_datei.ts
   // Vollständiger Code ohne Kürzungen

4. STRIKTE GUARDRAIL (KEINE PLATZHALTER):
   Verwende NIEMALS Platzhalter wie "// ... existing code ...", "/* ... rest of code ... */" oder "# ... remaining code". Der Server lehnt solche Commits automatisch ab, um Datenverlust zu verhindern. Wenn du nicht die ganze Datei schreiben willst, nutze immer Search/Replace!


--- [ENGLISH VERSION (Copy & Paste for Gemini System Instructions / Prompt)] ---

When modifying code or creating new files, strictly adhere to the following rules for the GitHub Bridge:

1. FILEPATH:
   Always specify the relative file path on the very first line of each code block:
   // filepath: path/to/file.ts (or # filepath: path/to/script.py for Python)

2. PARTIAL MODIFICATIONS (Search/Replace):
   When modifying parts of an existing file, always use exact search/replace blocks:
   // filepath: path/to/file.ts
   <<<<<<< SEARCH
   exact_existing_code_from_repo
   =======
   new_replacement_code
   >>>>>>> REPLACE

3. NEW OR FULL REPLACEMENT FILES:
   Provide the full file content starting with the filepath comment:
   // filepath: path/to/new_file.ts
   // Complete code without truncations

4. STRICT GUARDRAIL (NO PLACEHOLDERS):
   NEVER use comments like "// ... existing code ...", "/* ... rest of code ... */", or "# ... remaining code". Commits with placeholders are automatically rejected by the edge worker to prevent data loss. If you don't want to output the whole file, use search/replace instead!

// ==UserScript==
// @name         Gemini Git Bridge
// @namespace    https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev/
// @version      1.0.0
// @description  Commit code & search/replace patches directly from Google Gemini & AI Studio to GitHub via Cloudflare Worker
// @author       Antigravity
// @match        https://gemini.google.com/*
// @match        https://aistudio.google.com/*
// @grant        GM_xmlhttpRequest
// @grant        GM_notification
// @grant        GM_setValue
// @grant        GM_getValue
// @connect      gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev
// @run-at       document-idle
// ==/UserScript==

(function () {
  'use strict';

  // --- Deployed Bridge Configuration ---
  const BRIDGE_WORKER_URL = 'https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev';
  const BRIDGE_SECRET = 'fc1bec3f781b59dee2e576650460f417269fd44d3b0e39d77ae18480c270524f';

  // --- Storage Helpers ---
  function getStored(key, defaultValue) {
    if (typeof GM_getValue === 'function') {
      return GM_getValue(key, defaultValue);
    }
    return localStorage.getItem('gemini_bridge_' + key) || defaultValue;
  }

  function setStored(key, value) {
    if (typeof GM_setValue === 'function') {
      GM_setValue(key, value);
    } else {
      localStorage.setItem('gemini_bridge_' + key, value);
    }
  }

  let defaultOwnerRepo = getStored('owner_repo', 'alexanderpeschkaGit/Portfolio_VJ_Brave_V2_0');
  let defaultBranch = getStored('branch', 'preview');

  // --- Styles ---
  const STYLES = `
    .ggb-btn {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 6px;
      padding: 5px 12px;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1e293b, #0f172a);
      color: #38bdf8;
      border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35);
      border-radius: 8px;
      font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
      font-size: 12px;
      font-weight: 600;
      cursor: pointer;
      box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.25);
      transition: all 0.2s ease;
      user-select: none;
      z-index: 10;
    }
    .ggb-btn:hover {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #0284c7, #0369a1);
      color: #ffffff;
      border-color: #38bdf8;
      transform: translateY(-1px);
      box-shadow: 0 4px 12px rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.4);
    }
    .ggb-float-trigger {
      position: fixed;
      bottom: 24px;
      right: 24px;
      padding: 10px 18px;
      background: #0f172a;
      color: #38bdf8;
      border: 1px solid #38bdf8;
      border-radius: 24px;
      font-size: 13px;
      font-weight: 700;
      box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
      z-index: 999999;
      cursor: pointer;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 8px;
      transition: transform 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s;
    }
    .ggb-float-trigger:hover {
      transform: scale(1.05);
      box-shadow: 0 6px 24px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4);
    }
    .ggb-modal-overlay {
      position: fixed;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      right: 0;
      bottom: 0;
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);
      backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      z-index: 1000000;
      font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
    }
    .ggb-modal {
      background: #0f172a;
      border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3);
      border-radius: 16px;
      width: 580px;
      max-width: 92vw;
      max-height: 85vh;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      box-shadow: 0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
      color: #f1f5f9;
      overflow: hidden;
      animation: ggb-fadein 0.2s ease-out;
    }
    @keyframes ggb-fadein {
      from { opacity: 0; transform: scale(0.95); }
      to { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1); }
    }
    .ggb-modal-header {
      padding: 16px 20px;
      background: #1e293b;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);
    }
    .ggb-modal-header h3 {
      margin: 0;
      font-size: 15px;
      font-weight: 700;
      color: #38bdf8;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 8px;
    }
    .ggb-close-btn {
      background: transparent;
      border: none;
      color: #94a3b8;
      font-size: 18px;
      cursor: pointer;
      line-height: 1;
      padding: 4px;
    }
    .ggb-close-btn:hover { color: #ffffff; }
    .ggb-modal-body {
      padding: 20px;
      overflow-y: auto;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 14px;
    }
    .ggb-form-group {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 6px;
    }
    .ggb-form-group label {
      font-size: 12px;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: #94a3b8;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.5px;
    }
    .ggb-input, .ggb-textarea {
      background: #1e293b;
      border: 1px solid #334155;
      border-radius: 8px;
      padding: 9px 12px;
      color: #f8fafc;
      font-size: 13px;
      outline: none;
      transition: border-color 0.2s;
    }
    .ggb-input:focus, .ggb-textarea:focus {
      border-color: #38bdf8;
      box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.2);
    }
    .ggb-file-pill {
      background: #1e293b;
      border: 1px solid #334155;
      border-radius: 8px;
      padding: 8px 12px;
      font-family: monospace;
      font-size: 12px;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      color: #cbd5e1;
    }
    .ggb-status {
      font-size: 12px;
      padding: 10px 14px;
      border-radius: 8px;
      display: none;
    }
    .ggb-status.info { display: block; background: #0c4a6e; color: #7dd3fc; border: 1px solid #0284c7; }
    .ggb-status.success { display: block; background: #064e3b; color: #6ee7b7; border: 1px solid #059669; }
    .ggb-status.error { display: block; background: #7f1d1d; color: #fca5a5; border: 1px solid #dc2626; }
    .ggb-modal-footer {
      padding: 14px 20px;
      background: #1e293b;
      border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: flex-end;
      gap: 10px;
    }
  `;

  const styleEl = document.createElement('style');
  styleEl.textContent = STYLES;
  document.head.appendChild(styleEl);

  // --- Network Request to Cloudflare Worker Relay ---
  async function sendCommitToBridge(payload) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const url = `${BRIDGE_WORKER_URL}/commit`;

      if (typeof GM_xmlhttpRequest === 'function') {
        GM_xmlhttpRequest({
          method: 'POST',
          url: url,
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'x-bridge-secret': BRIDGE_SECRET
          },
          data: JSON.stringify(payload),
          onload: function (res) {
            try {
              const data = JSON.parse(res.responseText);
              if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 300 && data.success) {
                resolve(data);
              } else {
                reject(new Error(data.error || `HTTP ${res.status}: ${res.responseText}`));
              }
            } catch (e) {
              reject(new Error(`Failed to parse response: ${res.responseText}`));
            }
          },
          onerror: function (err) {
            reject(new Error('Network error calling Cloudflare Worker Bridge'));
          }
        });
      } else {
        fetch(url, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'x-bridge-secret': BRIDGE_SECRET
          },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        })
          .then((res) => res.json())
          .then((data) => {
            if (data.success) resolve(data);
            else reject(new Error(data.error || 'Commit failed'));
          })
          .catch(reject);
      }
    });
  }

  // --- Path & Patch Extraction Helpers ---
  function extractPathFromCode(codeText, preElement) {
    // 1. Look for inline comments: // filepath: ... or // path: ...
    const inlineMatch = codeText.match(/(?:\/\/|#|\/\*)\s*(?:filepath|file|path):\s*([^\r\n*]+)/i);
    if (inlineMatch) return inlineMatch[1].trim();

    // 2. Look for filename header right above the code block
    if (preElement) {
      let prev = preElement.previousElementSibling;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3 && prev; i++) {
        const text = prev.textContent.trim();
        const headerMatch = text.match(/(?:File|Pfad|Datei)?:\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_\-./\\]+\.[a-zA-Z0-9]+)/i);
        if (headerMatch) return headerMatch[1].trim();
        if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-./\\]+\.[a-zA-Z0-9]+$/.test(text)) return text;
        prev = prev.previousElementSibling;
      }
    }

    return '';
  }

  function isSearchReplace(text) {
    return text.includes('<<<<<<<') && text.includes('=======');
  }

  // --- Commit Dialog UI ---
  function openCommitDialog(initialFiles, suggestedMessage = '') {
    const existing = document.querySelector('.ggb-modal-overlay');
    if (existing) existing.remove();

    const overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className = 'ggb-modal-overlay';

    const defaultMsg = suggestedMessage || 'feat: update files via Gemini Git Bridge';

    overlay.innerHTML = `
      <div class="ggb-modal">
        <div class="ggb-modal-header">
          <h3>⚡ Gemini Git Bridge</h3>
          <button class="ggb-close-btn">&times;</button>
        </div>
        <div class="ggb-modal-body">
          <div class="ggb-form-group">
            <label>GitHub Repository (owner/repo)</label>
            <input type="text" class="ggb-input" id="ggb-repo" value="${defaultOwnerRepo}" placeholder="owner/repository" />
          </div>
          <div class="ggb-form-group">
            <label>Target Branch</label>
            <input type="text" class="ggb-input" id="ggb-branch" value="${defaultBranch}" placeholder="main" />
          </div>
          <div class="ggb-form-group">
            <label>Commit Message</label>
            <textarea class="ggb-textarea" id="ggb-msg" rows="2">${defaultMsg}</textarea>
          </div>
          <div class="ggb-form-group">
            <label>Dateien (${initialFiles.length})</label>
            <div id="ggb-files-list" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:6px;"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="ggb-status" id="ggb-status"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="ggb-modal-footer">
          <button class="ggb-btn" id="ggb-cancel-btn" style="background:transparent; color:#94a3b8; border-color:#334155;">Abbrechen</button>
          <button class="ggb-btn" id="ggb-commit-btn" style="padding: 7px 18px; font-size:13px;">🚀 Auf GitHub committen</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    document.body.appendChild(overlay);

    const filesList = overlay.querySelector('#ggb-files-list');
    const repoInput = overlay.querySelector('#ggb-repo');
    const branchInput = overlay.querySelector('#ggb-branch');
    const msgInput = overlay.querySelector('#ggb-msg');
    const statusBox = overlay.querySelector('#ggb-status');
    const commitBtn = overlay.querySelector('#ggb-commit-btn');
    const cancelBtn = overlay.querySelector('#ggb-cancel-btn');
    const closeBtn = overlay.querySelector('.ggb-close-btn');

    // Populate file list with editable paths
    const fileEntries = initialFiles.map((file, idx) => {
      const container = document.createElement('div');
      container.className = 'ggb-file-pill';
      const typeBadge = isSearchReplace(file.content || file.diff || '')
        ? '<span style="color:#f59e0b; font-size:11px;">[Search/Replace Patch]</span>'
        : '<span style="color:#10b981; font-size:11px;">[Vollständige Datei]</span>';

      container.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; flex:1;">
          <span>📄</span>
          <input type="text" class="ggb-input" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:12px; flex:1;" value="${file.path}" placeholder="pfad/zur/datei.ts" id="ggb-path-${idx}" />
          ${typeBadge}
        </div>
      `;
      filesList.appendChild(container);
      return { ...file, idx };
    });

    const close = () => overlay.remove();
    closeBtn.onclick = close;
    cancelBtn.onclick = close;

    commitBtn.onclick = async () => {
      const ownerRepo = repoInput.value.trim();
      const branch = branchInput.value.trim();
      const message = msgInput.value.trim();

      const [owner, repo] = ownerRepo.split('/');
      if (!owner || !repo) {
        statusBox.className = 'ggb-status error';
        statusBox.textContent = 'Fehler: Bitte Repository als "owner/repo" angeben (z.B. alexanderpeschkaGit/Portfolio_VJ_Brave_V2_0).';
        return;
      }

      setStored('owner_repo', ownerRepo);
      setStored('branch', branch);

      const filesToCommit = fileEntries.map((fe) => {
        const pathEl = overlay.querySelector(`#ggb-path-${fe.idx}`);
        const finalPath = pathEl ? pathEl.value.trim() : fe.path;
        return {
          path: finalPath,
          content: fe.content,
          diff: fe.diff
        };
      });

      if (filesToCommit.some((f) => !f.path)) {
        statusBox.className = 'ggb-status error';
        statusBox.textContent = 'Fehler: Alle Dateien müssen einen gültigen Zielpfad haben!';
        return;
      }

      commitBtn.disabled = true;
      commitBtn.style.opacity = '0.5';
      statusBox.className = 'ggb-status info';
      statusBox.innerHTML = `⏳ Sende Commit an Cloudflare Worker Relay... (${filesToCommit.length} Datei(en))`;

      try {
        const payload = {
          owner,
          repo,
          branch,
          message,
          files: filesToCommit
        };

        const result = await sendCommitToBridge(payload);

        statusBox.className = 'ggb-status success';
        statusBox.innerHTML = `
          ✅ <strong>Commit erfolgreich!</strong><br/>
          OID: <code style="color:#fff;">${result.commit.oid.slice(0, 7)}</code> auf Branch <code style="color:#fff;">${result.branch}</code><br/>
          <a href="${result.commit.url}" target="_blank" style="color:#38bdf8; text-decoration:underline; font-weight:600; display:inline-block; margin-top:4px;">
            🔗 Commit auf GitHub ansehen &rarr;
          </a>
        `;
        commitBtn.textContent = 'Erledigt';
      } catch (err) {
        statusBox.className = 'ggb-status error';
        statusBox.innerHTML = `❌ <strong>Fehler beim Commit:</strong><br/>${err.message}`;
        commitBtn.disabled = false;
        commitBtn.style.opacity = '1';
      }
    };
  }

  // --- Attach Action Buttons to Code Blocks in Gemini UI ---
  function enhanceCodeBlocks() {
    const codeBlocks = document.querySelectorAll('pre:not([data-ggb-processed])');

    codeBlocks.forEach((pre) => {
      pre.setAttribute('data-ggb-processed', 'true');

      const codeEl = pre.querySelector('code');
      const codeText = (codeEl ? codeEl.innerText : pre.innerText) || '';

      // Only attach if code block has enough content
      if (codeText.length < 15) return;

      const detectedPath = extractPathFromCode(codeText, pre);
      const isPatch = isSearchReplace(codeText);

      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'ggb-btn';
      btn.style.margin = '6px';
      btn.innerHTML = isPatch ? '⚡ Git Patch committen' : '🚀 Zu GitHub pushen';

      btn.onclick = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();

        const fileObj = {
          path: detectedPath || 'src/datei.ts',
          content: isPatch ? undefined : codeText,
          diff: isPatch ? codeText : undefined
        };

        openCommitDialog([fileObj], isPatch ? 'refactor: apply search/replace patch' : 'feat: add / update file');
      };

      // Find code header or prepend to pre
      const codeHeader = pre.parentElement?.querySelector('.code-block-decoration, .code-header, [class*="header"]');
      if (codeHeader) {
        codeHeader.appendChild(btn);
      } else {
        pre.style.position = 'relative';
        btn.style.position = 'absolute';
        btn.style.top = '8px';
        btn.style.right = '40px';
        pre.appendChild(btn);
      }
    });
  }

  // --- Floating Button to Scan Whole Chat for Patches ---
  function createFloatingTrigger() {
    if (document.querySelector('.ggb-float-trigger')) return;

    const trigger = document.createElement('div');
    trigger.className = 'ggb-float-trigger';
    trigger.innerHTML = '<span>⚡</span> <span>Git Bridge</span>';

    trigger.onclick = () => {
      // Find all code blocks in the current view
      const blocks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('pre code, pre'));
      const files = [];

      blocks.forEach((el) => {
        const text = el.innerText || '';
        const path = extractPathFromCode(text, el.closest('pre'));
        if (path) {
          files.push({
            path,
            content: isSearchReplace(text) ? undefined : text,
            diff: isSearchReplace(text) ? text : undefined
          });
        }
      });

      if (files.length === 0) {
        openCommitDialog([{ path: 'src/file.ts', content: '' }], 'chore: update via Gemini Git Bridge');
      } else {
        openCommitDialog(files, `feat: commit ${files.length} file(s) from Gemini chat`);
      }
    };

    document.body.appendChild(trigger);
  }

  // --- Observer for Dynamic DOM updates in Gemini ---
  const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
    enhanceCodeBlocks();
    createFloatingTrigger();
  });

  observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

  // Initial run
  enhanceCodeBlocks();
  createFloatingTrigger();
  console.log('[Gemini Git Bridge] Loaded. Connected to:', BRIDGE_WORKER_URL);
})();

================
File: _v1_backup/Gemini-to-GitHub-Bridge.info
================
# Gemini-to-GitHub Bridge (.info)

Universelle Edge-Brücke und Browser-Integration für atomare 1-Click-Git-Commits und Search/Replace-Patches direkt aus **Google Gemini** (`gemini.google.com`) und **Google AI Studio** (`aistudio.google.com`) in beliebige GitHub-Repositories.

---

## 1. Installation im Browser

Um die 1-Click-Commit-Buttons in Google Gemini und Google AI Studio zu nutzen:

1. **Tampermonkey** oder **Violentmonkey** im Browser öffnen.
2. **Neues Skript erstellen**.
3. **Inhalt von `gemini-bridge.user.js` hineinkopieren und speichern.**

### Konfiguration im Browser:
- Nach der Installation erscheint auf `gemini.google.com` und `aistudio.google.com` ein Einstellungs-Icon bzw. Bridge-Badge.
- Hier können die **Worker-URL** (Standard: `https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev`), das **Bridge-Secret**, das **Standard-Repository** (`owner/repo`) und der **Standard-Branch** (z. B. `preview` oder `main`) hinterlegt werden.

---

## 2. Cloudflare Worker Setup & Deployment

Der Worker-Code liegt im Unterverzeichnis `cf-gemini-git-bridge/`.

### Schnellbefehle (Wrangler CLI)
Vor der Ausführung immer in das Verzeichnis wechseln:
```bash
cd cf-gemini-git-bridge
```

- **Abhängigkeiten installieren:** `npm install`
- **Lokaler Test:** `npx wrangler dev`
- **Live Deployment:** `npx wrangler deploy`
- **GitHub Token hinterlegen:** `npx wrangler secret put GITHUB_TOKEN`
- **Bridge Secret hinterlegen:** `npx wrangler secret put BRIDGE_SECRET`

### Credentials (Cloudflare Secrets)
- `GITHUB_TOKEN`: Fine-Grained Personal Access Token (Permissions: `Contents: Read and write`)
- `BRIDGE_SECRET`: Shared Secret zur Authentifizierung der API-Calls (`Authorization: Bearer <token>` oder `x-bridge-secret: <token>`)

---

## 3. Endpunkte & Nutzung

- `GET /health` (oder `GET /`): Öffentlicher Status-Check (prüft, ob Worker läuft und Secrets gesetzt sind).
- `POST /push` (oder `/commit`): Führt atomare Commits und Search/Replace-Patches aus.
- `POST /graphql`: Authentifizierter Proxy für freie GitHub GraphQL Abfragen.

---

## 4. Schutz vor Code-Kürzungen (Hard Guardrail)

Der Worker lehnt Commits automatisch ab (`400 Bad Request`), falls die Patches oder Dateiinhalte Platzhalter wie `// ... existing code ...` oder `/* ... rest of code ... */` enthalten. Damit wird verhindert, dass unvollständiger KI-Code bestehende Dateien zerstört.

File: _v1_backup/README.md
================
# Gemini-to-GitHub Bridge

Universelle Edge-Brücke und Browser-Integration für atomare 1-Click-Git-Commits und Search/Replace-Patches direkt aus **Google Gemini** (`gemini.google.com`) und **Google AI Studio** (`aistudio.google.com`) in beliebige GitHub-Repositories.

---

## Architektur-Überblick

Das Projekt besteht aus zwei perfekt aufeinander abgestimmten Komponenten:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Browser (gemini.google.com / aistudio) │
│  └─ Tampermonkey: gemini-bridge.user.js │
└────────────────────┬────────────────────┘
                     │ HTTPS (JSON + x-bridge-secret)
                     ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Cloudflare Worker (cf-gemini-git-bridge)│
│  ├─ Auth & Health Check                 │
│  ├─ Hard Guardrails (Anti-Placeholder)  │
│  ├─ In-Memory Search/Replace Resolver   │
│  └─ GitHub GraphQL API v4               │
└────────────────────┬────────────────────┘
                     │ createCommitOnBranch (GraphQL)
                     ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ GitHub Repository (Beliebige Branches)  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

1. **Frontend-Layer (`gemini-bridge.user.js`)**: Userscript für Tampermonkey/Violentmonkey. Fügt Code-Blöcken und Chat-Antworten einen interaktiven „Commit to GitHub“-Button mit Diff-Vorschau und Branch-Auswahl hinzu.
2. **Backend-Layer (`cf-gemini-git-bridge/`)**: Serverless Cloudflare Worker. Validiert Secrets, prüft HEAD-OIDs (Race-Condition-Schutz), löst Patches gegen GitHub-Blobs auf, filtert LLM-Platzhalter (`// ... existing code ...`) heraus und führt atomare Commits via GitHub GraphQL API v4 durch.

---

## 1. Installation im Browser (Userscript)

Um die 1-Click-Commit-Buttons in Google Gemini und AI Studio zu aktivieren:

1. **Tampermonkey** oder **Violentmonkey** im Browser öffnen (falls noch nicht vorhanden, über den Browser-Extension-Store installieren).
2. **Neues Skript erstellen** (im Dashboard der Extension auf `+` bzw. `Neues Skript hinzufügen` klicken).
3. **Inhalt von [`gemini-bridge.user.js`](gemini-bridge.user.js) hineinkopieren und speichern** (`Strg + S`).

### Konfiguration im Browser
Nach der Installation erscheint auf `gemini.google.com` und `aistudio.google.com`:
- Ein Einstellungs-Icon (Zahnrad / Bridge-Badge) unten rechts oder am Code-Block.
- Dort können die **Worker-URL**, das **Bridge-Secret**, das **Standard-Repository** (z. B. `owner/repo`) und der **Standard-Branch** (z. B. `main` oder `preview`) konfiguriert werden.

---

## 2. Cloudflare Worker Setup & Deployment

Der Worker befindet sich im Unterverzeichnis `cf-gemini-git-bridge/`.

### Schnellbefehle (Wrangler CLI)

Vor dem Ausführen von Befehlen immer in den Worker-Ordner wechseln:

```bash
cd cf-gemini-git-bridge
```

| Aktion | Befehl |
|---|---|
| Abhängigkeiten installieren | `npm install` |
| Lokaler Test | `npx wrangler dev` |
| Live Deployment | `npx wrangler deploy` |
| GitHub Token hinterlegen | `npx wrangler secret put GITHUB_TOKEN` |
| Bridge Secret hinterlegen | `npx wrangler secret put BRIDGE_SECRET` |

### Benötigte Secrets (Cloudflare Worker)
- **`GITHUB_TOKEN`**: Fine-Grained Personal Access Token (PAT) von GitHub mit der Berechtigung `Contents: Read and write` für die Ziel-Repositories.
- **`BRIDGE_SECRET`**: Frei wählbares Secret (z. B. 64-stelliger Hex-Token), das zur Absicherung der Worker-API dient.

---

## 3. API-Endpunkte & Authentifizierung

Der Worker akzeptiert Authentifizierung über:
- Header: `Authorization: Bearer <BRIDGE_SECRET>`
- Header: `x-bridge-secret: <BRIDGE_SECRET>`

### 1. Health-Check (öffentlich)
- **Methode:** `GET /` oder `GET /health`
- **Antwort:**
```json
{
  "status": "ok",
  "service": "gemini-git-bridge",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "timestamp": "2026-10-15T14:30:00.000Z",
  "hasBridgeSecret": true,
  "hasGithubToken": true
}
```

### 2. Commit Relay (geschützt)
- **Methode:** `POST /push`, `POST /commit`, `POST /api/commit` oder `POST /`
- **Beispiel-Payload (Search/Replace Patch):**
```json
{
  "repo": "meinUser/meinRepo",
  "targetBranch": "main",
  "commitMessage": "fix: update button styles",
  "changes": [
    {
      "path": "src/styles.css",
      "search": "background: red;",
      "replace": "background: #2563eb;"
    }
  ]
}
```
- **Beispiel-Payload (Ganze Datei anlegen oder überschreiben):**
```json
{
  "repo": "meinUser/meinRepo",
  "targetBranch": "feature/login",
  "commitMessage": "feat: add config file",
  "changes": [
    {
      "path": "config.json",
      "content": "{\n  \"apiUrl\": \"https://api.example.com\"\n}"
    }
  ]
}
```
- **Beispiel-Payload (Datei löschen):**
```json
{
  "repo": "meinUser/meinRepo",
  "targetBranch": "main",
  "commitMessage": "chore: remove deprecated file",
  "deletions": [
    { "path": "old-file.txt" }
  ]
}
```

### 3. Raw GraphQL Proxy (geschützt)
- **Methode:** `POST /graphql`
- Leitet beliebige GraphQL-Queries/Mutationen direkt an die GitHub GraphQL API weiter.

---

## 4. Sicherheits-Features & Hard Guardrails

- **Zero-Local-State**: Worker läuft vollständig serverless auf Cloudflare Edge ohne Dateisystem-Abhängigkeiten.
- **LLM-Platzhalter-Schutz**: Verhindert Code-Zerstörung! Payloads, die Text wie `// ... existing code ...`, `/* ... rest of code ... */` oder `# ... existing code ...` enthalten, werden vom Worker strikt mit einem Fehler (`400 Bad Request`) abgelehnt.
- **HEAD-OID-Prüfung**: Vor jedem Commit wird die aktuelle Commit-OID des Ziel-Branches abgefragt (`expectedHeadOid`). So werden Race-Conditions und unbemerkte Überschreibungen verhindert.
- **Tolerantes Patching**: Suchblöcke tolerieren Windows- (CRLF) und Unix-Zeilenumbrüche (LF) sowie leicht abweichende Einrückungen.

File: .agents/rules/multi-ide-setup.md
================
---
trigger: model_decision
description: "Apply this rule when configuring or executing tasks across different IDE environments (Antigravity, Kimi Code, Cursor, Cline)."
---

# Multi-IDE Setup & Execution Adapters

Dieses Regelwerk definiert die standardisierten Betriebsmodi zur Ausführung von `#exec-wave`, `#delegate` und Subagenten in verschiedenen Entwicklungs- und IDE-Umgebungen.

---

## 1. Die drei Betriebsmodi (Mode A, Mode B, Mode C)

### Mode A: Native Multi-Model Subagents (z. B. Google Antigravity)
- **Architektur:** Vollwertige Unterstützung für `invoke_subagent` mit variabler Modell-Zuweisung (`flash_lite`, `flash`, `pro`).
- **Scatter-Gather:** Parallele Waves laufen echt nebenläufig über disjunkte Dateisätze.
- **Live-Streaming:** Der Coordinator erzeugt sofort nach Subagent-Start anklickbare Deep-Links im Format:
  `[⚡ Watch Stream](conversation://<subagent-conversation-id>)`.
- **3-Point Feedback Contract:** Jeder Subagent berichtet beim Abschluss strictly:
  1. `Status: [SUCCESS | BLOCKED | FAILED]`
  2. `Files Modified: [Liste relativer Pfade]`
  3. `Summary: [1-2 Sätze Kernänderungen]`

### Mode B: Fixed-Model oder Rate-Limited Subagents (z. B. Kimi Code / CLI Agents)
- **Architektur:** Subagenten laufen mit `Model: 'inherit'`, um die Kontext-Isolation zu wahren.
- **Pacing & Concurrency:** Bei strikten Rate-Limits oder fehlendem Multithreading werden parallele Batches als sequenzielle FIFO-Warteschlange abgearbeitet.
- **Fallback-Verlinkung:** Kann die IDE keine `conversation://`-Links auflösen, gibt der Coordinator den Pfad zum Transcript an:
  `file:///<appDataDir>/brain/<conversation-id>/.system_generated/logs/transcript.jsonl`.

### Mode C: Single-Agent IDEs ohne Subagent-Engine (z. B. Cursor Composer / Cline)
- **Architektur:** Ein einziger Agent führt alle Batches nacheinander in separaten Kontext-Hygiene-Durchgängen aus.
- **Kontext-Disziplin:** Lade jeweils nur die im aktuellen Batch spezifizierten Zieldateien.
- **Single-Action Protocol:** Strikt ein Terminal-Befehl pro Schritt, keine Verkettung mit `&&` oder `;`.
- **Modell-Switch-Hinweis:** Vor dem Start von `Tier: PRO`-Batches (Komplexität >= 7) empfiehlt der Agent einen Modellwechsel auf ein Pro-Modell oder führt einen expliziten Self-Review-Pass durch.

---

## 2. Invarianten & Qualitäts-Gates
1. **Disjunkte Datei-Inventare:** Parallele Subagenten dürfen niemals dieselbe Datei zeitgleich editieren.
2. **Shell-Verbot für Subagenten:** Parallele Subagenten führen keine globalen Git- oder Deployment-Befehle aus. Alle Verifikationen und Commits erfolgen durch den Coordinator.
3. **Dateigrößen-Gesetz:** Alle erzeugten und modifizierten Dateien bleiben strikt `< 500` Zeilen.

# Ask Workflow

- Explain or investigate without modifying files.
- Use plain language and define necessary technical terms in everyday words.
- Cover what the subject is, how it works, and why it matters at the depth appropriate to the question.
- For AI and software questions, utilize current online sources and cleanly differentiate between manufacturer claims, independent tests, and community experiences (e.g., GitHub/Forums). Note relevant model versions, release dates, and usage limits where applicable.
- For complex or debated topics, clearly separate verified facts from plausible interpretations, personal experiences, and speculation. Present multiple perspectives fairly and acknowledge uncertainties.
- Inspect repository files when needed so the answer is based on the actual project.
- Do not use fenced code blocks unless the user explicitly requests an example that requires one.
