import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import { createRequire } from 'module';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const rootDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
const workerDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');

const req = createRequire(path.join(rootDir, 'cf-gemini-git-bridge/package.json'));
let esbuild;
try {
  esbuild = req('esbuild');
} catch (e) {
  esbuild = await import('esbuild');
}

async function build() {
  console.log('[Build Worker] Bundling packages/edge-worker/src/index.ts...');
  const entryPoint = path.join(workerDir, 'src/index.ts');
  const distDir = path.join(workerDir, 'dist');
  const distTarget = path.join(distDir, 'worker.mjs');

  if (!fs.existsSync(distDir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(distDir, { recursive: true });
  }

  const result = await esbuild.build({
    entryPoints: [entryPoint],
    bundle: true,
    write: true,
    outfile: distTarget,
    format: 'esm',
    target: 'es2022'
  });

  console.log(`[Build Worker] Wrote: ${distTarget}`);
  console.log('✅ [Build Worker] Edge Worker successfully built.');
}

build().catch((err) => {
  console.error('[Build Worker] Build failed:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});

================
File: packages/edge-worker/src/modules/patchInMemory.ts
================
import type {
  FilePatchOperation,
  GeminiPatchPayload,
  LinterError
} from '../types';
import {
  LLM_PLACEHOLDER_PATTERNS,
  applySearchReplace
} from './patcher';
import { runPreflightLinter } from './linter';

/**
 * Stage 4 Linter Hook type: enables modules/linter.ts to inject AST/syntax checks
 * into the patch preflight pipeline without modifying patcher.ts.
 */
export type PreflightLinterHook = (
  files: { path: string; content: string }[],
  preflight?: GeminiPatchPayload['preflight']
) => Promise<LinterError[]> | LinterError[];

/**
 * Non-throwing helper that checks LLM_PLACEHOLDER_PATTERNS and returns
 * aggregated LinterError items for Stage 2 & 4 non-fail-fast error reporting.
 */
export function checkPlaceholderErrors(content: string, filePath: string): LinterError[] {
  const errors: LinterError[] = [];
  const lines = content.split('\n');
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const line = lines[i];
    for (const pattern of LLM_PLACEHOLDER_PATTERNS) {
      if (pattern.test(line)) {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: 'CUSTOM_RULE',
          line: i + 1,
          message: `Forbidden LLM placeholder pattern detected: "${line.trim()}"`,
          codeSnippet: line.trim()
        });
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  return errors;
}

/**
 * Evaluates custom preflight rules without failing fast.
 * Verifies forbidPatterns and requireExports across all resulting files.
 */
export function runBuiltinPreflightRules(
  files: { path: string; content: string }[],
  preflight?: GeminiPatchPayload['preflight']
): LinterError[] {
  const errors: LinterError[] = [];
  if (!preflight) {
    return errors;
  }

  // 1. forbidPatterns
  if (Array.isArray(preflight.forbidPatterns) && preflight.forbidPatterns.length > 0) {
    for (const pat of preflight.forbidPatterns) {
      let rx: RegExp;
      try {
        rx = new RegExp(pat);
      } catch {
        continue;
      }
      for (const file of files) {
        const lines = file.content.split('\n');
        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
          const line = lines[i];
          if (rx.test(line)) {
            errors.push({
              file: file.path,
              stage: 'CUSTOM_RULE',
              line: i + 1,
              message: `Forbidden pattern "${pat}" detected.`,
              codeSnippet: line.trim()
            });
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. requireExports
  if (preflight.requireExports && typeof preflight.requireExports === 'object') {
    for (const [requiredPath, symbols] of Object.entries(preflight.requireExports)) {
      const targetFile = files.find((f) => f.path === requiredPath);
      if (!targetFile) {
        for (const symbol of symbols) {
          errors.push({
            file: requiredPath,
            stage: 'CUSTOM_RULE',
            message: `Required export "${symbol}" missing because file "${requiredPath}" was not found among modified files.`
          });
        }
        continue;
      }

      for (const symbol of symbols) {
        const exportRegex = new RegExp(
          `(?:export\\s+(?:(?:default\\s+)?(?:const|let|var|function|class|interface|type|enum|async\\s+function)\\s+${symbol}\\b|export\\s*\\{[^}]*\\b${symbol}\\b[^}]*\\}|export\\s*\\*\\s*as\\s+${symbol}\\b)`
        );
        if (!exportRegex.test(targetFile.content)) {
          errors.push({
            file: requiredPath,
            stage: 'CUSTOM_RULE',
            message: `Required export "${symbol}" missing in "${requiredPath}".`
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return errors;
}

/**
 * Pure in-memory patch processor.
 * Applies patches, validates placeholders, checks idempotency and executes preflight rules.
 */
export async function applyPatchesInMemory(
  patches: FilePatchOperation[],
  existingFiles: Record<string, string | null>,
  preflight?: GeminiPatchPayload['preflight'],
  preflightHook: PreflightLinterHook = runPreflightLinter
): Promise<{
  additions: { path: string; content: string }[];
  deletions: { path: string }[];
  errors: LinterError[];
  hasEffectiveChanges: boolean;
}> {
  const additions: { path: string; content: string }[] = [];
  const deletions: { path: string }[] = [];
  const errors: LinterError[] = [];
  let hasEffectiveChanges = false;
  const resultingFiles: { path: string; content: string }[] = [];

  for (const op of patches) {
    if (op.action === 'delete') {
      if (existingFiles[op.path] !== null) {
        deletions.push({ path: op.path });
        hasEffectiveChanges = true;
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (op.action === 'create') {
      if (typeof op.fullContent !== 'string') {
        errors.push({
          file: op.path,
          stage: 'SEARCH_REPLACE',
          message: 'Operation "create" requires "fullContent" string.'
        });
        continue;
      }

      const phErrors = checkPlaceholderErrors(op.fullContent, op.path);
      if (phErrors.length > 0) {
        errors.push(...phErrors);
      }

      const existing = existingFiles[op.path];
      if (existing !== op.fullContent) {
        additions.push({ path: op.path, content: op.fullContent });
        hasEffectiveChanges = true;
      }
      resultingFiles.push({ path: op.path, content: op.fullContent });
      continue;
    }

    if (op.action === 'modify') {
      // Full content overwrite without search/replace blocks
      if (typeof op.fullContent === 'string' && (!op.blocks || op.blocks.length === 0)) {
        const phErrors = checkPlaceholderErrors(op.fullContent, op.path);
        if (phErrors.length > 0) {
          errors.push(...phErrors);
        }

        const existing = existingFiles[op.path];
        if (existing !== op.fullContent) {
          additions.push({ path: op.path, content: op.fullContent });
          hasEffectiveChanges = true;
        }
        resultingFiles.push({ path: op.path, content: op.fullContent });
        continue;
      }

      // Search/replace blocks modification
      const existing = existingFiles[op.path];
      if (existing === null || existing === undefined) {
        errors.push({
          file: op.path,
          stage: 'SEARCH_REPLACE',
          message: 'Target file does not exist on branch'
        });
        continue;
      }

      if (!op.blocks || op.blocks.length === 0) {
        errors.push({
          file: op.path,
          stage: 'SEARCH_REPLACE',
          message: 'Operation "modify" requires non-empty "blocks" or "fullContent".'
        });
        continue;
      }

      let currentContent = existing;
      let hasError = false;

      for (const block of op.blocks) {
        const phErrors = checkPlaceholderErrors(block.replace, op.path);
        if (phErrors.length > 0) {
          errors.push(...phErrors);
          hasError = true;
        }

        try {
          currentContent = applySearchReplace(currentContent, block.search, block.replace, op.path);
        } catch (err: any) {
          errors.push({
            file: op.path,
            stage: 'SEARCH_REPLACE',
            message: err.message,
            codeSnippet: block.search.slice(0, 200)
          });
          hasError = true;
        }
      }

      const finalPhErrors = checkPlaceholderErrors(currentContent, op.path);
      if (finalPhErrors.length > 0) {
        errors.push(...finalPhErrors);
        hasError = true;
      }

      if (!hasError) {
        if (currentContent !== existing) {
          additions.push({ path: op.path, content: currentContent });
          hasEffectiveChanges = true;
        }
        resultingFiles.push({ path: op.path, content: currentContent });
      }
    }
  }

  // Preflight custom rules (builtin)
  const builtinErrors = runBuiltinPreflightRules(resultingFiles, preflight);
  if (builtinErrors.length > 0) {
    errors.push(...builtinErrors);
  }

  // Stage 4 preflight linter hook (oxc-parser / AST syntax checks + custom rules)
  const effectiveHook = preflightHook ?? runPreflightLinter;
  if (effectiveHook) {
    try {
      const hookErrors = await effectiveHook(resultingFiles, preflight);
      if (Array.isArray(hookErrors) && hookErrors.length > 0) {
        errors.push(...hookErrors);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      errors.push({
        file: 'preflightHook',
        stage: 'CUSTOM_RULE',
        message: `Preflight linter hook threw an error: ${err.message}`
      });
    }
  }

  // Deduplicate errors across stages and rules
  const seenErrors = new Set<string>();
  const uniqueErrors: LinterError[] = [];
  for (const err of errors) {
    const key = `${err.file}:${err.stage}:${err.line ?? 0}:${err.column ?? 0}:${err.message}`;
    if (!seenErrors.has(key)) {
      seenErrors.add(key);
      uniqueErrors.push(err);
    }
  }

  return {
    additions,
    deletions,
    errors: uniqueErrors,
    hasEffectiveChanges
  };
}

================
File: packages/edge-worker/src/modules/syntaxScanner.ts
================
import type { LinterError } from '../types';

/**
 * Optional pluggable OXC WASM parser interface.
 */
export type OxcWasmParser = (
  filename: string,
  sourceText: string
) => Promise<{ errors?: any[] } | any> | { errors?: any[] } | any;

export type OxcWasmParserFn = OxcWasmParser;

let registeredOxcParser: OxcWasmParser | null = null;

/**
 * Registers an optional WASM-based oxc-parser hook for high-fidelity AST checks.
 */
export function registerOxcWasmParser(parser: OxcWasmParser | null): void {
  registeredOxcParser = parser;
}

/**
 * Supported file extensions for syntax validation.
 */
export const SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS = ['.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.mjs', '.cjs', '.json'];

export interface BracketEntry {
  char: '(' | '[' | '{' | '${';
  line: number;
  col: number;
}

/**
 * Validates JSON files via JSON.parse.
 */
function validateJson(filePath: string, content: string, getSnippet: (l: number) => string): LinterError[] {
  try {
    JSON.parse(content);
    return [];
  } catch (err: any) {
    const msg = err.message || 'Invalid JSON';
    let line = 1;
    let col = 1;
    const posMatch = msg.match(/at position (\d+)/i);
    if (posMatch) {
      const pos = parseInt(posMatch[1], 10);
      const prefixLines = content.slice(0, pos).split('\n');
      line = prefixLines.length;
      col = prefixLines[prefixLines.length - 1].length + 1;
    } else {
      const lcMatch = msg.match(/line (\d+).*?column (\d+)/i);
      if (lcMatch) {
        line = parseInt(lcMatch[1], 10);
        col = parseInt(lcMatch[2], 10);
      }
    }
    return [{
      file: filePath,
      stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX',
      line,
      column: col,
      message: `JSON SyntaxError: ${msg}`,
      codeSnippet: getSnippet(line)
    }];
  }
}

/**
 * Runs optional OXC WASM parser if registered.
 */
async function runOxcWasm(filePath: string, content: string, getSnippet: (l: number) => string): Promise<LinterError[] | null> {
  if (!registeredOxcParser) return null;
  try {
    const res = await registeredOxcParser(filePath, content);
    if (res && Array.isArray(res.errors) && res.errors.length > 0) {
      return res.errors.map((err: any) => ({
        file: filePath,
        stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX',
        line: err.line || 1,
        column: err.column || 1,
        message: `OXC SyntaxError: ${err.message || String(err)}`,
        codeSnippet: err.line ? getSnippet(err.line) : undefined
      }));
    }
    return [];
  } catch (oxcErr: any) {
    return [{
      file: filePath,
      stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX',
      line: 1,
      column: 1,
      message: `OXC Parser failure: ${oxcErr.message || String(oxcErr)}`,
      codeSnippet: getSnippet(1)
    }];
  }
}

/**
 * Lexical and structural syntax validation running in < 5 ms in Cloudflare Workers and Node.js.
 * Non-Fail-Fast: accumulates all detected syntax errors across the file.
 */
export async function validateSyntaxInWorker(filePath: string, content: string): Promise<LinterError[]> {
  const ext = filePath.includes('.') ? filePath.slice(filePath.lastIndexOf('.')).toLowerCase() : '';
  if (!SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS.includes(ext)) {
    return [];
  }

  const errors: LinterError[] = [];
  const lines = content.split('\n');
  const getSnippet = (lineNum: number) => {
    const idx = lineNum - 1;
    return idx >= 0 && idx < lines.length ? lines[idx].trim() : '';
  };

  // 1. JSON file validation via JSON.parse
  if (ext === '.json') {
    return validateJson(filePath, content, getSnippet);
  }

  // 2. Optional registered OXC WASM Parser Hook
  const oxcErrors = await runOxcWasm(filePath, content, getSnippet);
  if (oxcErrors) {
    return oxcErrors;
  }

  // 3. Fast In-Worker Lexer and Bracket/Conflict-Marker Verifier
  let currentLine = 1;
  let currentCol = 1;
  const bracketStack: BracketEntry[] = [];
  let state: 'CODE' | 'SINGLE_QUOTE' | 'DOUBLE_QUOTE' | 'TEMPLATE' | 'BLOCK_COMMENT' | 'LINE_COMMENT' | 'REGEX' = 'CODE';
  let stringStartLine = 1;
  let stringStartCol = 1;
  let lastNonWhitespaceChar = '';
  let inRegexCharClass = false;

  const len = content.length;
  let i = 0;

  while (i < len) {
    const ch = content[i];
    const nextCh = i + 1 < len ? content[i + 1] : '';

    // Check merge conflict markers at the start of a line
    if (currentCol === 1 && (ch === '<' || ch === '=' || ch === '>')) {
      const restOfLine = content.slice(i, i + 10);
      if (restOfLine.startsWith('<<<<<<<') || restOfLine.startsWith('=======') || restOfLine.startsWith('>>>>>>>')) {
        const marker = restOfLine.slice(0, 7);
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX',
          line: currentLine,
          column: currentCol,
          message: `Git merge conflict marker detected ("${marker}"). Resolve all conflict markers before committing.`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      }
    }

    if (state === 'LINE_COMMENT') {
      if (ch === '\n') { state = 'CODE'; currentLine++; currentCol = 1; } else { currentCol++; }
      i++;
      continue;
    }

    if (state === 'BLOCK_COMMENT') {
      if (ch === '*' && nextCh === '/') { state = 'CODE'; i += 2; currentCol += 2; continue; }
      if (ch === '\n') { currentLine++; currentCol = 1; } else { currentCol++; }
      i++;
      continue;
    }

    if (state === 'SINGLE_QUOTE' || state === 'DOUBLE_QUOTE') {
      const quoteChar = state === 'SINGLE_QUOTE' ? "'" : '"';
      if (ch === '\\') { i += 2; currentCol += 2; continue; }
      if (ch === quoteChar) { state = 'CODE'; lastNonWhitespaceChar = quoteChar; i++; currentCol++; continue; }
      if (ch === '\n') {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: stringStartLine, column: stringStartCol,
          message: `Unterminated ${state === 'SINGLE_QUOTE' ? 'single' : 'double'}-quoted string literal.`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
        });
        state = 'CODE'; currentLine++; currentCol = 1; i++; continue;
      }
      currentCol++; i++;
      continue;
    }

    if (state === 'TEMPLATE') {
      if (ch === '\\') { i += 2; currentCol += 2; continue; }
      if (ch === '$' && nextCh === '{') {
        bracketStack.push({ char: '${', line: currentLine, col: currentCol });
        state = 'CODE'; i += 2; currentCol += 2; continue;
      }
      if (ch === '`') { state = 'CODE'; lastNonWhitespaceChar = '`'; i++; currentCol++; continue; }
      if (ch === '\n') { currentLine++; currentCol = 1; } else { currentCol++; }
      i++;
      continue;
    }

    if (state === 'REGEX') {
      if (ch === '\\') { i += 2; currentCol += 2; continue; }
      if (ch === '[' && !inRegexCharClass) { inRegexCharClass = true; i++; currentCol++; continue; }
      if (ch === ']' && inRegexCharClass) { inRegexCharClass = false; i++; currentCol++; continue; }
      if (ch === '/' && !inRegexCharClass) {
        state = 'CODE'; lastNonWhitespaceChar = '/'; i++; currentCol++;
        while (i < len && /[a-z]/i.test(content[i])) { i++; currentCol++; }
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === '\n') {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: stringStartLine, column: stringStartCol,
          message: 'Unterminated regular expression literal.', codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
        });
        state = 'CODE'; currentLine++; currentCol = 1; i++; continue;
      }
      currentCol++; i++;
      continue;
    }

    // STATE: 'CODE'
    if (ch === '/' && nextCh === '/') { state = 'LINE_COMMENT'; i += 2; currentCol += 2; continue; }
    if (ch === '/' && nextCh === '*') {
      state = 'BLOCK_COMMENT'; stringStartLine = currentLine; stringStartCol = currentCol;
      i += 2; currentCol += 2; continue;
    }

    if (ch === '/') {
      const regexPreceders = '=([{:;,?!&|+-*^~<>%return\n';
      const isRegex = lastNonWhitespaceChar === '' || regexPreceders.includes(lastNonWhitespaceChar);
      if (isRegex) {
        state = 'REGEX'; inRegexCharClass = false; stringStartLine = currentLine; stringStartCol = currentCol;
        i++; currentCol++; continue;
      }
    }

    if (ch === "'") { state = 'SINGLE_QUOTE'; stringStartLine = currentLine; stringStartCol = currentCol; i++; currentCol++; continue; }
    if (ch === '"') { state = 'DOUBLE_QUOTE'; stringStartLine = currentLine; stringStartCol = currentCol; i++; currentCol++; continue; }
    if (ch === '`') { state = 'TEMPLATE'; stringStartLine = currentLine; stringStartCol = currentCol; i++; currentCol++; continue; }

    if (ch === '(' || ch === '[' || ch === '{') {
      bracketStack.push({ char: ch, line: currentLine, col: currentCol });
      lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch;
      i++; currentCol++;
      continue;
    }

    if (ch === ')') {
      const top = bracketStack.pop();
      if (!top) {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: currentLine, column: currentCol,
          message: "Unexpected closing parenthesis ')' with no matching '('.", codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      } else if (top.char !== '(') {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: currentLine, column: currentCol,
          message: `Mismatched bracket: expected '${top.char === '{' ? '}' : top.char === '[' ? ']' : '}'}' but found ')'. (Opened at line ${top.line}, col ${top.col})`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      }
      lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch; i++; currentCol++; continue;
    }

    if (ch === ']') {
      const top = bracketStack.pop();
      if (!top) {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: currentLine, column: currentCol,
          message: "Unexpected closing bracket ']' with no matching '['.", codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      } else if (top.char !== '[') {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: currentLine, column: currentCol,
          message: `Mismatched bracket: expected '${top.char === '{' ? '}' : top.char === '(' ? ')' : '}'}' but found ']'. (Opened at line ${top.line}, col ${top.col})`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      }
      lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch; i++; currentCol++; continue;
    }

    if (ch === '}') {
      const top = bracketStack.pop();
      if (!top) {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: currentLine, column: currentCol,
          message: "Unexpected closing brace '}' with no matching '{'.", codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      } else if (top.char === '${') {
        state = 'TEMPLATE';
      } else if (top.char !== '{') {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: currentLine, column: currentCol,
          message: `Mismatched bracket: expected '${top.char === '(' ? ')' : ']'}' but found '}'. (Opened at line ${top.line}, col ${top.col})`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      }
      lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch; i++; currentCol++; continue;
    }

    if (ch === '\n') { currentLine++; currentCol = 1; i++; continue; }
    if (!/\s/.test(ch)) { lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch; }
    currentCol++; i++;
  }

  // End of file diagnostics
  if (state === 'BLOCK_COMMENT') {
    errors.push({
      file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: stringStartLine, column: stringStartCol,
      message: 'Unterminated multi-line comment ("/* ... */").', codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
    });
  } else if (state === 'TEMPLATE') {
    errors.push({
      file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: stringStartLine, column: stringStartCol,
      message: 'Unterminated template literal ("`...`").', codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
    });
  } else if (state === 'SINGLE_QUOTE' || state === 'DOUBLE_QUOTE') {
    errors.push({
      file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: stringStartLine, column: stringStartCol,
      message: `Unterminated string literal (${state === 'SINGLE_QUOTE' ? "'" : '"'}).`, codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
    });
  }

  // Check remaining open brackets
  while (bracketStack.length > 0) {
    const unclosed = bracketStack.pop()!;
    const name = unclosed.char === '(' ? "parenthesis '('" : unclosed.char === '[' ? "bracket '['" : unclosed.char === '${' ? "template expression '${'" : "brace '{'";
    errors.push({
      file: filePath, stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX', line: unclosed.line, column: unclosed.col,
      message: `Unclosed ${name} opened at line ${unclosed.line}, column ${unclosed.col}.`, codeSnippet: getSnippet(unclosed.line)
    });
  }

  return errors;
}

================
File: packages/edge-worker/src/modules/webhookHandler.ts

import type {
  HybridWorkerEnv,
  HybridTaskResult,
  GitHubWebhookPayload,
  GeminiPatchPayload
} from '../types';
import { handleMixRequest } from './mixer';
import { handlePatchRequest } from './patcher';

/**
 * Computes an HMAC-SHA256 signature in GitHub format ("sha256=<hex>").
 * Uses pure Web Crypto API (SubtleCrypto).
 */
export async function computeGithubWebhookSignature(
  rawBody: string | ArrayBuffer,
  secret: string
): Promise<string> {
  const enc = new TextEncoder();
  const keyData = enc.encode(secret);
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    keyData,
    { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' },
    false,
    ['sign']
  );
  const data = typeof rawBody === 'string' ? enc.encode(rawBody) : new Uint8Array(rawBody);
  const signature = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, data);
  const hex = Array.from(new Uint8Array(signature))
    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('');
  return `sha256=${hex}`;
}

/**
 * Verifies GitHub x-hub-signature-256 header using Web Crypto API.
 * Guarantees constant-time comparison via crypto.subtle.verify.
 */
export async function verifyGithubWebhookSignature(
  rawBody: string | ArrayBuffer,
  signatureHeader: string | null | undefined,
  secret: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!signatureHeader || !secret) {
    return false;
  }
  if (!signatureHeader.startsWith('sha256=')) {
    return false;
  }
  const hex = signatureHeader.slice(7).trim();
  if (hex.length !== 64 || !/^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(hex)) {
    return false;
  }

  const sigBytes = new Uint8Array(32);
  for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
    sigBytes[i] = parseInt(hex.substring(i * 2, i * 2 + 2), 16);
  }

  const enc = new TextEncoder();
  const keyData = enc.encode(secret);
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    keyData,
    { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' },
    false,
    ['verify']
  );

  const data = typeof rawBody === 'string' ? enc.encode(rawBody) : new Uint8Array(rawBody);
  return await crypto.subtle.verify('HMAC', key, sigBytes, data);
}

/**
 * Helper to post feedback comments back to GitHub PR/Issue.
 */
export async function postGithubComment(
  commentsUrl: string,
  body: string,
  token: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(commentsUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        'User-Agent': 'gemini-git-bridge/2.0.0'
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ body })
    });
    return res.ok;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Parses Gemini response text and extracts JSON patch payload.
 */
export function extractPatchPayload(text: string): GeminiPatchPayload {
  const match = text.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/i);
  const rawJson = (match && match[1]) ? match[1].trim() : text.trim();
  return JSON.parse(rawJson) as GeminiPatchPayload;
}

/**
 * Executes the full background worker workflow:
 * 1. Mixes repo context via handleMixRequest
 * 2. Asks Gemini REST API for GeminiPatchPayload
 * 3. Applies patches in-memory and commits via handlePatchRequest
 * 4. Posts result comment back to GitHub Issue or PR
 */
export async function executeWorkerWorkflow(
  payload: GitHubWebhookPayload,
  env: HybridWorkerEnv
): Promise<HybridTaskResult> {
  const repo = payload.repository?.full_name;
  if (!repo) {
    return { mode: 'worker', status: 'ERROR', message: 'Missing repository in payload' };
  }

  const branch =
    payload.pull_request?.head?.ref ||
    payload.repository?.default_branch ||
    'main';

  const rawComment =
    payload.comment?.body ||
    payload.issue?.body ||
    payload.pull_request?.body ||
    '';

  // Strip @gemini mention
  const prompt = rawComment.replace(/@gemini\b/gi, '').trim();
  if (!prompt) {
    return { mode: 'worker', status: 'ERROR', message: 'No instruction prompt found after @gemini mention' };
  }

  const githubToken = env.GITHUB_PAT || env.GITHUB_TOKEN;
  if (!githubToken) {
    return { mode: 'worker', status: 'ERROR', message: 'GITHUB_PAT / GITHUB_TOKEN not configured in Worker environment' };
  }

  const commentsUrl = payload.issue?.comments_url || payload.pull_request?.comments_url;

  try {
    // 1. Repo Mix
    const mixResult = await handleMixRequest({ repo, branch }, githubToken);
    if (mixResult.status !== 'OK') {
      const err = mixResult.errorMessage || 'Failed to assemble repository context';
      if (commentsUrl) {
        await postGithubComment(commentsUrl, `❌ **Gemini Bridge Error:** Failed to mix repository context: ${err}`, githubToken);
      }
      return { mode: 'worker', status: 'ERROR', message: err };
    }

    // 2. Query Gemini REST API
    const geminiApiKey = env.GEMINI_API_KEY;
    if (!geminiApiKey) {
      const err = 'GEMINI_API_KEY not configured in Worker environment';
      if (commentsUrl) {
        await postGithubComment(commentsUrl, `❌ **Gemini Bridge Error:** ${err}`, githubToken);
      }
      return { mode: 'worker', status: 'ERROR', message: err };
    }

    const systemPrompt = `You are an automated Git patch assistant.
Given a repository XML context and an issue prompt, return ONLY a valid JSON object strictly conforming to the GeminiPatchPayload schema.
Do NOT output markdown fences or additional text.`;

    const userQuery = `Task Instruction:\n${prompt}\n\nRepository Context:\n${mixResult.xmlContent}`;

    const geminiRes = await fetch(
      `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${geminiApiKey}`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          contents: [
            {
              role: 'user',
              parts: [{ text: `${systemPrompt}\n\n${userQuery}` }]
            }
          ]
        })
      }
    );

    if (!geminiRes.ok) {
      const statusText = await geminiRes.text();
      const err = `Gemini API call failed (${geminiRes.status}): ${statusText}`;
      if (commentsUrl) {
        await postGithubComment(commentsUrl, `❌ **Gemini Bridge Error:** ${err}`, githubToken);
      }
      return { mode: 'worker', status: 'ERROR', message: err };
    }

    const geminiData: any = await geminiRes.json();
    const candidateText = geminiData?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || '';
    const patchPayload = extractPatchPayload(candidateText);

    // 3. Apply Patch via Patcher Engine
    const patchResponse = await handlePatchRequest(
      {
        repo,
        branch,
        expectedHeadOid: mixResult.headOid,
        payload: patchPayload
      },
      githubToken
    );

    // 4. Post Feedback Comment
    if (commentsUrl) {
      if (patchResponse.status === 'COMMITTED') {
        const msg = `✅ **Gemini Bridge applied patch successfully!**\n\n- Commit: \`${patchResponse.newHeadOid}\`\n- Headline: ${patchPayload.commitMessage}`;
        await postGithubComment(commentsUrl, msg, githubToken);
      } else if (patchResponse.status === 'PREFLIGHT_FAILED') {
        const errorList = (patchResponse.errors || [])
          .map((e) => `- [${e.stage}] \`${e.file}:${e.line || 1}\` ${e.message}`)
          .join('\n');
        const msg = `❌ **Gemini Bridge Preflight Linter Failed:**\n\n${errorList}`;
        await postGithubComment(commentsUrl, msg, githubToken);
      } else {
        const msg = `⚠️ **Gemini Bridge Patch Result:** ${patchResponse.status}\n${patchResponse.errorMessage || ''}`;
        await postGithubComment(commentsUrl, msg, githubToken);
      }
    }

    return {
      mode: 'worker',
      status: patchResponse.status === 'COMMITTED' ? 'SUCCESS' : (patchResponse.status === 'PREFLIGHT_FAILED' ? 'PREFLIGHT_FAILED' : 'ERROR'),
      commitSha: patchResponse.newHeadOid,
      errors: patchResponse.errors,
      message: patchResponse.errorMessage
    };
  } catch (err: any) {
    if (commentsUrl) {
      await postGithubComment(commentsUrl, `💥 **Gemini Bridge Execution Exception:** ${err.message}`, githubToken);
    }
    return { mode: 'worker', status: 'ERROR', message: err.message, errors: [err] };
  }
}

/**
 * Route handler for POST /api/webhook/github.
 * Verifies HMAC signature, validates @gemini trigger, responds with 202 immediately,
 * and schedules background execution via ctx.waitUntil.
 */
export async function handleGithubWebhookRequest(
  request: Request,
  env: HybridWorkerEnv,
  ctx?: { waitUntil: (p: Promise<any>) => void }
): Promise<Response> {
  if (request.method !== 'POST') {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Method Not Allowed' }), {
      status: 405,
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
    });
  }

  const rawBody = await request.text();
  const signatureHeader = request.headers.get('x-hub-signature-256');
  const secret = env.GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET || env.WORKER_SECRET || env.BRIDGE_SECRET || '';

  const isValid = await verifyGithubWebhookSignature(rawBody, signatureHeader, secret);
  if (!isValid) {
    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ success: false, error: 'Unauthorized: Invalid GitHub webhook HMAC signature' }),
      { status: 401, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    );
  }

  let payload: GitHubWebhookPayload;
  try {
    payload = JSON.parse(rawBody) as GitHubWebhookPayload;
  } catch {
    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ success: false, error: 'Malformed JSON payload' }),
      { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    );
  }

  const commentText =
    payload.comment?.body ||
    payload.issue?.body ||
    payload.pull_request?.body ||
    '';

  const hasGeminiMention = /@gemini\b/i.test(commentText);
  if (!hasGeminiMention) {
    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ status: 'IGNORED', message: 'No @gemini trigger found in payload' }),
      { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    );
  }

  // P1: Respond immediately with 202 Accepted to prevent GitHub 10s webhook timeout.
  // Execution runs in background via ctx.waitUntil.
  const workflowPromise = executeWorkerWorkflow(payload, env).catch((err) => {
    console.error('[webhookHandler] Background workflow unhandled error:', err);
  });

  if (ctx && typeof ctx.waitUntil === 'function') {
    ctx.waitUntil(workflowPromise);
  }

  return new Response(
    JSON.stringify({
      status: 'ACCEPTED',
      message: 'Webhook received, signature verified, and execution scheduled.'
    }),
    { status: 202, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
  );
}

/**
 * Chunk-based UTF-8 to Base64 encoding.
 * Processes bytes in chunks (32KB) to prevent "Maximum call stack size exceeded" errors
 * that occur with large files when using String.fromCharCode.apply on large arrays.
 */
export function utf8ToBase64(str: string): string {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
  const CHUNK_SIZE = 0x8000; // 32768 bytes
  let binary = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += CHUNK_SIZE) {
    const chunk = bytes.subarray(i, i + CHUNK_SIZE);
    binary += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, chunk as unknown as number[]);
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

/**
 * Decodes Base64 string back to UTF-8 string safely.
 */
export function base64ToUtf8(base64: string): string {
  const binary = atob(base64);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) {
    bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
  }
  return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
}

File: packages/edge-worker/src/utils/github.ts

import type { GitHubCommitResult } from '../types';
import { utf8ToBase64 } from './encoding';

const GITHUB_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT = 'https://api.github.com/graphql';

/**
 * Splits "owner/repo" into { owner, repo }.
 */
export function parseRepoOwner(repoInput: string, ownerInput?: string): { owner: string; repo: string } {
  if (ownerInput && repoInput && !repoInput.includes('/')) {
    return { owner: ownerInput.trim(), repo: repoInput.trim() };
  }
  const parts = repoInput.trim().replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '').split('/');
  if (parts.length < 2 || !parts[0] || !parts[1]) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid repository format "${repoInput}". Expected "owner/repo".`);
  }
  return {
    owner: parts[0],
    repo: parts.slice(1).join('/')
  };
}

/**
 * Generic GitHub GraphQL executor.
 */
export async function executeGraphQL<T = any>(
  query: string,
  variables: Record<string, any>,
  token: string
): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(GITHUB_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'User-Agent': 'Cloudflare-Worker-gemini-git-bridge',
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json'
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables })
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    throw new Error(`GitHub GraphQL HTTP error ${res.status} (${res.statusText}): ${text}`);
  }

  const json: any = await res.json();
  if (json.errors && json.errors.length > 0) {
    const msg = json.errors.map((e: any) => e.message).join(' | ');
    throw new Error(`GitHub GraphQL error: ${msg}`);
  }

  return json.data as T;
}

/**
 * Retrieves the current HEAD commit OID for a branch.
 */
export async function getBranchHeadOid(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  token: string
): Promise<{ headOid: string; resolvedBranch: string; repositoryId?: string }> {
  const qualifiedBranch = branch.startsWith('refs/heads/') ? branch : `refs/heads/${branch}`;

  const query = `
    query GetBranchHead($owner: String!, $repo: String!, $branch: String!) {
      repository(owner: $owner, name: $repo) {
        id
        ref(qualifiedName: $branch) { name target { oid } }
        defaultBranchRef { name target { oid } }
      }
    }
  `;

  const data = await executeGraphQL<any>(query, { owner, repo, branch: qualifiedBranch }, token);
  const repository = data?.repository;

  if (!repository) {
    throw new Error(`Repository "${owner}/${repo}" was not found or is not accessible with the provided token.`);
  }

  if (repository.ref?.target?.oid) {
    return { headOid: repository.ref.target.oid, resolvedBranch: repository.ref.name, repositoryId: repository.id };
  }

  if (!branch && repository.defaultBranchRef?.target?.oid) {
    return { headOid: repository.defaultBranchRef.target.oid, resolvedBranch: repository.defaultBranchRef.name, repositoryId: repository.id };
  }

  throw new Error(`Branch "${branch}" (or "${qualifiedBranch}") not found in repository "${owner}/${repo}".`);
}

/**
 * Creates a new Git ref (branch) pointing to a specific commit OID.
 */
export async function createBranchRef(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branchName: string,
  oid: string,
  token: string,
  repositoryId?: string
): Promise<{ refName: string; oid: string }> {
  const cleanBranch = branchName.replace(/^refs\/heads\//, '');
  const qualifiedName = `refs/heads/${cleanBranch}`;

  // 1. Try GraphQL createRef mutation if repositoryId is known
  if (repositoryId) {
    const mutation = `
      mutation CreateRef($input: CreateRefInput!) {
        createRef(input: $input) { ref { name target { oid } } }
      }
    `;
    try {
      const data = await executeGraphQL<any>(mutation, { input: { repositoryId, name: qualifiedName, oid } }, token);
      const ref = data?.createRef?.ref;
      if (ref?.name) return { refName: ref.name, oid: ref.target?.oid || oid };
    } catch {
      // Fall through to REST if GraphQL fails
    }
  }

  // 2. REST API fallback
  const restUrl = `https://api.github.com/repos/${encodeURIComponent(owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo)}/git/refs`;
  const res = await fetch(restUrl, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'User-Agent': 'Cloudflare-Worker-gemini-git-bridge',
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json'
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ ref: qualifiedName, sha: oid })
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const errText = await res.text();
    throw new Error(`Failed to create branch "${cleanBranch}" on "${owner}/${repo}": HTTP ${res.status} - ${errText}`);
  }

  const json: any = await res.json();
  return { refName: json.ref, oid: json.object?.sha || oid };
}

/**
 * Fetches existing file content from GitHub using GraphQL Blob or REST raw content fallback.
 */
export async function fetchFileContent(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  path: string,
  token: string
): Promise<string> {
  // 1. Try GraphQL Blob object expression
  const expression = `${branch}:${path}`;
  const query = `
    query GetFileBlob($owner: String!, $repo: String!, $expression: String!) {
      repository(owner: $owner, name: $repo) {
        object(expression: $expression) {
          ... on Blob {
            text
            isTruncated
          }
        }
      }
    }
  `;

  try {
    const data = await executeGraphQL<any>(query, { owner, repo, expression }, token);
    const blob = data?.repository?.object;
    if (blob && typeof blob.text === 'string' && !blob.isTruncated) {
      return blob.text;
    }
  } catch {
    // If GraphQL blob query failed, proceed to REST fallback
  }

  // 2. REST raw contents fallback
  const encodedPath = path.split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/');
  const restUrl = `https://api.github.com/repos/${encodeURIComponent(owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo)}/contents/${encodedPath}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`;
  const res = await fetch(restUrl, {
    headers: {
      'User-Agent': 'Cloudflare-Worker-gemini-git-bridge',
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
      'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.raw+json'
    }
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Failed to fetch file "${path}" from "${owner}/${repo}" on branch "${branch}": HTTP ${res.status}`);
  }

  return await res.text();
}

export interface BatchFetchParams {
  repo: string; // "owner/repo"
  branch: string;
  paths: string[];
  token: string;
}

/**
 * Fetches multiple files in a SINGLE GitHub GraphQL request using dynamic aliases (f0, f1, ...).
 * Returns a map of { [path]: content | null } where null indicates the file does not exist on the branch.
 */
export async function fetchMultipleFilesViaGraphQL(
  params: BatchFetchParams
): Promise<Record<string, string | null>>;
export async function fetchMultipleFilesViaGraphQL(
  repoWithOwner: string,
  branch: string,
  paths: string[],
  token: string
): Promise<Record<string, string | null>>;
export async function fetchMultipleFilesViaGraphQL(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  paths: string[],
  token: string
): Promise<Record<string, string | null>>;
export async function fetchMultipleFilesViaGraphQL(
  arg1: string | BatchFetchParams,
  arg2?: string,
  arg3?: string | string[],
  arg4?: string | string[],
  arg5?: string
): Promise<Record<string, string | null>> {
  let owner: string;
  let repo: string;
  let branch: string;
  let paths: string[];
  let token: string;

  if (typeof arg1 === 'object' && arg1 !== null) {
    const parsed = parseRepoOwner(arg1.repo);
    owner = parsed.owner;
    repo = parsed.repo;
    branch = arg1.branch;
    paths = arg1.paths;
    token = arg1.token;
  } else if (Array.isArray(arg3) && typeof arg4 === 'string') {
    const parsed = parseRepoOwner(arg1);
    owner = parsed.owner;
    repo = parsed.repo;
    branch = arg2 as string;
    paths = arg3;
    token = arg4;
  } else {
    owner = arg1;
    repo = arg2 as string;
    branch = arg3 as string;
    paths = arg4 as string[];
    token = arg5 as string;
  }

  const uniquePaths = Array.from(new Set(paths));
  const result: Record<string, string | null> = {};
  if (uniquePaths.length === 0) {
    return result;
  }

  const varDefs: string[] = ['$owner: String!', '$repo: String!'];
  const fields: string[] = [];
  const variables: Record<string, any> = { owner, repo };

  uniquePaths.forEach((p, idx) => {
    const varName = `exp_${idx}`;
    varDefs.push(`$${varName}: String!`);
    variables[varName] = `${branch}:${p}`;
    fields.push(`
      f${idx}: object(expression: $${varName}) {
        ... on Blob {
          text
          isTruncated
        }
      }
    `);
  });

  const query = `
    query BatchGetFileBlobs(${varDefs.join(', ')}) {
      repository(owner: $owner, name: $repo) {
        ${fields.join('\n')}
      }
    }
  `;

  const data = await executeGraphQL<any>(query, variables, token);
  const repoObj = data?.repository;
  if (!repoObj) {
    throw new Error(`Repository "${owner}/${repo}" was not found or is not accessible.`);
  }

  for (let idx = 0; idx < uniquePaths.length; idx++) {
    const p = uniquePaths[idx];
    const blob = repoObj[`f${idx}`];
    if (!blob) {
      result[p] = null;
    } else if (blob.isTruncated) {
      result[p] = await fetchFileContent(owner, repo, branch, p, token);
    } else {
      result[p] = typeof blob.text === 'string' ? blob.text : null;
    }
  }

  return result;
}

/**
 * Executes createCommitOnBranch mutation.
 */
export async function createCommitOnBranch(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  expectedHeadOid: string,
  message: string,
  additions: { path: string; contents: string }[],
  deletions: { path: string }[],
  token: string
): Promise<GitHubCommitResult> {
  const mutation = `
    mutation CreateCommitOnBranch($input: CreateCommitOnBranchInput!) {
      createCommitOnBranch(input: $input) {
        commit {
          oid
          url
          messageHeadline
        }
      }
    }
  `;

  const headline = message.split('\n')[0].trim();
  const body = message.includes('\n') ? message.split('\n').slice(1).join('\n').trim() : undefined;

  const fileChanges: Record<string, any> = {};
  if (additions.length > 0) {
    fileChanges.additions = additions;
  }
  if (deletions.length > 0) {
    fileChanges.deletions = deletions;
  }

  const cleanBranch = branch.replace(/^refs\/heads\//, '');

  const variables = {
    input: {
      branch: {
        repositoryNameWithOwner: `${owner}/${repo}`,
        branchName: cleanBranch
      },
      expectedHeadOid,
      message: {
        headline,
        body: body || null
      },
      fileChanges
    }
  };

  const data = await executeGraphQL<any>(mutation, variables, token);
  const commit = data?.createCommitOnBranch?.commit;

  if (!commit?.oid) {
    throw new Error('createCommitOnBranch did not return commit information.');
  }

  return {
    oid: commit.oid,
    url: commit.url,
    messageHeadline: commit.messageHeadline
  };
}

  branch: string;
  expectedHeadOid: string;
  path: string;
  content: string;
  commitMessage: string;
  token: string;
}

/**
 * Commits a single UTF-8 file via GitHub GraphQL createCommitOnBranch (e.g. for Stage 1 /_context/repo-mix.xml persistence).
 */
export async function commitSingleFileViaGraphQL(
  params: CommitSingleFileParams
): Promise<GitHubCommitResult>;
export async function commitSingleFileViaGraphQL(
  repoWithOwner: string,
  branch: string,
  expectedHeadOid: string,
  path: string,
  content: string,
  commitMessage: string,
  token: string
): Promise<GitHubCommitResult>;
export async function commitSingleFileViaGraphQL(
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  expectedHeadOid: string,
  path: string,
  content: string,
  commitMessage: string,
  token: string
): Promise<GitHubCommitResult>;
export async function commitSingleFileViaGraphQL(
  arg1: string | CommitSingleFileParams,
  arg2?: string,
  arg3?: string,
  arg4?: string,
  arg5?: string,
  arg6?: string,
  arg7?: string,
  arg8?: string
): Promise<GitHubCommitResult> {
  if (typeof arg1 === 'object' && arg1 !== null) {
    const { owner, repo } = parseRepoOwner(arg1.repo);
    const encoded = utf8ToBase64(arg1.content);
    return createCommitOnBranch(
      owner,
      repo,
      arg1.branch,
      arg1.expectedHeadOid,
      arg1.commitMessage,
      [{ path: arg1.path, contents: encoded }],
      [],
      arg1.token
    );
  }

  if (typeof arg8 === 'string') {
    const encoded = utf8ToBase64(arg6 as string);
    return createCommitOnBranch(
      arg1,
      arg2 as string,
      arg3 as string,
      arg4 as string,
      arg7 as string,
      [{ path: arg5 as string, contents: encoded }],
      [],
      arg8
    );
  }

  const { owner, repo } = parseRepoOwner(arg1);
  const encoded = utf8ToBase64(arg5 as string);
  return createCommitOnBranch(
    owner,
    repo,
    arg2 as string,
    arg3 as string,
    arg6 as string,
    [{ path: arg4 as string, contents: encoded }],
    [],
    arg7 as string
  );
}
