// packages/local-cli/src/localRunner.ts
import * as fs from "node:fs";
import * as path from "node:path";
import { execSync } from "node:child_process";

// packages/edge-worker/src/modules/syntaxScanner.ts
var registeredOxcParser = null;
var SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS = [".ts", ".tsx", ".js", ".jsx", ".mjs", ".cjs", ".json"];
function validateJson(filePath, content, getSnippet) {
  try {
    JSON.parse(content);
    return [];
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err.message || "Invalid JSON";
    let line = 1;
    let col = 1;
    const posMatch = msg.match(/at position (\d+)/i);
    if (posMatch) {
      const pos = parseInt(posMatch[1], 10);
      const prefixLines = content.slice(0, pos).split("\n");
      line = prefixLines.length;
      col = prefixLines[prefixLines.length - 1].length + 1;
    } else {
      const lcMatch = msg.match(/line (\d+).*?column (\d+)/i);
      if (lcMatch) {
        line = parseInt(lcMatch[1], 10);
        col = parseInt(lcMatch[2], 10);
      }
    }
    return [{
      file: filePath,
      stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
      line,
      column: col,
      message: `JSON SyntaxError: ${msg}`,
      codeSnippet: getSnippet(line)
    }];
  }
}
async function runOxcWasm(filePath, content, getSnippet) {
  if (!registeredOxcParser) return null;
  try {
    const res = await registeredOxcParser(filePath, content);
    if (res && Array.isArray(res.errors) && res.errors.length > 0) {
      return res.errors.map((err) => ({
        file: filePath,
        stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
        line: err.line || 1,
        column: err.column || 1,
        message: `OXC SyntaxError: ${err.message || String(err)}`,
        codeSnippet: err.line ? getSnippet(err.line) : void 0
      }));
    }
    return [];
  } catch (oxcErr) {
    return [{
      file: filePath,
      stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
      line: 1,
      column: 1,
      message: `OXC Parser failure: ${oxcErr.message || String(oxcErr)}`,
      codeSnippet: getSnippet(1)
    }];
  }
}
async function validateSyntaxInWorker(filePath, content) {
  const ext = filePath.includes(".") ? filePath.slice(filePath.lastIndexOf(".")).toLowerCase() : "";
  if (!SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS.includes(ext)) {
    return [];
  }
  const errors = [];
  const lines = content.split("\n");
  const getSnippet = (lineNum) => {
    const idx = lineNum - 1;
    return idx >= 0 && idx < lines.length ? lines[idx].trim() : "";
  };
  if (ext === ".json") {
    return validateJson(filePath, content, getSnippet);
  }
  const oxcErrors = await runOxcWasm(filePath, content, getSnippet);
  if (oxcErrors) {
    return oxcErrors;
  }
  let currentLine = 1;
  let currentCol = 1;
  const bracketStack = [];
  let state = "CODE";
  let stringStartLine = 1;
  let stringStartCol = 1;
  let lastNonWhitespaceChar = "";
  let inRegexCharClass = false;
  const len = content.length;
  let i = 0;
  while (i < len) {
    const ch = content[i];
    const nextCh = i + 1 < len ? content[i + 1] : "";
    if (currentCol === 1 && (ch === "<" || ch === "=" || ch === ">")) {
      const restOfLine = content.slice(i, i + 10);
      if (restOfLine.startsWith("<<<<<<<") || restOfLine.startsWith("=======") || restOfLine.startsWith(">>>>>>>")) {
        const marker = restOfLine.slice(0, 7);
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
          line: currentLine,
          column: currentCol,
          message: `Git merge conflict marker detected ("${marker}"). Resolve all conflict markers before committing.`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      }
    }
    if (state === "LINE_COMMENT") {
      if (ch === "\n") {
        state = "CODE";
        currentLine++;
        currentCol = 1;
      } else {
        currentCol++;
      }
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (state === "BLOCK_COMMENT") {
      if (ch === "*" && nextCh === "/") {
        state = "CODE";
        i += 2;
        currentCol += 2;
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "\n") {
        currentLine++;
        currentCol = 1;
      } else {
        currentCol++;
      }
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (state === "SINGLE_QUOTE" || state === "DOUBLE_QUOTE") {
      const quoteChar = state === "SINGLE_QUOTE" ? "'" : '"';
      if (ch === "\\") {
        i += 2;
        currentCol += 2;
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === quoteChar) {
        state = "CODE";
        lastNonWhitespaceChar = quoteChar;
        i++;
        currentCol++;
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "\n") {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
          line: stringStartLine,
          column: stringStartCol,
          message: `Unterminated ${state === "SINGLE_QUOTE" ? "single" : "double"}-quoted string literal.`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
        });
        state = "CODE";
        currentLine++;
        currentCol = 1;
        i++;
        continue;
      }
      currentCol++;
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (state === "TEMPLATE") {
      if (ch === "\\") {
        i += 2;
        currentCol += 2;
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "$" && nextCh === "{") {
        bracketStack.push({ char: "${", line: currentLine, col: currentCol });
        state = "CODE";
        i += 2;
        currentCol += 2;
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "`") {
        state = "CODE";
        lastNonWhitespaceChar = "`";
        i++;
        currentCol++;
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "\n") {
        currentLine++;
        currentCol = 1;
      } else {
        currentCol++;
      }
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (state === "REGEX") {
      if (ch === "\\") {
        i += 2;
        currentCol += 2;
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "[" && !inRegexCharClass) {
        inRegexCharClass = true;
        i++;
        currentCol++;
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "]" && inRegexCharClass) {
        inRegexCharClass = false;
        i++;
        currentCol++;
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "/" && !inRegexCharClass) {
        state = "CODE";
        lastNonWhitespaceChar = "/";
        i++;
        currentCol++;
        while (i < len && /[a-z]/i.test(content[i])) {
          i++;
          currentCol++;
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "\n") {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
          line: stringStartLine,
          column: stringStartCol,
          message: "Unterminated regular expression literal.",
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
        });
        state = "CODE";
        currentLine++;
        currentCol = 1;
        i++;
        continue;
      }
      currentCol++;
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "/" && nextCh === "/") {
      state = "LINE_COMMENT";
      i += 2;
      currentCol += 2;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "/" && nextCh === "*") {
      state = "BLOCK_COMMENT";
      stringStartLine = currentLine;
      stringStartCol = currentCol;
      i += 2;
      currentCol += 2;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "/") {
      const regexPreceders = "=([{:;,?!&|+-*^~<>%return\n";
      const isRegex = lastNonWhitespaceChar === "" || regexPreceders.includes(lastNonWhitespaceChar);
      if (isRegex) {
        state = "REGEX";
        inRegexCharClass = false;
        stringStartLine = currentLine;
        stringStartCol = currentCol;
        i++;
        currentCol++;
        continue;
      }
    }
    if (ch === "'") {
      state = "SINGLE_QUOTE";
      stringStartLine = currentLine;
      stringStartCol = currentCol;
      i++;
      currentCol++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === '"') {
      state = "DOUBLE_QUOTE";
      stringStartLine = currentLine;
      stringStartCol = currentCol;
      i++;
      currentCol++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "`") {
      state = "TEMPLATE";
      stringStartLine = currentLine;
      stringStartCol = currentCol;
      i++;
      currentCol++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "(" || ch === "[" || ch === "{") {
      bracketStack.push({ char: ch, line: currentLine, col: currentCol });
      lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch;
      i++;
      currentCol++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === ")") {
      const top = bracketStack.pop();
      if (!top) {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
          line: currentLine,
          column: currentCol,
          message: "Unexpected closing parenthesis ')' with no matching '('.",
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      } else if (top.char !== "(") {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
          line: currentLine,
          column: currentCol,
          message: `Mismatched bracket: expected '${top.char === "{" ? "}" : top.char === "[" ? "]" : "}"}' but found ')'. (Opened at line ${top.line}, col ${top.col})`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      }
      lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch;
      i++;
      currentCol++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "]") {
      const top = bracketStack.pop();
      if (!top) {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
          line: currentLine,
          column: currentCol,
          message: "Unexpected closing bracket ']' with no matching '['.",
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      } else if (top.char !== "[") {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
          line: currentLine,
          column: currentCol,
          message: `Mismatched bracket: expected '${top.char === "{" ? "}" : top.char === "(" ? ")" : "}"}' but found ']'. (Opened at line ${top.line}, col ${top.col})`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      }
      lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch;
      i++;
      currentCol++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "}") {
      const top = bracketStack.pop();
      if (!top) {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
          line: currentLine,
          column: currentCol,
          message: "Unexpected closing brace '}' with no matching '{'.",
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      } else if (top.char === "${") {
        state = "TEMPLATE";
      } else if (top.char !== "{") {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
          line: currentLine,
          column: currentCol,
          message: `Mismatched bracket: expected '${top.char === "(" ? ")" : "]"}' but found '}'. (Opened at line ${top.line}, col ${top.col})`,
          codeSnippet: getSnippet(currentLine)
        });
      }
      lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch;
      i++;
      currentCol++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "\n") {
      currentLine++;
      currentCol = 1;
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (!/\s/.test(ch)) {
      lastNonWhitespaceChar = ch;
    }
    currentCol++;
    i++;
  }
  if (state === "BLOCK_COMMENT") {
    errors.push({
      file: filePath,
      stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
      line: stringStartLine,
      column: stringStartCol,
      message: 'Unterminated multi-line comment ("/* ... */").',
      codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
    });
  } else if (state === "TEMPLATE") {
    errors.push({
      file: filePath,
      stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
      line: stringStartLine,
      column: stringStartCol,
      message: 'Unterminated template literal ("`...`").',
      codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
    });
  } else if (state === "SINGLE_QUOTE" || state === "DOUBLE_QUOTE") {
    errors.push({
      file: filePath,
      stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
      line: stringStartLine,
      column: stringStartCol,
      message: `Unterminated string literal (${state === "SINGLE_QUOTE" ? "'" : '"'}).`,
      codeSnippet: getSnippet(stringStartLine)
    });
  }
  while (bracketStack.length > 0) {
    const unclosed = bracketStack.pop();
    const name = unclosed.char === "(" ? "parenthesis '('" : unclosed.char === "[" ? "bracket '['" : unclosed.char === "${" ? "template expression '${'" : "brace '{'";
    errors.push({
      file: filePath,
      stage: "OXC_SYNTAX",
      line: unclosed.line,
      column: unclosed.col,
      message: `Unclosed ${name} opened at line ${unclosed.line}, column ${unclosed.col}.`,
      codeSnippet: getSnippet(unclosed.line)
    });
  }
  return errors;
}

// packages/edge-worker/src/modules/linter.ts
async function validateSyntaxInWorker2(filePath, content) {
  return validateSyntaxInWorker(filePath, content);
}
function runCustomPreflightRules(files, preflight) {
  const errors = [];
  for (const file of files) {
    const phErrors = checkPlaceholderErrors(file.content, file.path);
    if (phErrors.length > 0) {
      errors.push(...phErrors);
    }
  }
  const builtinErrors = runBuiltinPreflightRules(files, preflight);
  if (builtinErrors.length > 0) {
    errors.push(...builtinErrors);
  }
  return errors;
}
async function runPreflightLinter(files, preflight) {
  const errors = [];
  for (const file of files) {
    const syntaxErrors = await validateSyntaxInWorker2(file.path, file.content);
    if (syntaxErrors.length > 0) {
      errors.push(...syntaxErrors);
    }
  }
  const customErrors = runCustomPreflightRules(files, preflight);
  if (customErrors.length > 0) {
    errors.push(...customErrors);
  }
  const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  const deduplicated = [];
  for (const err of errors) {
    const key = `${err.file}:${err.stage}:${err.line ?? 0}:${err.column ?? 0}:${err.message}`;
    if (!seen.has(key)) {
      seen.add(key);
      deduplicated.push(err);
    }
  }
  return deduplicated;
}

// packages/edge-worker/src/modules/patcher.ts
var LLM_PLACEHOLDER_PATTERNS = [
  /\/\/\s*\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of|remaining|other)?\s*code\s*\.{0,3}/i,
  /#\s*\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of|remaining|other)?\s*code\s*\.{0,3}/i,
  /\/\*\s*\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of|remaining|other)?\s*code\s*\.{0,3}\s*\*\//i,
  /<!--\s*\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of|remaining|other)?\s*code\s*\.{0,3}\s*-->/i,
  /\.{3,}\s*(?:existing|rest of)\s*code\s*\.{3,}/i,
  /\/\/\s*\.{3,}\s*$/m
];
function checkPlaceholderErrors(content, filePath) {
  return checkPlaceholderErrors2(content, filePath);
}
function runBuiltinPreflightRules(files, preflight) {
  return runBuiltinPreflightRules2(files, preflight);
}
function applySearchReplace(original, search, replace, filePath = "") {
  if (original.includes(search)) {
    return original.replace(search, replace);
  }
  const normOriginal = original.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n");
  const normSearch = search.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n");
  const normReplace = replace.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n");
  if (normOriginal.includes(normSearch)) {
    const updated = normOriginal.replace(normSearch, normReplace);
    if (original.includes("\r\n")) {
      return updated.replace(/\n/g, "\r\n");
    }
    return updated;
  }
  const origLines = normOriginal.split("\n");
  const searchLines = normSearch.split("\n");
  let matchStart = -1;
  for (let i = 0; i <= origLines.length - searchLines.length; i++) {
    let matches = true;
    for (let j = 0; j < searchLines.length; j++) {
      if (origLines[i + j].trim() !== searchLines[j].trim()) {
        matches = false;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (matches) {
      matchStart = i;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (matchStart !== -1) {
    const before = origLines.slice(0, matchStart).join("\n");
    const after = origLines.slice(matchStart + searchLines.length).join("\n");
    const updated = (before ? before + "\n" : "") + normReplace + (after ? "\n" + after : "");
    if (original.includes("\r\n")) {
      return updated.replace(/\n/g, "\r\n");
    }
    return updated;
  }
  const snippet = search.length > 200 ? `${search.slice(0, 200)}...` : search;
  throw new Error(`Search block could not be resolved for file "${filePath}". Target snippet:
${snippet}`);
}

// packages/edge-worker/src/modules/patchInMemory.ts
function checkPlaceholderErrors2(content, filePath) {
  const errors = [];
  const lines = content.split("\n");
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const line = lines[i];
    for (const pattern of LLM_PLACEHOLDER_PATTERNS) {
      if (pattern.test(line)) {
        errors.push({
          file: filePath,
          stage: "CUSTOM_RULE",
          line: i + 1,
          message: `Forbidden LLM placeholder pattern detected: "${line.trim()}"`,
          codeSnippet: line.trim()
        });
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  return errors;
}
function runBuiltinPreflightRules2(files, preflight) {
  const errors = [];
  if (!preflight) {
    return errors;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(preflight.forbidPatterns) && preflight.forbidPatterns.length > 0) {
    for (const pat of preflight.forbidPatterns) {
      let rx;
      try {
        rx = new RegExp(pat);
      } catch {
        continue;
      }
      for (const file of files) {
        const lines = file.content.split("\n");
        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
          const line = lines[i];
          if (rx.test(line)) {
            errors.push({
              file: file.path,
              stage: "CUSTOM_RULE",
              line: i + 1,
              message: `Forbidden pattern "${pat}" detected.`,
              codeSnippet: line.trim()
            });
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  if (preflight.requireExports && typeof preflight.requireExports === "object") {
    for (const [requiredPath, symbols] of Object.entries(preflight.requireExports)) {
      const targetFile = files.find((f) => f.path === requiredPath);
      if (!targetFile) {
        for (const symbol of symbols) {
          errors.push({
            file: requiredPath,
            stage: "CUSTOM_RULE",
            message: `Required export "${symbol}" missing because file "${requiredPath}" was not found among modified files.`
          });
        }
        continue;
      }
      for (const symbol of symbols) {
        const exportRegex = new RegExp(
          `(?:export\\s+(?:(?:default\\s+)?(?:const|let|var|function|class|interface|type|enum|async\\s+function)\\s+${symbol}\\b|export\\s*\\{[^}]*\\b${symbol}\\b[^}]*\\}|export\\s*\\*\\s*as\\s+${symbol}\\b)`
        );
        if (!exportRegex.test(targetFile.content)) {
          errors.push({
            file: requiredPath,
            stage: "CUSTOM_RULE",
            message: `Required export "${symbol}" missing in "${requiredPath}".`
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return errors;
}
async function applyPatchesInMemory(patches, existingFiles, preflight, preflightHook = runPreflightLinter) {
  const additions = [];
  const deletions = [];
  const errors = [];
  let hasEffectiveChanges = false;
  const resultingFiles = [];
  for (const op of patches) {
    if (op.action === "delete") {
      if (existingFiles[op.path] !== null) {
        deletions.push({ path: op.path });
        hasEffectiveChanges = true;
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (op.action === "create") {
      if (typeof op.fullContent !== "string") {
        errors.push({
          file: op.path,
          stage: "SEARCH_REPLACE",
          message: 'Operation "create" requires "fullContent" string.'
        });
        continue;
      }
      const phErrors = checkPlaceholderErrors2(op.fullContent, op.path);
      if (phErrors.length > 0) {
        errors.push(...phErrors);
      }
      const existing = existingFiles[op.path];
      if (existing !== op.fullContent) {
        additions.push({ path: op.path, content: op.fullContent });
        hasEffectiveChanges = true;
      }
      resultingFiles.push({ path: op.path, content: op.fullContent });
      continue;
    }
    if (op.action === "modify") {
      if (typeof op.fullContent === "string" && (!op.blocks || op.blocks.length === 0)) {
        const phErrors = checkPlaceholderErrors2(op.fullContent, op.path);
        if (phErrors.length > 0) {
          errors.push(...phErrors);
        }
        const existing2 = existingFiles[op.path];
        if (existing2 !== op.fullContent) {
          additions.push({ path: op.path, content: op.fullContent });
          hasEffectiveChanges = true;
        }
        resultingFiles.push({ path: op.path, content: op.fullContent });
        continue;
      }
      const existing = existingFiles[op.path];
      if (existing === null || existing === void 0) {
        errors.push({
          file: op.path,
          stage: "SEARCH_REPLACE",
          message: "Target file does not exist on branch"
        });
        continue;
      }
      if (!op.blocks || op.blocks.length === 0) {
        errors.push({
          file: op.path,
          stage: "SEARCH_REPLACE",
          message: 'Operation "modify" requires non-empty "blocks" or "fullContent".'
        });
        continue;
      }
      let currentContent = existing;
      let hasError = false;
      for (const block of op.blocks) {
        const phErrors = checkPlaceholderErrors2(block.replace, op.path);
        if (phErrors.length > 0) {
          errors.push(...phErrors);
          hasError = true;
        }
        try {
          currentContent = applySearchReplace(currentContent, block.search, block.replace, op.path);
        } catch (err) {
          errors.push({
            file: op.path,
            stage: "SEARCH_REPLACE",
            message: err.message,
            codeSnippet: block.search.slice(0, 200)
          });
          hasError = true;
        }
      }
      const finalPhErrors = checkPlaceholderErrors2(currentContent, op.path);
      if (finalPhErrors.length > 0) {
        errors.push(...finalPhErrors);
        hasError = true;
      }
      if (!hasError) {
        if (currentContent !== existing) {
          additions.push({ path: op.path, content: currentContent });
          hasEffectiveChanges = true;
        }
        resultingFiles.push({ path: op.path, content: currentContent });
      }
    }
  }
  const builtinErrors = runBuiltinPreflightRules2(resultingFiles, preflight);
  if (builtinErrors.length > 0) {
    errors.push(...builtinErrors);
  }
  const effectiveHook = preflightHook ?? runPreflightLinter;
  if (effectiveHook) {
    try {
      const hookErrors = await effectiveHook(resultingFiles, preflight);
      if (Array.isArray(hookErrors) && hookErrors.length > 0) {
        errors.push(...hookErrors);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      errors.push({
        file: "preflightHook",
        stage: "CUSTOM_RULE",
        message: `Preflight linter hook threw an error: ${err.message}`
      });
    }
  }
  const seenErrors = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  const uniqueErrors = [];
  for (const err of errors) {
    const key = `${err.file}:${err.stage}:${err.line ?? 0}:${err.column ?? 0}:${err.message}`;
    if (!seenErrors.has(key)) {
      seenErrors.add(key);
      uniqueErrors.push(err);
    }
  }
  return {
    additions,
    deletions,
    errors: uniqueErrors,
    hasEffectiveChanges
  };
}

// packages/local-cli/src/localRunner.ts
function loadEnvFile(cwd) {
  const envPath = path.join(cwd, ".env");
  if (!fs.existsSync(envPath)) return;
  try {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf-8");
    const lines = raw.split(/\r?\n/);
    for (const line of lines) {
      const trimmed = line.trim();
      if (!trimmed || trimmed.startsWith("#")) continue;
      const idx = trimmed.indexOf("=");
      if (idx > 0) {
        const key = trimmed.slice(0, idx).trim();
        let val = trimmed.slice(idx + 1).trim();
        if (val.startsWith('"') && val.endsWith('"') || val.startsWith("'") && val.endsWith("'")) {
          val = val.slice(1, -1);
        }
        if (!process.env[key]) {
          process.env[key] = val;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch {
  }
}
function extractJsonFromResponse(raw) {
  const trimmed = raw.trim();
  const jsonMatch = trimmed.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/i);
  if (jsonMatch && jsonMatch[1]) {
    return jsonMatch[1].trim();
  }
  return trimmed;
}
async function callGeminiForPatch(prompt, contextFiles, apiKey, model = "gemini-2.0-flash") {
  const fileContextString = contextFiles.map((f) => `### File: ${f.path}
\`\`\`
${f.content}
\`\`\``).join("\n\n");
  const systemInstruction = `You are a high-precision code modification agent.
You must respond ONLY with a valid JSON matching the GeminiPatchPayload schema.
Do NOT include explanations outside of the JSON block.
Schema:
{
  "commitMessage": "string",
  "preflight": {
    "forbidPatterns": ["optional regex string"],
    "requireExports": { "path/to/file.ts": ["exportName"] }
  },
  "patches": [
    {
      "path": "string",
      "action": "modify" | "create" | "delete",
      "blocks": [
        {
          "search": "exact string to replace",
          "replace": "new replacement string (never use placeholders like // ... existing code ...)"
        }
      ],
      "fullContent": "full content for create or full replacement"
    }
  ]
}`;
  const userContent = `Task Prompt:
${prompt}

Context Files:
${fileContextString}`;
  try {
    const genaiMod = await import("@google/genai").catch(() => null);
    if (genaiMod && genaiMod.GoogleGenAI) {
      const ai = new genaiMod.GoogleGenAI({ apiKey });
      const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
        model,
        contents: `${systemInstruction}

${userContent}`
      });
      const text = response.text || "";
      const jsonStr = extractJsonFromResponse(text);
      return JSON.parse(jsonStr);
    }
  } catch {
  }
  const endpoint = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${model}:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`;
  const res = await fetch(endpoint, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      contents: [
        {
          role: "user",
          parts: [{ text: `${systemInstruction}

${userContent}` }]
        }
      ]
    })
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errText = await res.text();
    throw new Error(`Gemini API error (${res.status}): ${errText}`);
  }
  const data = await res.json();
  const rawText = data?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || "";
  if (!rawText) {
    throw new Error("Gemini API returned empty response");
  }
  const jsonText = extractJsonFromResponse(rawText);
  return JSON.parse(jsonText);
}
async function runLocalHybridTask(input, options = {}) {
  const cwd = options.cwd || process.cwd();
  loadEnvFile(cwd);
  const apiKey = options.apiKey || process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) {
    return {
      mode: "local",
      status: "ERROR",
      message: "GEMINI_API_KEY is not set in environment or options."
    };
  }
  const targetPaths = input.targetPaths || [];
  const originalFiles = [];
  for (const relPath of targetPaths) {
    const absPath = path.resolve(cwd, relPath);
    if (fs.existsSync(absPath) && fs.statSync(absPath).isFile()) {
      originalFiles.push({
        path: relPath.replace(/\\/g, "/"),
        content: fs.readFileSync(absPath, "utf-8")
      });
    }
  }
  try {
    const patchPayload = await callGeminiForPatch(
      input.prompt,
      originalFiles,
      apiKey,
      options.model
    );
    const existingRecord = {};
    for (const f of originalFiles) {
      existingRecord[f.path] = f.content;
    }
    for (const patch of patchPayload.patches) {
      if (!(patch.path in existingRecord)) {
        const absPath = path.resolve(cwd, patch.path);
        existingRecord[patch.path] = fs.existsSync(absPath) ? fs.readFileSync(absPath, "utf-8") : null;
      }
    }
    const patchResult = await applyPatchesInMemory(
      patchPayload.patches,
      existingRecord,
      patchPayload.preflight
    );
    if (patchResult.errors.length > 0) {
      return {
        mode: "local",
        status: "PREFLIGHT_FAILED",
        errors: patchResult.errors,
        message: `Preflight checks failed with ${patchResult.errors.length} error(s).`
      };
    }
    const modifiedPaths = [];
    for (const item of patchResult.deletions) {
      const absPath = path.resolve(cwd, item.path);
      if (fs.existsSync(absPath)) {
        fs.unlinkSync(absPath);
        modifiedPaths.push(item.path);
      }
    }
    for (const item of patchResult.additions) {
      const absPath = path.resolve(cwd, item.path);
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(absPath), { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(absPath, item.content, "utf-8");
      modifiedPaths.push(item.path);
    }
    let commitSha;
    if (!options.skipCommit && modifiedPaths.length > 0) {
      try {
        const commitMsg = patchPayload.commitMessage || `Gemini Bridge local patch for: ${input.prompt.slice(0, 50)}`;
        for (const p of modifiedPaths) {
          execSync(`git add "${p}"`, { cwd, stdio: "pipe" });
        }
        execSync(`git commit -m "${commitMsg.replace(/"/g, '\\"')}"`, { cwd, stdio: "pipe" });
        commitSha = execSync("git rev-parse HEAD", { cwd, stdio: "pipe" }).toString().trim();
      } catch (gitErr) {
        return {
          mode: "local",
          status: "SUCCESS",
          message: `Files written to disk, but git commit failed: ${gitErr.message}`
        };
      }
    }
    return {
      mode: "local",
      status: "SUCCESS",
      commitSha,
      message: `Successfully applied local patch (${modifiedPaths.length} file(s) modified).`
    };
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      mode: "local",
      status: "ERROR",
      message: err.message || "Unknown error occurred during local execution",
      errors: [err]
    };
  }
}

// packages/local-cli/src/cli.ts
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import * as path2 from "node:path";
function parseCliArgs(argv) {
  const args = argv.slice(2);
  let prompt = "";
  let repo = "local/repo";
  let branch = "main";
  const targetPaths = [];
  let model;
  let skipCommit = false;
  let help = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
    const arg = args[i];
    if (arg === "--help" || arg === "-h") {
      help = true;
    } else if (arg === "--prompt" || arg === "-p") {
      prompt = args[++i] || "";
    } else if (arg === "--repo" || arg === "-r") {
      repo = args[++i] || "local/repo";
    } else if (arg === "--branch" || arg === "-b") {
      branch = args[++i] || "main";
    } else if (arg === "--path" || arg === "--target" || arg === "-t") {
      const val = args[++i];
      if (val) targetPaths.push(val);
    } else if (arg === "--model" || arg === "-m") {
      model = args[++i];
    } else if (arg === "--skip-commit") {
      skipCommit = true;
    } else if (!arg.startsWith("-") && !prompt) {
      prompt = arg;
    }
  }
  return {
    input: {
      prompt,
      repo,
      branch,
      targetPaths: targetPaths.length > 0 ? targetPaths : void 0
    },
    model,
    skipCommit,
    help
  };
}
function printHelp() {
  console.log(`
Gemini Bridge Local CLI (Stage 5 Hybrid Routing)
Usage:
  gemini-local [options] [prompt]

Options:
  -p, --prompt <prompt>      Task instruction for Gemini
  -t, --path <filepath>      Target file(s) to include in context (repeatable)
  -r, --repo <owner/repo>    Repository name identifier (default: local/repo)
  -b, --branch <branch>      Branch name (default: main)
  -m, --model <model>        Gemini model (default: gemini-2.0-flash)
      --skip-commit          Apply changes to disk without creating a Git commit
  -h, --help                 Display this help message

Environment:
  GEMINI_API_KEY             Google Gemini API Key (or set in .env)
`);
}
async function runCli(argv = process.argv) {
  const { input, model, skipCommit, help } = parseCliArgs(argv);
  if (help || !input.prompt) {
    printHelp();
    return help ? 0 : 1;
  }
  console.log(`[gemini-local] Starting local task: "${input.prompt}"`);
  if (input.targetPaths && input.targetPaths.length > 0) {
    console.log(`[gemini-local] Target files: ${input.targetPaths.join(", ")}`);
  }
  const result = await runLocalHybridTask(input, { model, skipCommit });
  if (result.status === "SUCCESS") {
    console.log(`[gemini-local] Status: SUCCESS`);
    if (result.commitSha) {
      console.log(`[gemini-local] Commit SHA: ${result.commitSha}`);
    }
    if (result.message) {
      console.log(`[gemini-local] ${result.message}`);
    }
    return 0;
  } else if (result.status === "PREFLIGHT_FAILED") {
    console.error(`[gemini-local] Preflight validation failed!`);
    if (result.errors) {
      for (const err of result.errors) {
        console.error(`  - [${err.stage}] ${err.file}:${err.line || 1} - ${err.message}`);
      }
    }
    return 2;
  } else {
    console.error(`[gemini-local] Error: ${result.message}`);
    return 1;
  }
}
try {
  const currentFilePath = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
  const entryArg = process.argv[1] ? path2.resolve(process.argv[1]) : "";
  const isDirectRun = entryArg === currentFilePath || entryArg && entryArg.endsWith("gemini-local");
  if (isDirectRun) {
    runCli().then((code) => {
      if (code !== 0) process.exit(code);
    });
  }
} catch {
}
export {
  applyPatchesInMemory,
  extractJsonFromResponse,
  parseCliArgs,
  printHelp,
  runCli,
  runLocalHybridTask
};

import fs from 'fs';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const rootDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
const cliDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');

const req = createRequire(path.join(rootDir, 'cf-gemini-git-bridge/package.json'));
let esbuild;
try {
  esbuild = req('esbuild');
} catch (e) {
  esbuild = await import('esbuild');
}

async function build() {
  console.log('[Build CLI] Bundling packages/local-cli/src/cli.ts...');
  const entryPoint = path.join(cliDir, 'src/cli.ts');
  const distDir = path.join(cliDir, 'dist');
  const distTarget = path.join(distDir, 'cli.mjs');

  if (!fs.existsSync(distDir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(distDir, { recursive: true });
  }

  const result = await esbuild.build({
    entryPoints: [entryPoint],
    bundle: true,
    write: true,
    outfile: distTarget,
    format: 'esm',
    platform: 'node',
    target: 'node18',
    packages: 'external'
  });

  console.log(`[Build CLI] Wrote: ${distTarget}`);
  console.log('✅ [Build CLI] Local CLI successfully built.');
}

build().catch((err) => {
  console.error('[Build CLI] Build failed:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});

const USERSCRIPT_BANNER = `// ==UserScript==
// @name         Gemini Git Bridge & Shadow-DOM HUD
// @namespace    https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev/
// @version      2.0.0
// @description  Commit code & search/replace patches directly from Google Gemini & AI Studio to GitHub via Cloudflare Worker (Shadow-DOM HUD)
// @author       Antigravity

// @match        https://aistudio.google.com/*

// @grant        GM_setValue
// @grant        GM_getValue

// @connect      *.workers.dev
// @run-at       document-idle
// ==/UserScript==

// --- Trusted Types (TrustedHTML) compatibility for Google Gemini & AI Studio ---
if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.trustedTypes && typeof window.trustedTypes.createPolicy === 'function') {
  try {
    window.trustedTypes.createPolicy('default', {
      createHTML: (string) => string,
      createScript: (string) => string,
      createScriptURL: (string) => string,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    try {
      window.trustedTypes.createPolicy('geminiBridgePolicy', {
        createHTML: (string) => string,
      });
    } catch (e2) {
      // Ignored if policy already exists
    }
  }
}
`;

async function build() {
  console.log('[Build Userscript] Bundling packages/userscript-hud/src/index.ts...');
  const entryPoint = path.join(hudDir, 'src/index.ts');
  const distDir = path.join(hudDir, 'dist');
  const distTarget = path.join(distDir, 'gemini-bridge.user.js');
  const rootTarget = path.join(rootDir, 'gemini-bridge.user.js');

  if (!fs.existsSync(distDir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(distDir, { recursive: true });
  }

  const result = await esbuild.build({
    entryPoints: [entryPoint],
    bundle: true,
    write: false,
    format: 'iife',
    target: 'es2022',
    minify: false,
    banner: {
      js: USERSCRIPT_BANNER,
    },
  });

  if (result.errors.length > 0) {
    console.error('[Build Userscript] Build failed with errors:', result.errors);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const bundledCode = result.outputFiles[0].text;

  fs.writeFileSync(distTarget, bundledCode, 'utf8');
  console.log(`[Build Userscript] Wrote: ${distTarget} (${(bundledCode.length / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB)`);

  fs.writeFileSync(rootTarget, bundledCode, 'utf8');
  console.log(`[Build Userscript] Synced to root: ${rootTarget}`);
  console.log('✅ [Build Userscript] Userscript successfully built.');
}

build().catch((err) => {
  console.error('[Build Userscript] Fatal error:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});

File: packages/userscript-hud/src/services/selfHealing.ts

import type {
  LinterError,
  WorkerPatchResponse,
  HudConfig,
  HudState
} from '../types';
import { injectPromptText } from '../dom/injector';

/**
 * Maximum number of automated self-healing turns before stopping and requiring manual review.
 */
export const MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES = 3;

/**
 * Builds a structured #solve self-healing prompt targeting all collected preflight & syntax errors.
 */
export function buildSelfHealingPrompt(
  errors: LinterError[],
  repo: string,
  branch: string,
  headOid: string,
  retryAttempt: number
): string {
  const formattedErrors = errors
    .map((err, idx) => {
      const loc = err.line ? (err.column ? `:${err.line}:${err.column}` : `:${err.line}`) : '';
      const snippet = err.codeSnippet ? `\n   > ${err.codeSnippet}` : '';
      return `${idx + 1}. [Stage: ${err.stage}] ${err.file}${loc} — ${err.message}${snippet}`;
    })
    .join('\n');

  return `#solve Linter & Preflight Self-Healing Loop (Retry ${retryAttempt}/${MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES})

The previous patch for repository "${repo}" on branch "${branch}" was rejected during Edge-Worker preflight validation.
Expected HEAD commit: "${headOid}".

Aggregated Preflight Errors (${errors.length}):
${formattedErrors}

Fix Instructions:
1. Fix ALL listed syntax and rule errors in a single cohesive response.
2. Hard Guardrail: NEVER use placeholder comments like "// ... existing code ...", "/* remaining code */", or "# ... existing code ...". Always provide complete search/replace blocks or full file content.
3. Provide your correction as a single valid JSON codeblock matching the GeminiPatchPayload schema:
\`\`\`json
{
  "commitMessage": "fix: resolve preflight linter errors (retry ${retryAttempt})",
  "patches": [
    {
      "path": "path/to/file.ext",
      "action": "modify",
      "blocks": [
        {
          "search": "exact string to replace",
          "replace": "replacement string"
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
\`\`\`
`;
}

/**
 * Evaluates whether the self-healing auto-retry loop should trigger.
 */
export function shouldTriggerSelfHealing(
  response: WorkerPatchResponse,
  autoPilot: boolean,
  currentRetryCount: number
): { trigger: boolean; nextRetryCount: number; reason: string } {
  if (response.status === 'COMMITTED') {
    return {
      trigger: false,
      nextRetryCount: 0,
      reason: 'COMMITTED_SUCCESS'
    };
  }

  if (response.status !== 'PREFLIGHT_FAILED') {
    return {
      trigger: false,
      nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount,
      reason: 'STATUS_NOT_PREFLIGHT_FAILED'
    };
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(response.errors) || response.errors.length === 0) {
    return {
      trigger: false,
      nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount,
      reason: 'NO_ERRORS_REPORTED'
    };
  }

  if (currentRetryCount >= MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES) {
    return {
      trigger: false,
      nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount,
      reason: 'MAX_RETRIES_EXCEEDED'
    };
  }

  if (!autoPilot) {
    return {
      trigger: false,
      nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount,
      reason: 'AUTOPILOT_DISABLED'
    };
  }

  return {
    trigger: true,
    nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount + 1,
    reason: 'PREFLIGHT_FAILED_AUTO_RETRY'
  };
}

/**
 * Executes a single turn of the automated self-healing loop:
 * checks conditions, constructs the structured prompt, and injects it into the LLM chat.
 */
export function executeSelfHealingTurn(
  response: WorkerPatchResponse,
  cfg: HudConfig,
  state: HudState,
  autoSubmit = true
): { triggered: boolean; nextRetryCount: number; prompt?: string; reason: string } {
  const evalResult = shouldTriggerSelfHealing(response, cfg.autoPilot, state.retryCount);

  if (!evalResult.trigger) {
    return {
      triggered: false,
      nextRetryCount: evalResult.nextRetryCount,
      reason: evalResult.reason
    };
  }

  const prompt = buildSelfHealingPrompt(
    response.errors || [],
    cfg.repo,
    cfg.branch,
    state.headOid,
    evalResult.nextRetryCount
  );

  injectPromptText(prompt, autoSubmit);

  return {
    triggered: true,
    nextRetryCount: evalResult.nextRetryCount,
    prompt,
    reason: evalResult.reason
  };
}

import { mountShadowHud, ShadowHudController } from './ui/shadowHud';
import { startDomHarvester } from './dom/harvester';
import { loadHudConfig } from './services/storage';

export * from './types';
export * from './services/storage';
export * from './services/workerClient';
export * from './services/selfHealing';
export * from './dom/injector';
export * from './dom/harvester';
export * from './ui/shadowHud';

let controllerInstance: ShadowHudController | null = null;

export function initGeminiEdgeHud(): ShadowHudController | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof document === 'undefined') {
    return null;
  }

  // Prevent duplicate initialization
  if (controllerInstance) {
    return controllerInstance;
  }

  // Only run in the top window, ignore Google sub-iframes (accounts, ogs)
  if (window.top !== window.self) {
    return null;
  }

  const cfg = loadHudConfig();
  console.log('[Gemini Edge HUD] Initializing with config:', {
    workerUrl: cfg.workerUrl,
    repo: cfg.repo,
    branch: cfg.branch,
  });

  controllerInstance = mountShadowHud();

  startDomHarvester(
    (patch) => {
      controllerInstance?.addHarvestedPatch(patch);
    },
    (patch) => {
      controllerInstance?.handleApplyPatch(patch);
    }
  );

  console.log('⚡ [Gemini Edge HUD] Successfully mounted in Shadow DOM.');
  return controllerInstance;
}

// Auto-run if in browser environment
if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof document !== 'undefined') {
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => initGeminiEdgeHud());
  } else {
    initGeminiEdgeHud();
  }
}

# Projekt-Review & Vollständige Dokumentation des Zusammenspiels (`01_PROJECT_REVIEW_AND_HANDOFF.md`)

> **Verwendungszweck:** Autoritative Gesamtdokumentation des Zusammenspiels aller 3 Schichten und 18 TypeScript-Module sowie strukturierte Checkliste für das vorab geplante Code- & Security-Review (`#review`).

---

## 1. Vollständiges Zusammenspiel aller Einzelteile & Subroutinen (End-to-End Architektur)

### A. Sequenzdiagramm des Gesamtsystems (Mix -> Paste -> Harvest -> Patch -> Lint -> Self-Heal / Commit)

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant User as Entwickler (Browser)
    participant HUD as Shadow-DOM HUD (shadowHud.ts + hudStylesAndDom.ts)
    participant Inj as DOM-Injector & Harvester (injector.ts / harvester.ts)
    participant LLM as Gemini / AI Studio DOM
    participant WClient as Worker-Client (workerClient.ts via GM_xmlhttpRequest)
    participant Router as Edge-Worker Router (index.ts + verifyAuth)
    participant Mixer as Untertool A: Mixer (mixer.ts)
    participant Patcher as Untertool B: Patcher (patcher.ts + patchInMemory.ts)
    participant Linter as Untertool C: Linter (linter.ts + syntaxScanner.ts)
    participant GH as GitHub API (REST Tarball & GraphQL v4 via github.ts)

    User->>HUD: Klick auf "Load Repo Mix (/api/mix)"
    HUD->>WClient: requestRepoMix(cfg) [Bearer WORKER_SECRET]
    WClient->>Router: POST /api/mix (MixRequest)
    Router->>Mixer: handleMixRequest(body, githubToken)
    Mixer->>GH: GET /repos/{owner}/{repo}/tarball/{branch} (1 Subrequest)
    GH-->>Mixer: Gzip Tarball Stream -> DecompressionStream('gzip')
    Note over Mixer: Extrahiert 40-char headOid (PAX 'g' / getBranchHeadOid)<br/>Überspringt Ignorierlisten & >512KB am 512B-Header (shouldSkipTarEntry)<br/>Baut XML mit escapeCdata
    Mixer-->>HUD: MixResponse { status: "OK", headOid (40-char), xmlContent, stats }
    HUD->>Inj: injectRepoMixFile(xmlContent, "repo-mix.xml")
    Inj->>LLM: Synthetisches ClipboardEvent('paste') mit File("repo-mix.xml")
    User->>LLM: Sendet Aufgabe / #solve Prompt
    LLM-->>Inj: Generiert Antwort mit JSON-Patchblock oder SEARCH/REPLACE
    Inj->>HUD: MutationObserver (harvester.ts + enhanceCanvasCards) meldet HarvestedPatch
    User->>HUD: Klick auf "Apply Patch" (oder automatisch via Auto-Pilot)
    HUD->>WClient: requestWorkerPatch({ repo, branch, expectedHeadOid, payload })
    WClient->>Router: POST /api/patch
    Router->>Patcher: handlePatchRequest(body, githubToken)
    Patcher->>GH: getBranchHeadOid() -> Prüft expectedHeadOid (sonst GIT_CONFLICT)
    Patcher->>GH: fetchMultipleFilesViaGraphQL() (1 Subrequest Batch-Read via Aliases)
    Patcher->>Patcher: applyPatchesInMemory() (3-Stufen applySearchReplace + checkPlaceholderErrors)
    Patcher->>Linter: runPreflightLinter(modifiedFiles, payload.preflight)
    Linter->>Linter: validateSyntaxInWorker() [syntaxScanner.ts] + runCustomPreflightRules()
    alt 0 Fehler in errors: LinterError[]
        Patcher->>GH: createCommitOnBranch(expectedHeadOid, additions, deletions)
        GH-->>Patcher: { oid: newHeadOid, url: commitUrl }
        Patcher-->>HUD: { status: "COMMITTED", newHeadOid, commitUrl }
        Note over HUD: Aktualisiert headOid = newHeadOid & setzt retryCount = 0
    else >= 1 Fehler in errors: LinterError[] (Kein Commit!)
        Patcher-->>HUD: { status: "PREFLIGHT_FAILED", errors: LinterError[] }
        Note over HUD: selfHealing.ts prüft autoPilot && retryCount < 3
        HUD->>Inj: executeSelfHealingTurn() -> injectPromptText(buildSelfHealingPrompt)
        Inj->>LLM: Sendet aggregierten #solve Korrektur-Prompt (Retry 1..3)
    end
```

Wenn du im neuen Chat **Prompt B** aus [plans/00_STARTCHAT_ORCHESTRATOR.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/plans/00_STARTCHAT_ORCHESTRATOR.md) ausführst, werden folgende Punkte systematisch geprüft:

# Live-Deployment & Browser-E2E-Verifikationsplan (`02_LIVE_DEPLOYMENT_AND_E2E_PLAN.md`)

> **Status:** Als ausführungsfertiger Plan gespeichert (`PLAN_DONE`). Wird nach erfolgreichem Abschluss des 3-Punkte-Reviews ([plans/01_PROJECT_REVIEW_AND_HANDOFF.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/plans/01_PROJECT_REVIEW_AND_HANDOFF.md)) und expliziter User-Freigabe für den Hard-Stop-Befehl `npx wrangler deploy` ausgeführt.

## Execution Metadata
- **Recommended Execution Model:** FLASH (`Model: 'flash'` für die deterministischen Build-/Deploy-/Smoke-Einzelbefehle mit `Complexity: 4/10 < 6`, gesteuert durch den Master-Orchestrator auf **Argon Medium**).
- **Complexity Assessment:** Isolierte Deployment- und Live-Endpunkt-Verifikationsschritte (`GET /health`, `POST /api/mix`, `POST /api/patch` Preflight/Idempotenz-Dry-Run) auf dem bereits vollständig getesteten Stand (`10/10 PASS`).

---

### 1. Architektur & Logik (5-Schritte Live-Rollout & E2E-Test)

1. **Schritt 1 – Pre-Deploy Gate & Userscript-Bundle-Check (Quiet Zone):**
   - Einzelne Ausführung von `node scripts/patches_and_tests/worker-smoke.mjs` (`10/10 PASS` Pflicht) und Prüfung von `git status --short`.
2. **Schritt 2 – Cloudflare Worker V2 Live-Deployment (Hard Stop – erfordert explizite User-Freigabe):**
   - Ausführung von `npx wrangler deploy` im Arbeitsverzeichnis `packages/edge-worker`.
   - Da `packages/edge-worker/wrangler.jsonc` den identischen Worker-Namen `"name": "gemini-git-bridge"` trägt, bleiben die auf Cloudflare gesetzten Secrets (`GITHUB_TOKEN` / `GITHUB_PAT` und `BRIDGE_SECRET` / `WORKER_SECRET`) nahtlos erhalten.
3. **Schritt 3 – Automatisierter Live-V2-Endpunkt-Test (`worker-smoke.mjs` Live-Probes):**
   - Prüfung von `GET https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev/health` (erwartet `version: "2.0.0"`, `hasBridgeSecret: true`, `hasGithubToken: true`).
   - Lesender Live-Test von `POST https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev/api/mix` (`persistToGitHub: false`) gegen das Ziel-Repository: verifiziert, dass der 1-Subrequest Tarball-Stream live eine 40-stellige `headOid`, valides `<repository>`-XML und `stats` liefert.
   - Nicht-destruktiver Live-Test von `POST https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev/api/patch`:
     - Test A (Pre-Flight Linter Guardrail Live): Sendet einen absichtlich fehlerhaften Payload im Dry-Run (mit `// ... existing code ...` Platzhalter) und verifiziert, dass der Live-Worker ohne Commit sofort `status: "PREFLIGHT_FAILED"` mit `errors: LinterError[]` zurückgibt.
4. **Schritt 4 – Tampermonkey Userscript Installation & Browser-HUD-Test:**
   - Übernahme der Root-Datei [gemini-bridge.user.js](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/gemini-bridge.user.js) in Tampermonkey.
   - Öffnen von `https://gemini.google.com` bzw. `https://aistudio.google.com`:
     - Prüfung der Shadow-DOM-Pill (`⚡ Gemini Edge HUD`, `#gemini-edge-hud-root`).
     - Klick auf `📦 Load Repo Mix (/api/mix)` -> verifiziert das automatische Einfügen von `repo-mix.xml` in das Prompt-Feld und die Anzeige der 40-stelligen `headOid`.
5. **Schritt 5 – Abschluss-Dokumentation & Optionaler Git-Push:**
   - Protokollierung der Live-Testergebnisse in [plans/TEST_LOG.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/plans/TEST_LOG.md), [plans/EXECUTION_LOG.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/plans/EXECUTION_LOG.md) und [plans/STATE.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/plans/STATE.md).
   - Lokaler Git-Commit des finalen Testprotokolls (und optional `git push` nach expliziter User-Freigabe).

### 3. Risiko- & Komplexitäts-Check

| **2. Live `/api/mix` & `/api/patch` Dry-Run** | **`4 / 10`** | Rein lesender `/api/mix`-Test (`persistToGitHub: false`) und absichtlicher `PREFLIGHT_FAILED`-Dry-Run erzeugen **keine** Commits auf GitHub. | **Flash (`Model: 'flash'`)** |
| **Gesamtausführung Deployment-Plan** | **`4 / 10`** | Unterhalb `Complexity < 6` -> vollständig über `/flash` Single-Action Protocol ausführbar. | **Flash (`Model: 'flash'`)** |

- Da die Gesamt-Komplexität bei **`4/10` (`< 6`)** liegt, können die Schritte 1 bis 3 nach deiner Freigabe für `npx wrangler deploy` in einem einzigen delegierten **Flash-Subagenten-Lauf (`Model: 'flash'`)** strikt nach dem Single-Action Protocol ausgeführt werden.

- **Commit Format:** `chore(release): verify live v2 edge worker deployment and e2e probes`.
- **Remote Safety:** `npx wrangler deploy` und `git push` erfolgen ausschließlich auf explizite Anweisung des Users.

================
File: plans/03_HYBRID_ROUTING_PLAN.md

# HYBRID ROUTING BLUEPRINT: STAGE 5 (`cloudflare_orchestrator_Bridge`)

## Mandatory Execution Metadata Header
- **Stage:** 5 (Hybrid Routing: Local CLI vs. Cloudflare Worker GitHub Webhook)
- **Target Components:**
  - `shared/hybridTypes.ts` (Schnittstellen-Verträge für hybride Workflows)
  - `packages/local-cli/` (Pfad A: Node.js CLI mit `@google/genai`, `simple-git`, `fs` & In-Memory Preflight)
  - `packages/edge-worker/src/modules/webhookHandler.ts` & `src/index.ts` (Pfad B: Webhook-Route `POST /api/webhook/github` mit HMAC-SHA256 & `ctx.waitUntil`)
- **Overall Complexity:** 5/10 (Klare Trennung durch Disjunkte Module, nutzt bestehende `patchInMemory.ts` & `linter.ts`)
- **Delegation Target:** Artifact Executor Subagent
- **P1 Invariant Compliance:**
  1. Zero Node.js imports (`fs`, `child_process`, `simple-git`) in `packages/edge-worker/`.
  2. Pure Web Crypto (`HMAC-SHA256`) für GitHub Webhook Signatur-Prüfung (`x-hub-signature-256`).
  3. GitHub Webhook 10s-Timeout Schutz: Sofort `202 Accepted`, Gemini & GraphQL-Commit im Hintergrund via `ctx.waitUntil`.
  4. P1 Preflight Hard Guardrail: Jeder Pfad (Lokal & Worker) validiert Änderungen über `applyPatchesInMemory` + `runPreflightLinter` vor dem Festplatten-/Git-Write.
  5. File-Size Modularity: Alle neuen Dateien strikt `< 500` Zeilen.

---

## 1. Context & Data Flow

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    Trigger["Trigger: Dev CLI oder GitHub Event"] --> Router["Hybrid Router (shared/hybridTypes.ts)"]
    
    Router -->|"EXECUTION_MODE=local"| LocalRunner["packages/local-cli/src/localRunner.ts<br/>(Google Gen AI SDK + fs + simple-git)"]
    Router -->|"EXECUTION_MODE=worker"| WorkerWebhook["packages/edge-worker/src/modules/webhookHandler.ts<br/>(HMAC-SHA256 + ctx.waitUntil)"]

    LocalRunner --> CoreLinter["packages/edge-worker/src/modules/patchInMemory.ts<br/>+ linter.ts (Non-Fail-Fast AST / Custom Rules)"]
    WorkerWebhook --> CoreLinter

    CoreLinter -->|"Lokal: 0 Errors"| LocalFS["Write to Disk & Git Commit"]
    CoreLinter -->|"Worker: 0 Errors"| CloudCommit["createCommitOnBranch (GraphQL)<br/>+ GitHub Issue/PR Comment"]
```

---

## 2. Implementation Steps

### Step 1: Verträge & Typen (`shared/hybridTypes.ts`)
- Definiere `ExecutionMode = 'local' | 'worker'`, `HybridTaskInput`, `HybridTaskResult` und `HybridWorkerEnv`.
- Re-exportiere diese in `shared/types.ts`.

### Step 2: Pfad A – Lokales CLI-Paket (`packages/local-cli/`)
- Erstelle `packages/local-cli/package.json` und `tsconfig.json`.
- Implementiere `packages/local-cli/src/localRunner.ts`:
  - Liest `.env` (`GEMINI_API_KEY`, optional `GITHUB_PAT`).
  - Sammelt lokalen Datei-Kontext der angegebenen Pfade.
  - Fragt Gemini API via `@google/genai` nach `GeminiPatchPayload` JSON.
  - Wendet `applyPatchesInMemory` und `runPreflightLinter` an.
  - Erst bei 0 Fehlern: Schreibt auf Disk und committet lokal via `simple-git`.
- Erstelle CLI-Entrypoint `packages/local-cli/src/cli.ts` (ausführbar mit `node`).

### Step 3: Pfad B – Edge-Worker GitHub Webhook (`packages/edge-worker/`)
- Erstelle `packages/edge-worker/src/modules/webhookHandler.ts`:
  - `verifyGithubWebhookSignature(rawBody, signatureHeader, secret)` per Web Crypto `crypto.subtle.verify`.
  - Filtert auf `@gemini`-Erwähnungen in Issues / PR-Kommentaren.
  - Führt asynchron via `ctx.waitUntil`: Repo-Mix (`handleMixRequest`) -> Gemini REST Call -> Patcher (`handlePatchRequest`) -> GitHub Kommentar-Feedback aus.
- Verdrahte Route `POST /api/webhook/github` in `packages/edge-worker/src/index.ts` **vor** der Standard-`verifyAuth` (da Webhooks über HMAC signiert sind).

### Step 4: Smoke-Test-Erweiterung & Verifikation
- Erweitere `scripts/patches_and_tests/worker-smoke.mjs` um Probe 11:
  - Prüft HMAC-Signatur-Validierung im Webhook-Handler.
  - Prüft, dass `packages/edge-worker/` keine Node-Builtins importiert.
- Führe TypeScript-Checks (`tsc --noEmit`) und Smoke-Suite (`worker-smoke.mjs`) aus.
- Protokolliere den Fortschritt in `plans/EXECUTION_LOG.md`, `plans/TEST_LOG.md` und `plans/STATE.md`.

# 04_BOILERPLATE_HARMONIZATION_EXECUTION_PLAN.md — Exekutionsplan für Verfassungs- & Regelwerks-Harmonisierung

> **Execution Metadata:**
> - **Execution Mode:** Optimiert für `#flash` / `#exec-wave`
> - **Gesamt-Komplexität:** `4/10` (Dokumentations-, Regelwerks- und Repo-Hygiene-Harmonisierung; kein Breaking Code Change)
> - **Single-Action Protocol:** Einzelbefehle in PowerShell ohne `&&`/`;`-Verkettung
> - **Dateigrößen-Gesetz:** Alle Dateien bleiben strikt `< 500` Zeilen (Probe 10 Enforced)
> - **Referenzen:** [AGENTS.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/AGENTS.md), [plans/STATE.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/plans/STATE.md), [plans/04_BOILERPLATE_HARMONIZATION_WAVE_PLAN.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/plans/04_BOILERPLATE_HARMONIZATION_WAVE_PLAN.md)

## 1. Übersicht & Zielsetzung

Dieser Exekutionsplan harmonisiert die Verfassung (`AGENTS.md`), die Regelwerke in `.agents/rules/` und die Repo-Hygiene in `.agents/skills/` mit dem aktuellen Codebestand nach den Meilensteinen Stage 5 (Hybrides Routing), Stage 6–6.2 (RepoMix Atomic Chunking & `.txt`-Migration) und Stage 6.3 (Userscript "Apply All" FIFO Fail-Fast Queue).

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    subgraph Phase 1 [Phase 1: Repo-Hygiene]

    end

        B2A["Batch 02A (FLASH)<br/>multi-ide-setup.md & orchestrator-protocol.md"]
        B2B["Batch 02B (FLASH)<br/>userscript-shadow-hud.md & edge-worker-architecture.md"]

    subgraph Phase 3 [Phase 3: Verfassungs-Konsolidierung]
        B3["Batch 03 (PRO)<br/>AGENTS.md Single Source of Truth Update"]
    end

    subgraph Phase 4 [Phase 4: End-to-End Verifikation]
        B4["Batch 04 (FLASH)<br/>TypeScript Checks, Smoke Tests & Checkpoint Commit"]
    end

    Phase 1 --> Phase 2
    B2A --> Phase 3
    B2B --> Phase 3
    Phase 3 --> Phase 4
```

### Phase 2: Parallele Regelwerks-Synchronisation (Parallel Wave)

#### Batch 02A: Multi-IDE-Setup & Orchestrator-Protokoll
- **Complexity:** `4/10` | **Modell-Tier:** `FLASH` (`flash`)
- **Ziel:** Erstellung der fehlenden Datei `.agents/rules/multi-ide-setup.md` zur Schließung des toten Links in `exec-wave/SKILL.md` und Anpassung von `.agents/rules/orchestrator-protocol.md` an die 3-Tier-Modellmatrix.

- **Schritt 1: Erstellung `.agents/rules/multi-ide-setup.md`**
  - **Pfad:** `.agents/rules/multi-ide-setup.md`
  - **Inhalt & Struktur:**
    ```markdown
    ---
    trigger: model_decision
    description: "Apply this rule when configuring or executing tasks across different IDE environments (Antigravity, Kimi Code, Cursor, Cline)."
    ---

    # Multi-IDE Setup & Execution Adapters

    Dieses Regelwerk definiert die standardisierten Betriebsmodi zur Ausführung von `#exec-wave`, `#delegate` und Subagenten in verschiedenen Entwicklungs- und IDE-Umgebungen.

    ---

    ## 1. Die drei Betriebsmodi (Mode A, Mode B, Mode C)

    ### Mode A: Native Multi-Model Subagents (z. B. Google Antigravity)
    - **Architektur:** Vollwertige Unterstützung für `invoke_subagent` mit variabler Modell-Zuweisung (`flash_lite`, `flash`, `pro`).
    - **Scatter-Gather:** Parallele Waves laufen echt nebenläufig über disjunkte Dateisätze.
    - **Live-Streaming:** Der Coordinator erzeugt sofort nach Subagent-Start anklickbare Deep-Links im Format:
      `[⚡ Watch Stream](conversation://<subagent-conversation-id>)`.
    - **3-Point Feedback Contract:** Jeder Subagent berichtet beim Abschluss strictly:
      1. `Status: [SUCCESS | BLOCKED | FAILED]`
      2. `Files Modified: [Liste relativer Pfade]`
      3. `Summary: [1-2 Sätze Kernänderungen]`

    ### Mode B: Fixed-Model oder Rate-Limited Subagents (z. B. Kimi Code / CLI Agents)
    - **Architektur:** Subagenten laufen mit `Model: 'inherit'`, um die Kontext-Isolation zu wahren.
    - **Pacing & Concurrency:** Bei strikten Rate-Limits oder fehlendem Multithreading werden parallele Batches als sequenzielle FIFO-Warteschlange abgearbeitet.
    - **Fallback-Verlinkung:** Kann die IDE keine `conversation://`-Links auflösen, gibt der Coordinator den Pfad zum Transcript an:
      `file:///<appDataDir>/brain/<conversation-id>/.system_generated/logs/transcript.jsonl`.

    ### Mode C: Single-Agent IDEs ohne Subagent-Engine (z. B. Cursor Composer / Cline)
    - **Architektur:** Ein einziger Agent führt alle Batches nacheinander in separaten Kontext-Hygiene-Durchgängen aus.
    - **Kontext-Disziplin:** Lade jeweils nur die im aktuellen Batch spezifizierten Zieldateien.
    - **Single-Action Protocol:** Strikt ein Terminal-Befehl pro Schritt, keine Verkettung mit `&&` oder `;`.
    - **Modell-Switch-Hinweis:** Vor dem Start von `Tier: PRO`-Batches (Komplexität >= 7) empfiehlt der Agent einen Modellwechsel auf ein Pro-Modell oder führt einen expliziten Self-Review-Pass durch.

    ---

    ## 2. Invarianten & Qualitäts-Gates
    1. **Disjunkte Datei-Inventare:** Parallele Subagenten dürfen niemals dieselbe Datei editieren.
    2. **Shell-Verbot für Subagenten:** Parallele Subagenten führen keine Terminal-Befehle (`git`, `npm`, `tsc`) aus. Alle Verifikationen und Commits erfolgen durch den Coordinator.
    3. **Dateigrößen-Gesetz:** Alle erzeugten und modifizierten Dateien bleiben strikt `< 500` Zeilen.
    ```

- **Schritt 2: Update `.agents/rules/orchestrator-protocol.md`**
  - **Pfad:** `.agents/rules/orchestrator-protocol.md`
  - **Änderungen:**
    1. Angleichung von Section 1 & Section 3 an die 3-Tier-Matrix aus `exec-wave/SKILL.md`:
       - Tier 1: `flash_lite` (Komplexität 1–3: Trivial/Mechanisch, Types, Cleanups)
       - Tier 2: `flash` (Komplexität 4–6: Standard-Implementierungen, Scoped Features, Unit-Tests)
       - Tier 3: `pro` / `inherit` (Komplexität 7–10: Architektur, Cross-Package State, Pro Decomposition)
    2. Integration der Pflicht zur Erzeugung von klickbaren Stream-Links `[⚡ Watch Stream](conversation://<subagent-conversation-id>)`.
    3. Verweis auf `.agents/rules/multi-ide-setup.md` für die Multi-IDE-Adaption.

- **Verifikationsbefehle:**
  ```powershell
  Test-Path ".agents/rules/multi-ide-setup.md"
  Get-Content ".agents/rules/orchestrator-protocol.md" | Measure-Object -Line
  ```
- **Akzeptanzkriterien:**
  - [ ] `.agents/rules/multi-ide-setup.md` existiert und ist `< 500` Zeilen.
  - [ ] `.agents/rules/orchestrator-protocol.md` enthält die 3-Tier-Matrix und bleibt `< 500` Zeilen.

      - Flex-Header über der Harvested-Patches-Liste mit `⚡ Apply All (${patchCount})` (`hud-btn-primary`) und `🗑️ Clear` (`hud-btn-secondary`).
      - Sequenzielle FIFO-Abarbeitung (`[...state.harvestedPatches].reverse()`) mit direktem Fail-Fast bei Fehler (`PREFLIGHT_FAILED`, `GIT_CONFLICT`, `ERROR`).
      - Automatisches Pruning erfolgreich committeter oder idempotenter Patches (`COMMITTED`, `NO_CHANGES_NEEDED`) aus `state.harvestedPatches`.

# 04_BOILERPLATE_HARMONIZATION_WAVE_PLAN.md — Roadmap zur Verfassungs- & Regelwerks-Synchronisation

> **Execution Metadata:**
> - **Typ:** Multi-Batch Roadmap für `#exec-wave`
> - **Gesamt-Komplexität:** `4/10` (Dokumentations-, Regelwerks- und Repo-Hygiene-Harmonisierung; kein Code-Umbau, aber strikte Konsistenz)
> - **Modell-Tier-Mix:** `FLASH_LITE` (Batch 1), `FLASH` (Batches 2A, 2B, 4), `PRO` (Batch 3)
> - **Single-Action Protocol:** Einzelbefehle ohne `&&`/`;`-Verkettung
> - **Dateigrößen-Gesetz:** Alle Zieldateien bleiben strikt `< 500` Zeilen

---

## 1. Ausgangslage & Befund (Delta-Analyse)

In den letzten Tagen wurde die Codebase tiefgreifend und erfolgreich umgebaut:
- **Monorepo-Struktur:** 3 Pakete (`packages/edge-worker`, `packages/userscript-hud`, `packages/local-cli`) + `shared/`.
- **RepoMix Chunking & .txt Migration (Stage 6–6.2):** `mixChunker.ts`, `tarUtils.ts`, 85k-Char-Budget, 100k Hard Cutoff, max 10 Parts, dynamische `.repomixignore.cf`-Auswertung und MIME-Typ `text/plain` (`.txt` statt `.xml`).
- **Userscript Queue Management (Stage 6.3):** FIFO Fail-Fast "⚡ Apply All", "🗑️ Clear Queue", dynamische Badges und Concurrency-Schutz.
- **Skills-Modernisierung:** Umstellung von flachen `.agents/skills/*.md`-Dateien auf Skill-Ordner mit `SKILL.md` sowie neue Skills (`bundle`, `repomix`, `deep-refactor`, `zip-clean`, `fetch-skill`).

### Identifizierte Gaps & Inkonsistenzen:
1. **`AGENTS.md`:**
   - Invariante 3.1 ignoriert `packages/local-cli/` und `shared/`.
   - Datenfluss 1 (Mix-Flow) beschreibt noch das alte Single-File `.xml`-Pasting statt Multi-Part `.txt`.
   - Datenfluss 2 (Patch-Flow) ignoriert die Stage 6.3 Queue ("Apply All", FIFO Fail-Fast).
   - P1-Matrix führt noch die veraltete 2-Stufen-Modellpolitik (Argon vs. Flash) und unvollständige Modulverweise.
2. **`.agents/rules/`:**
   - `userscript-shadow-hud.md`: Fehlt die Dokumentation von Stage 6.3 (Queue Header, FIFO Fail-Fast, Auto-Pruning) und Multi-Part `.txt`-Injection.
   - `edge-worker-architecture.md`: Bezieht sich noch auf XML-Ausgabe statt der Stage 6.2 `.txt`-Migration.
   - `orchestrator-protocol.md`: Verwendet noch das binäre Modell-Routing statt der in `exec-wave` etablierten 3-Tier-Matrix (`flash_lite`, `flash`, `pro`).
   - **Toter Link:** `exec-wave/SKILL.md` verweist auf `.agents/rules/multi-ide-setup.md`, die Datei existiert jedoch noch nicht!
3. **Skill-Hygiene:**
   - 7 verwaiste Flat-Files (`ask.md`, `delegate.md`, `exec.md`, `flash.md`, `grillme.md`, `refactor.md`, `review.md`) liegen ungetrackt neben ihren neuen Verzeichnis-Pendants `*.agents/skills/*/SKILL.md`.

#### Batch 02B: Edge-Worker- & Userscript-Regelwerk
- **Complexity:** `4/10` | **Tier:** `FLASH` (`flash`)
- **Dateien:**
  - Update: `.agents/rules/edge-worker-architecture.md` (Ergänzung der Stage 6.2 `.txt`-Migration [MIME `text/plain`], 10-Parts-Guardrail und dynamische `.repomixignore.cf`-Auswertung)
  - Update: `.agents/rules/userscript-shadow-hud.md` (Ergänzung von Stage 6.3 Queue-Controls ["⚡ Apply All" FIFO Fail-Fast, "🗑️ Clear Queue", State-Pruning] und Multi-Part `.txt`-Pasting)
- **Akzeptanzkriterium:** Beide Architektur-Regeln spiegeln 100 % der im Code aktiven Features wider; beide Dateien bleiben `< 500` Zeilen.

#### Batch 03: `AGENTS.md` Single Source of Truth Harmonisierung
- **Complexity:** `6/10` | **Tier:** `PRO` (`pro` / Master-Orchestrator)
- **Dateien:** `AGENTS.md`

     - Mix-Flow: Multi-Part `.txt`-Chunking (`part-01.txt` etc.), 85k-Target, 100k-Cutoff, dynamic `.repomixignore.cf`, Multi-File-Paste via `injectRepoMixParts`.

## STUFE 1: Cloudflare Worker – Hauptorchestrator & Repo-Mixer (`#delegate #flash`)

- **Voraussetzung:** Stufe 0.5b & 0.6 abgeschlossen (`packages/edge-worker/src/utils/github.ts`, `shared/types.ts` und `.agents/rules/edge-worker-architecture.md` liegen bereit).
- **Pflicht-Lektüre vorab:** `agents.md` (Root) und `.agents/rules/edge-worker-architecture.md`.
- **Aufgabe:**
  1. Implementiere `packages/edge-worker/src/modules/mixer.ts`:
     - 1-Subrequest Tarball-Stream (`GET https://api.github.com/repos/{owner}/{repo}/tarball/{branch}`).
     - Extraktion der 40-stelligen `headOid` primär aus dem **PAX Global Extended Header** (`typeflag === 'g'`, `comment=<40-char-sha>`), Root-Ordnername als Fallback.
     - 512-Byte-TAR-Stream-Parser mit USTAR `prefix` und PAX `path=...` Unterstützung.
     - Header-Level RAM-Filter gemäß `.agents/rules/edge-worker-architecture.md` (überspringt `.git/`, `node_modules/`, `dist/`, `build/`, `_v1_backup/`, Lockfiles, Binärdateien und Dateien > 512 KB direkt im Stream ohne String-Allokation).
     - XML-Generierung (`<repository><file_tree>...<files><file path="..."><![CDATA[...]]></file></files></repository>`) mit sicherem `]]>`-Escaping und Token-Schätzung.
     - Optionale Persistierung via `commitSingleFileViaGraphQL` aus `src/utils/github.ts`.
  2. Verbinde in `packages/edge-worker/src/index.ts` die Route `POST /api/mix`, den Health-Check `GET /health` und die Auth-Prüfung (unterstützt sowohl `WORKER_SECRET` als auch `BRIDGE_SECRET` sowie `GITHUB_PAT` / `GITHUB_TOKEN`).
  3. Validiere mit `npx tsc --noEmit` (in `packages/edge-worker`) sowie `node scripts/patches_and_tests/worker-smoke.mjs`. Dokumentiere das Ergebnis in `plans/TEST_LOG.md` und den Checkpoint in `plans/EXECUTION_LOG.md`.

## STUFE 4: Pre-Flight Linter (`linter.ts`) & Self-Healing Loop (`#delegate #solve #flash` – Arbeitspaket 6 & 7)

- **Voraussetzung:** Stufe 2 (`PreflightLinterHook` in `patcher.ts`) und Stufe 3 (`userscript-hud`) geplant/bereit.
- **Pflicht-Lektüre vorab:** `agents.md` (Root), `.agents/rules/edge-worker-architecture.md` und `.agents/rules/userscript-shadow-hud.md`.
- **Arbeitspaket 6 (Untertool C `linter.ts` – AST/Syntax-Prüfung & Custom Rules ohne Fail-Fast | Complexity: `6/10` | `Model: 'flash'`):**
  1. Implementiere `packages/edge-worker/src/modules/linter.ts`:
     - Exportiert `runPreflightLinter(files, preflight)` kompatibel zu `PreflightLinterHook`.
     - **AST-/Syntax-Check (`stage: "OXC_SYNTAX"`):** Prüft alle geänderten `.ts`, `.tsx`, `.js`, `.jsx`, `.mjs`, `.cjs`, `.json`-Dateien im RAM (unterstützt `oxc-parser` WASM + deterministischen Worker-AST/Delimiter/String/JSON-Syntax-Verifier mit exakter `line`, `column` und `codeSnippet`).
     - **Custom Rules (`stage: "CUSTOM_RULE"`):** Prüft `validateNoPlaceholders`, `preflight.forbidPatterns` (RegExp mit Zeilen-/Spaltenberechnung) und `preflight.requireExports` (Symbol-Export-Nachweis).
     - **Non-Fail-Fast (`P1` Komponente 4):** Sammelt sämtliche Fehler über alle geänderten Dateien hinweg im Array `errors: LinterError[]` und bindet `runPreflightLinter` als Standard-Hook in `patcher.ts` (`handlePatchRequest`) ein.
- **Arbeitspaket 7 (Self-Healing Auto-Retry Loop im HUD & End-to-End Smoke-Probes 8–9 | Complexity: `4/10` | `Model: 'flash'`):**
  1. Implementiere `packages/userscript-hud/src/services/selfHealing.ts` und binde es in `shadowHud.ts` ein:
     - Erzeugt bei `status === "PREFLIGHT_FAILED"` aus `errors: LinterError[]` einen kompakten `#solve`-Korrektur-Prompt mit Datei, Zeile, Spalte, Fehlermeldung und Snippet.
     - Führt bei aktiviertem Auto-Pilot bis zu `retryCount < 3` automatische Korrektur-Turns aus und setzt `retryCount = 0` bei `status === "COMMITTED"` zurück.
  2. Erweitere `scripts/patches_and_tests/worker-smoke.mjs` um **Probe 8 (`linter.ts` Syntax & Custom Rules Aggregation)** und **Probe 9 (`userscript-hud` Bundle, Trusted-Types Shadow-DOM & Self-Healing Loop)** und führe den vollständigen End-to-End-Verifikationslauf (`tsc --noEmit` + `worker-smoke.mjs` `9/9 PASS`) durch.

================
File: scripts/patches_and_tests/test-local-cli.mjs
================
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'url';
import { execSync } from 'child_process';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const ROOT_DIR = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..');
const CLI_PATH = path.join(ROOT_DIR, 'packages/local-cli/dist/cli.mjs');

console.log('=== Stufe II: Funktionaler Test des Local CLI (packages/local-cli) ===\n');

let passCount = 0;
let totalCount = 0;

function assert(condition, message) {
  totalCount++;
  if (!condition) {
    console.error(`  ❌ [FAIL] ${message}`);
    throw new Error(`Assertion failed: ${message}`);
  }
  console.log(`  ✅ [PASS] ${message}`);
  passCount++;
}

// 1. Test CLI Help output
try {
  const helpOut = execSync(`node "${CLI_PATH}" --help`, { encoding: 'utf-8' });
  assert(helpOut.includes('Gemini Bridge Local CLI'), 'CLI --help displays banner');
  assert(helpOut.includes('--prompt'), 'CLI --help displays --prompt option');
  assert(helpOut.includes('--skip-commit'), 'CLI --help displays --skip-commit option');
} catch (e) {
  assert(false, `CLI --help failed: ${e.message}`);
}

// 2. Test CLI without arguments (exits 1 and prints usage)
try {
  execSync(`node "${CLI_PATH}"`, { encoding: 'utf-8', stdio: 'pipe' });
  assert(false, 'CLI without arguments should exit with code 1');
} catch (e) {
  assert(e.status === 1, 'CLI without arguments correctly exits with status code 1');
  assert(e.stdout.includes('Usage:'), 'CLI without arguments displays usage');
}

// 3. Test CLI with prompt but without GEMINI_API_KEY (exits 1 with clear error)
try {
  execSync(`node "${CLI_PATH}" -p "Test prompt"`, {
    encoding: 'utf-8',
    stdio: 'pipe',
    env: { ...process.env, GEMINI_API_KEY: '' }
  });
  assert(false, 'CLI without GEMINI_API_KEY should exit with code 1');
} catch (e) {
  assert(e.status === 1, 'CLI without API key exits with code 1');
  const errOutput = e.stderr || e.stdout;
  assert(errOutput.includes('GEMINI_API_KEY is not set'), 'Error message clearly indicates missing GEMINI_API_KEY');
}

// 4. Test programmatic helper functions: parseCliArgs, extractJsonFromResponse & applyPatchesInMemory
const { parseCliArgs, extractJsonFromResponse, applyPatchesInMemory } = await import(pathToFileURL(CLI_PATH).href);

const parsed = parseCliArgs([
  'node',
  'cli.mjs',
  '--prompt',
  'Fix bug in auth',
  '--path',
  'src/auth.ts',
  '--path',
  'src/utils.ts',
  '--repo',
  'sikanda/bridge',
  '--branch',
  'feature/auth',
  '--model',
  'gemini-2.5-pro',
  '--skip-commit'
]);

assert(parsed.input.prompt === 'Fix bug in auth', 'parseCliArgs correctly parses --prompt');
assert(parsed.input.targetPaths?.length === 2, 'parseCliArgs collects multiple --path flags');
assert(parsed.input.repo === 'sikanda/bridge', 'parseCliArgs parses --repo');
assert(parsed.input.branch === 'feature/auth', 'parseCliArgs parses --branch');
assert(parsed.model === 'gemini-2.5-pro', 'parseCliArgs parses --model');
assert(parsed.skipCommit === true, 'parseCliArgs parses --skip-commit');

// 5. Test extractJsonFromResponse markdown stripping
const jsonRaw = '{"commitMessage":"test"}';
assert(extractJsonFromResponse(jsonRaw) === jsonRaw, 'extractJsonFromResponse passes plain JSON unchanged');

const jsonInFences = '```json\n{"commitMessage":"test in markdown"}\n```';
assert(extractJsonFromResponse(jsonInFences) === '{"commitMessage":"test in markdown"}', 'extractJsonFromResponse strips ```json markdown fences');

const jsonInGenericFences = '```\n{"commitMessage":"generic markdown"}\n```';
assert(extractJsonFromResponse(jsonInGenericFences) === '{"commitMessage":"generic markdown"}', 'extractJsonFromResponse strips generic ``` markdown fences');

// 6. Test In-Memory Preflight Integration in local runner (via applyPatchesInMemory)


// 6a. Hard Guardrail: Reject LLM Placeholders (// ... existing code ...)
const placeholderPatch = [
  {
    path: 'test.ts',
    action: 'modify',
    blocks: [
      {
        search: 'const a = 1;',
        replace: 'const a = 1;\n// ... existing code ...\nconst b = 2;'
      }
    ]
  }
];
const placeholderResult = await applyPatchesInMemory(placeholderPatch, { 'test.ts': 'const a = 1;' });
assert(placeholderResult.errors.length > 0, 'In-Memory Preflight catches placeholder patterns');
assert(placeholderResult.errors[0].message.includes('LLM placeholder'), 'Error specifically identifies LLM placeholder');

// 6b. Syntax Error Guardrail: Reject unmatched curly brackets
const syntaxErrorPatch = [
  {
    path: 'test.ts',
    action: 'modify',
    blocks: [
      {
        search: 'function test() {}',
        replace: 'function test() { const invalid = 123;'
      }
    ]
  }
];
const syntaxResult = await applyPatchesInMemory(syntaxErrorPatch, { 'test.ts': 'function test() {}' });
assert(syntaxResult.errors.length > 0, 'In-Memory Preflight catches unclosed syntax brackets');
assert(syntaxResult.errors[0].stage === 'OXC_SYNTAX', 'Error stage is correctly tagged as OXC_SYNTAX');

// 6c. Clean Patch Application: Valid Search/Replace with 0 errors
const validPatch = [
  {
    path: 'test.ts',
    action: 'modify',
    blocks: [
      {
        search: 'export const version = "1.0.0";',
        replace: 'export const version = "2.0.0";'
      }
    ]
  }
];
const validResult = await applyPatchesInMemory(validPatch, { 'test.ts': 'export const version = "1.0.0";' });
assert(validResult.errors.length === 0, 'Clean patch produces 0 preflight errors');
assert(validResult.additions[0].content === 'export const version = "2.0.0";', 'Addition contains correctly patched content');

console.log(`\n🎉 Stufe II Test abgeschlossen: ${passCount}/${totalCount} Assertions bestanden!`);

File: scripts/patches_and_tests/test-webhook-worker.mjs
