import type {
  GeminiPatchPayload,
  FilePatchOperation,
  PatchBlock,
  CommitRequest,
  HarvestedPatch,
} from '../types';

/**
 * Extracts a file path hint from code comments or preceding sibling elements in the DOM.
 */
export function extractPathFromCode(codeText: string, preElement?: HTMLElement | null): string {
  // 1. Inline comment hints: // filepath: ... | # path: ... | /* path: ... */
  const inlineMatch = codeText.match(/(?:\/\/|#|\/\*)\s*(?:filepath|file|path):\s*([^\r\n*]+)/i);
  if (inlineMatch && inlineMatch[1]) {
    return inlineMatch[1].trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
  }

  // 2. Look for filename header right above the code block in the DOM
  if (preElement) {
    let prev = preElement.previousElementSibling;
    for (let i = 0; i < 3 && prev; i++) {
      const text = prev.textContent?.trim() || '';
      const headerMatch = text.match(/(?:File|Pfad|Datei)?:\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_\-./\\]+\.[a-zA-Z0-9]+)/i);
      if (headerMatch && headerMatch[1]) {
        return headerMatch[1].trim();
      }
      if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-./\\]+\.[a-zA-Z0-9]+$/.test(text)) {
        return text;
      }
      prev = prev.previousElementSibling;
    }
  }

  return '';
}

export function isSearchReplace(text: string): boolean {
  return text.includes('<<<<<<<') && text.includes('=======') && text.includes('>>>>>>>');
}

/**
 * Parses a V2 GeminiPatchPayload from code text (e.g. from ```json code blocks).
 */
export function parseV2PatchPayloadFromCode(codeText: string): GeminiPatchPayload | null {
  if (!codeText || codeText.trim().length === 0) return null;

  let textToParse = codeText.trim();

  // Strip markdown ```json code fences if present
  const fenceMatch = textToParse.match(/^```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```$/i);
  if (fenceMatch) {
    textToParse = fenceMatch[1].trim();
  }

  // Quick check: does it look like JSON?
  if (!textToParse.startsWith('{') || !textToParse.endsWith('}')) {
    // Try to find the outermost JSON object
    const startIdx = textToParse.indexOf('{');
    const endIdx = textToParse.lastIndexOf('}');
    if (startIdx !== -1 && endIdx > startIdx) {
      textToParse = textToParse.substring(startIdx, endIdx + 1);
    } else {
      return null;
    }
  }

  try {
    const obj = JSON.parse(textToParse);
    if (
      obj &&
      typeof obj === 'object' &&
      typeof obj.commitMessage === 'string' &&
      Array.isArray(obj.patches) &&
      obj.patches.length > 0
    ) {
      // Validate patches structure
      const validPatches = obj.patches.every(
        (p: any) =>
          p &&
          typeof p.path === 'string' &&
          (p.action === 'modify' || p.action === 'create' || p.action === 'delete')
      );
      if (validPatches) {
        return obj as GeminiPatchPayload;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Not valid JSON
  }

  return null;
}

/**
 * Parses search/replace blocks or raw file content into both V2 GeminiPatchPayload and V1 CommitRequest.
 */
export function convertLegacyBlocksToV2Payload(
  codeText: string,
  defaultPath?: string
): { payload: GeminiPatchPayload; legacyPayload: CommitRequest } | null {
  if (!codeText || codeText.trim().length === 0) return null;

  const detectedPath = extractPathFromCode(codeText) || defaultPath || 'src/update.ts';
  const hasDiffBlocks = isSearchReplace(codeText);

  if (hasDiffBlocks) {
    const blocks: PatchBlock[] = [];
    const regex = /<{7}[^\r\n]*\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n={7}\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n>{7}[^\r\n]*/g;
    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;

    while ((match = regex.exec(codeText)) !== null) {
      blocks.push({
        search: match[1],
        replace: match[2],
      });
    }

    if (blocks.length > 0) {
      const fileOp: FilePatchOperation = {
        path: detectedPath,
        action: 'modify',
        blocks,
      };

      const payload: GeminiPatchPayload = {
        commitMessage: `refactor(${detectedPath}): apply search/replace patch`,
        patches: [fileOp],
      };

      const legacyPayload: CommitRequest = {
        repo: '',
        message: payload.commitMessage,
        files: [
          {
            path: detectedPath,
            diff: codeText,
            patches: blocks,
          },
        ],
      };

      return { payload, legacyPayload };
    }
  }

  // Not search/replace: Treat as full file update
  const fileOp: FilePatchOperation = {
    path: detectedPath,
    action: 'create',
    fullContent: codeText,
  };

  const payload: GeminiPatchPayload = {
    commitMessage: `feat(${detectedPath}): update file via Gemini Git Bridge`,
    patches: [fileOp],
  };

  const legacyPayload: CommitRequest = {
    repo: '',
    message: payload.commitMessage,
    files: [
      {
        path: detectedPath,
        content: codeText,
      },
    ],
  };

  return { payload, legacyPayload };
}

/**
 * Starts the DOM Harvester MutationObserver.
 * Watches for code blocks and Gemini Canvas cards, attaching Trusted-Types-safe buttons.
 */
export function startDomHarvester(
  onPatchHarvested: (patch: HarvestedPatch) => void,
  onInlineApply: (patch: HarvestedPatch) => void
): MutationObserver {
  const processedSet = new WeakSet<Element>();

  function processCodeBlock(pre: HTMLElement) {
    if (processedSet.has(pre) || pre.getAttribute('data-ggh-processed') === 'true') {
      return;
    }
    processedSet.add(pre);
    pre.setAttribute('data-ggh-processed', 'true');

    const codeEl = pre.querySelector('code') || pre;
    const codeText = (codeEl.textContent || '').trim();
    if (codeText.length < 15) return;

    let harvested: HarvestedPatch | null = null;

    // Check for V2 JSON payload first
    const v2Payload = parseV2PatchPayloadFromCode(codeText);
    if (v2Payload) {
      harvested = {
        id: 'patch-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7),
        mode: 'v2_json',
        commitMessage: v2Payload.commitMessage,
        payload: v2Payload,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),
      };
    } else {
      // Check for legacy diff or file
      const detectedPath = extractPathFromCode(codeText, pre);
      const converted = convertLegacyBlocksToV2Payload(codeText, detectedPath || undefined);
      if (converted) {
        harvested = {
          id: 'patch-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7),
          mode: isSearchReplace(codeText) ? 'v1_search_replace' : 'v2_json',
          commitMessage: converted.payload.commitMessage,
          payload: converted.payload,
          legacyPayload: converted.legacyPayload,
          timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),
        };
      }
    }

    if (!harvested) return;

    onPatchHarvested(harvested);

    // Create Trusted-Types-safe inline action button
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'ggb-inline-btn';
    btn.style.cssText = `
      display: inline-flex !important;
      align-items: center !important;
      gap: 6px !important;
      padding: 5px 12px !important;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #0284c7, #0369a1) !important;
      color: #ffffff !important;
      border: 1px solid #38bdf8 !important;
      border-radius: 8px !important;
      font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif !important;
      font-size: 12px !important;
      font-weight: 600 !important;
      cursor: pointer !important;
      box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.35) !important;
      margin: 6px !important;
      z-index: 2147483647 !important;
      user-select: none !important;
      transition: all 0.2s ease !important;
    `;
    btn.textContent =
      harvested.mode === 'v2_json' ? '⚡ Push Patch (/api/patch)' : '🚀 Push via Bridge';

    const currentPatch = harvested;
    btn.onclick = (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      onInlineApply(currentPatch);
    };

    const codeHeader = pre.parentElement?.querySelector(
      '.code-block-decoration, .code-header, [class*="header"]'
    );
    if (codeHeader) {
      if (codeHeader.querySelector('.ggb-inline-btn')) return;
      codeHeader.appendChild(btn);
    } else {
      if (pre.querySelector('.ggb-inline-btn')) return;
      pre.style.position = 'relative';
      btn.style.position = 'absolute';
      btn.style.top = '8px';
      btn.style.right = '40px';
      pre.appendChild(btn);
    }
  }

  function processCanvasCards() {
    // Gemini Canvas export buttons & cards
    const exportCandidateButtons = Array.from(
      document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('button:not([data-ggh-canvas-processed])')
    );
    const exportBtns = exportCandidateButtons.filter((b) => {
      const txt = (b.textContent || b.getAttribute('aria-label') || '').trim();
      return txt.includes('Exportieren') || txt.includes('Export');
    });

    exportBtns.forEach((exportBtn) => {
      exportBtn.setAttribute('data-ggh-canvas-processed', 'true');
      const headerBar = exportBtn.parentElement;
      if (!headerBar || headerBar.querySelector('.ggb-canvas-btn')) return;

      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'ggb-canvas-btn';
      btn.style.cssText = `
        display: inline-flex !important;
        align-items: center !important;
        gap: 6px !important;
        padding: 5px 12px !important;
        background: #0284c7 !important;
        color: #ffffff !important;
        border: 1px solid #38bdf8 !important;
        border-radius: 8px !important;
        font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif !important;
        font-size: 12px !important;
        font-weight: 600 !important;
        cursor: pointer !important;
        margin-right: 8px !important;
        z-index: 2147483647 !important;
      `;
      btn.textContent = '🚀 Push Canvas to GitHub';

      btn.onclick = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();

        const card = exportBtn.closest(
          '[class*="card"], [class*="container"], [class*="editor"], [class*="canvas"], [class*="panel"], section, div'
        );
        let cardText = '';
        if (card) {
          const contentArea =
            card.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[contenteditable], [class*="content"], [class*="body"], [class*="text"]') ||
            (card as HTMLElement);
          cardText = contentArea.textContent || '';
        }

        const detectedPath = extractPathFromCode(cardText, null);
        const converted = convertLegacyBlocksToV2Payload(
          cardText,
          detectedPath || 'canvas-update.md'
        );

        if (converted) {
          const patch: HarvestedPatch = {
            id: 'canvas-' + Date.now(),
            mode: isSearchReplace(cardText) ? 'v1_search_replace' : 'v2_json',
            commitMessage: converted.payload.commitMessage,
            payload: converted.payload,
            legacyPayload: converted.legacyPayload,
            timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),
          };
          onInlineApply(patch);
        }
      };

      headerBar.insertBefore(btn, exportBtn);
    });
  }

  function scanDOM() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
      'code-block:not([data-ggh-processed]), pre:not([data-ggh-processed])'
    );
    blocks.forEach((el) => {
      if (el.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'pre' && el.closest('code-block')) {
        el.setAttribute('data-ggh-processed', 'true');
        return;
      }
      processCodeBlock(el);
    });
    processCanvasCards();
  }

  const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
    scanDOM();
  });

  if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
    if (document.body) {
      observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    } else {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        if (document.body) {
          observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
        }
      });
    }
    scanDOM();
  }

  return observer;
}

export * from '../../../shared/types';
import type { GeminiPatchPayload, CommitRequest, LinterError } from '../../../shared/types';

export interface HudConfig {

# Master-Orchestrator Setup & Kontext-Handoff Prompts (`00_STARTCHAT_ORCHESTRATOR.md`)

> **Verwendungszweck:** Enthält zwei fertige Copy-Paste-Prompts für den Start eines **neuen, schlanken Chat-Fensters**, ohne dass irgendwelche Architektur-, `P1`-, Subroutinen- oder Status-Informationen verlorengehen:
> 1. **PROMPT A (Universal Master-Orchestrator Setup Prompt):** Startet den Master-Orchestrator in einem neuen Chat mit vollem Systemverständnis, minimalem Context-Window und unserer Modell-Politik (**Argon Medium** für Planung/Review & `Complexity >= 6`, **Flash `Model: 'flash'`** für Subagenten mit `Complexity < 6`).
> 2. **PROMPT B (3-Punkte-Code- & Security-Review `#review` vor Live-Deployment):** Startet direkt den strukturierten Review-Durchlauf gemäß [plans/01_PROJECT_REVIEW_AND_HANDOFF.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/plans/01_PROJECT_REVIEW_AND_HANDOFF.md), bevor der gespeicherte Live-Deployment-Plan ([plans/02_LIVE_DEPLOYMENT_AND_E2E_PLAN.md](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/plans/02_LIVE_DEPLOYMENT_AND_E2E_PLAN.md)) ausgeführt wird.

Du bist der **Master-Orchestrator & Architektur-Hüter** für **`cloudflare_orchestrator_Bridge`** (`githubgeminibridge`).

  - **Schicht 2 – Cloudflare Edge-Worker (`packages/edge-worker/src/`):** `index.ts` (Dual-Secret Auth & V1/V2 Router), `utils/github.ts` (`getBranchHeadOid`, `fetchMultipleFilesViaGraphQL`, `createCommitOnBranch`, `commitSingleFileViaGraphQL`), `utils/encoding.ts`, `modules/mixer.ts` (`POST /api/mix` 1-Subrequest Tarball-Stream), `modules/patcher.ts` + `modules/patchInMemory.ts` (`POST /api/patch` Non-Fail-Fast Batch-Patcher & V1 `/push`/`/commit`), `modules/linter.ts` + `modules/syntaxScanner.ts` (`OXC_SYNTAX` & `CUSTOM_RULE` Pre-Flight Linter).
  - **Schicht 1 – Tampermonkey Shadow-DOM HUD (`packages/userscript-hud/src/` & Root `gemini-bridge.user.js`):** `index.ts`, `types.ts`, `services/storage.ts` (`sanitizeNoGithubPat`), `services/workerClient.ts` (`GM_xmlhttpRequest`), `services/selfHealing.ts` (`retryCount < 3`), `dom/injector.ts` (`ClipboardEvent('paste')`), `dom/harvester.ts` (V2 JSON + V1 Search/Replace + Gemini Canvas `enhanceCanvasCards`), `ui/shadowHud.ts` + `ui/hudStylesAndDom.ts` (Trusted-Types Shadow-DOM `#gemini-edge-hud-root`).
- **Verifikations-Stand:** Alle 18 TypeScript-Dateien `< 500` Zeilen (`overLimitZone.length === 0`), `tsc --noEmit` (0 Fehler in beiden Paketen), `worker-smoke.mjs` **`10/10 PASS`**.

**Deine erste Aufgabe in diesem Turn (Read-Only Orientierung):**
Lies jetzt nur die oben unter `1ST PRIORITY` genannten Status-, Verfassungs- und Handoff-Dateien ein, bestätige kurz den Stand `10/10 PASS` und melde **„Master-Orchestrator (Argon Medium) bereit für Review (`01_PROJECT_REVIEW_AND_HANDOFF.md`) oder Live-Deployment (`02_LIVE_DEPLOYMENT_AND_E2E_PLAN.md`)“**.

================
File: packages/edge-worker/src/modules/mixChunker.ts
================
import type { MixPart } from '../types';
import { escapeCdata } from './mixer';

export const DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_CHARS = 85_000;     // 85k Chars (15% buffer below Gemini's 100k char cutoff)
export const HARD_GEMINI_CHAR_LIMIT = 100_000;     // Hard 100k character cutoff in Gemini Web UI
export const MAX_GEMINI_PARTS = 10;                // Gemini Web maximum file attachments per prompt guardrail
export const DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_BYTES = 512 * 1024; // Legacy byte ceiling
export const DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_TOKENS = 125_000;   // Legacy token ceiling

/**
 * Universal instructions embedded directly into the RepoMix snapshot.
 * Grounding the AI on codebase structure, distinguishing read-only vs modification tasks,
 * and enforcing bridge-compatible patch formatting without placeholders.
 */
export const DEFAULT_MIX_INSTRUCTIONS = [
  '<instructions>',
  '# REPOSITORY CONTEXT & PROTOCOL',
  '1. CONTEXT: The files below represent the authoritative codebase snapshot at commit headOid.',
  '2. MULTI-PART: If split across parts, all parts form the complete repository (Part 1 contains the master file_tree).',
  '3. READ-ONLY TASKS: If the user asks questions, explains logic, or reviews code: Answer naturally in the requested language. Do NOT emit patches.',
  '4. MODIFICATION TASKS: If and only if the user explicitly requests code changes, bugfixes, or new files:',
  '   - Never rewrite whole existing files.',
  '   - Never use placeholders (e.g. "// ... existing code ...", "/* rest unchanged */").',
  '   - Search snippets must match existing code verbatim (including whitespace/indentation).',
  '   - Output changes strictly in one of these formats:',
  '     Format A (V2 Patch-JSON, recommended):',
  '     ```json',
  '     {',
  '       "branch": "<branch>",',
  '       "expectedHeadOid": "<headOid>",',
  '       "message": "feat/fix: summary",',
  '       "files": [',
  '         { "path": "relative/path.ts", "type": "modify", "search": "<exact old code>", "replace": "<new replacement code>" },',
  '         { "path": "relative/new_file.ts", "type": "create", "content": "<full file content>" }',
  '       ]',
  '     }',
  '     ```',
  '     Format B (Search/Replace):',
  '     // filepath: relative/path.ts',
  '     <<<<<<< SEARCH',
  '     <exact old code>',
  '     =======',
  '     <new replacement code>',
  '     >>>>>>> REPLACE',
  '</instructions>',
].join('\n');

export const CONTINUATION_MIX_INSTRUCTIONS = (partNum: number, totalParts: number): string =>
  `<instructions>\n# Part ${partNum}/${totalParts} Continuation: Refer to Part 1 for master file_tree and modification protocol.\n</instructions>`;

export interface ChunkMixInput {
  owner: string;
  repo: string;
  branch: string;
  headOid: string;
  fileTreeEntries: string[];
  files: Array<{ path: string; content: string }>;
  recentCommitsText?: string;
  maxChunkChars?: number;
  maxChunkBytes?: number;
  maxChunkTokens?: number;
  splitOutput?: boolean;
}

export interface ChunkMixResult {
  xmlContent: string;
  parts?: MixPart[];
  isChunked: boolean;
  stats: {
    fileCount: number;
    estimatedTokens: number;
    charCount: number;
  };
}

/**
 * Formats a single file as a safe XML `<file>` element.
 */
function formatFileXml(f: { path: string; content: string }): string {
  return `  <file path="${f.path}"><![CDATA[${escapeCdata(f.content)}]]></file>`;
}

/**
 * Builds a complete structured XML document for a single mix part or the whole repository.
 */
function buildPartXml(params: {
  owner: string;
  repo: string;
  branch: string;
  headOid: string;
  partNum?: number;
  totalParts?: number;
  instructionsText?: string;
  recentCommitsText?: string;
  treeText: string;
  fileXmlList: string[];
}): string {
  const repoAttr =
    params.partNum !== undefined && params.totalParts !== undefined
      ? `<repository repo="${params.owner}/${params.repo}" branch="${params.branch}" headOid="${params.headOid}" part="${params.partNum}" totalParts="${params.totalParts}">`
      : `<repository repo="${params.owner}/${params.repo}" branch="${params.branch}" headOid="${params.headOid}">`;

  const sections: string[] = [repoAttr];
  if (params.instructionsText) {
    sections.push(params.instructionsText);
  }
  if (params.recentCommitsText) {
    sections.push(params.recentCommitsText);
  }
  sections.push(
    '<file_tree>',
    params.treeText,
    '</file_tree>',
    '<files>',
    ...params.fileXmlList,
    '</files>',
    '</repository>'
  );

  return sections.join('\n');
}

/**
 * Builds structured XML representation(s) of a repository.
 * Atomically partitions files across multiple sequentially numbered MixParts
 * without splitting individual files, keeping each chunk strictly within character budgets (<= 85k default, <= 100k hard limit).
 */
export function buildChunkedMixXml(input: ChunkMixInput): ChunkMixResult {
  const {
    owner,
    repo,
    branch,
    headOid,
    fileTreeEntries,
    files,
    maxChunkChars = DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_CHARS,
    maxChunkBytes = DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_BYTES,
    maxChunkTokens = DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_TOKENS,
    splitOutput,
  } = input;

  const effectiveMaxChars = Math.min(
    maxChunkChars,
    maxChunkBytes,
    maxChunkTokens * 4
  );
  const hardFileLimit = Math.max(effectiveMaxChars, HARD_GEMINI_CHAR_LIMIT);

  // 1. Format all files and filter oversized single files (> 100k chars)
  const treeEntries = [...fileTreeEntries];
  const validFiles: Array<{ path: string; content: string; fileXml: string }> = [];

  for (const f of files) {
    const fileXml = formatFileXml(f);
    const standaloneXml = buildPartXml({
      owner,
      repo,
      branch,
      headOid,
      partNum: 1,
      totalParts: 1,
      instructionsText: DEFAULT_MIX_INSTRUCTIONS,
      treeText: f.path,
      fileXmlList: [fileXml],
    });

    if (standaloneXml.length > hardFileLimit) {
      const idx = treeEntries.indexOf(f.path);
      const skippedAnnotation = `${f.path} [SKIPPED: >100k chars for Gemini Chat limit]`;
      if (idx !== -1) {
        treeEntries[idx] = skippedAnnotation;
      } else {
        treeEntries.push(skippedAnnotation);
      }
    } else {
      validFiles.push({ path: f.path, content: f.content, fileXml });
    }
  }

  // 2. Full Tree with budget cap (<= 14k chars to reserve >= 71k chars for code)
  const maxTreeChars = Math.min(Math.floor(effectiveMaxChars * 0.45), 14_000);
  let fullTreeXml = [...treeEntries].sort().join('\n');
  if (fullTreeXml.length > maxTreeChars) {
    const truncated = fullTreeXml.slice(0, maxTreeChars);
    const lastNewline = truncated.lastIndexOf('\n');
    fullTreeXml =
      (lastNewline !== -1 ? truncated.slice(0, lastNewline) : truncated) +
      '\n... [file_tree truncated to preserve character budget]';
  }

  // 3. Single-part fast path
  const singleXml = buildPartXml({
    owner,
    repo,
    branch,
    headOid,
    instructionsText: DEFAULT_MIX_INSTRUCTIONS,
    recentCommitsText: input.recentCommitsText,
    treeText: fullTreeXml,
    fileXmlList: validFiles.map((f) => f.fileXml),
  });

  const shouldSplit =
    splitOutput === true ||
    (splitOutput !== false &&
      validFiles.length > 1 &&
      singleXml.length > effectiveMaxChars);

  if (!shouldSplit) {
    return {
      xmlContent: singleXml,
      isChunked: false,
      stats: {
        fileCount: validFiles.length,
        estimatedTokens: Math.ceil(singleXml.length / 4),
        charCount: singleXml.length,
      },
    };
  }

  // 4. Multi-part atomic pre-file partitioning with Gemini 10-parts guardrail
  function partitionFiles(budgetChars: number) {
    const buckets: Array<Array<{ path: string; content: string; fileXml: string }>> = [];
    let currentBucket: Array<{ path: string; content: string; fileXml: string }> = [];
    const part1BaseOverhead =
      fullTreeXml.length +
      220 +
      DEFAULT_MIX_INSTRUCTIONS.length +
      (input.recentCommitsText ? input.recentCommitsText.length + 1 : 0);
    let currentBucketChars = part1BaseOverhead;

    for (let i = 0; i < validFiles.length; i++) {
      const f = validFiles[i];
      const isPart1 = buckets.length === 0;
      const fileAdditionChars = isPart1
        ? f.fileXml.length + 1
        : f.fileXml.length + 1 + f.path.length + 1;

      if (currentBucket.length > 0) {
        if (currentBucketChars + fileAdditionChars > budgetChars) {
          buckets.push(currentBucket);
          currentBucket = [];
          currentBucketChars = 260 + CONTINUATION_MIX_INSTRUCTIONS(2, 2).length;
        }
      } else if (isPart1) {
        // Empty Part 1: check if file alone with fullTreeXml would exceed hardFileLimit
        if (currentBucketChars + fileAdditionChars > Math.max(budgetChars, hardFileLimit)) {
          buckets.push([]);
          currentBucket = [];
          currentBucketChars = 260 + CONTINUATION_MIX_INSTRUCTIONS(2, 2).length;
        }
      }

      currentBucket.push(f);
      currentBucketChars += (buckets.length === 0 ? f.fileXml.length + 1 : f.fileXml.length + 1 + f.path.length + 1);
    }

    if (currentBucket.length > 0) {
      buckets.push(currentBucket);
    }
    return buckets;
  }

  let dynamicMaxChars = effectiveMaxChars;
  let chunkBuckets = partitionFiles(dynamicMaxChars);

  if (chunkBuckets.length > MAX_GEMINI_PARTS) {
    // Upscale budget so total parts fit within MAX_GEMINI_PARTS
    const totalRawChars =
      validFiles.reduce((acc, f) => acc + f.fileXml.length, 0) +
      fullTreeXml.length +
      (input.recentCommitsText ? input.recentCommitsText.length : 0);
    dynamicMaxChars = Math.max(dynamicMaxChars, Math.ceil(totalRawChars / (MAX_GEMINI_PARTS - 1)));
    chunkBuckets = partitionFiles(dynamicMaxChars);

    while (chunkBuckets.length > MAX_GEMINI_PARTS) {
      let minCombinedSize = Infinity;
      let minIndex = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < chunkBuckets.length - 1; j++) {
        const combined = chunkBuckets[j].length + chunkBuckets[j + 1].length;
        if (combined < minCombinedSize) {
          minCombinedSize = combined;
          minIndex = j;
        }
      }
      chunkBuckets[minIndex] = [...chunkBuckets[minIndex], ...chunkBuckets[minIndex + 1]];
      chunkBuckets.splice(minIndex + 1, 1);
    }
  }

  const totalParts = chunkBuckets.length;
  const parts: MixPart[] = [];
  let totalChars = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < totalParts; i++) {
    const bucket = chunkBuckets[i];
    const partNum = i + 1;
    const partTreeText =
      i === 0
        ? fullTreeXml
        : `# Part ${partNum}/${totalParts} files (${bucket.length} files):\n${bucket.map((b) => b.path).join('\n')}`;

    const partInstructions =
      partNum === 1
        ? DEFAULT_MIX_INSTRUCTIONS
        : CONTINUATION_MIX_INSTRUCTIONS(partNum, totalParts);

    const partXml = buildPartXml({
      owner,
      repo,
      branch,
      headOid,
      partNum,
      totalParts,
      instructionsText: partInstructions,
      recentCommitsText: partNum === 1 ? input.recentCommitsText : undefined,
      treeText: partTreeText,
      fileXmlList: bucket.map((f) => f.fileXml),
    });

    totalChars += partXml.length;

    parts.push({
      partIndex: partNum,
      totalParts,
      fileName: totalParts === 1 ? 'repo-mix.txt' : `repo-mix.part${partNum}.txt`,
      xmlContent: partXml,
      stats: {
        fileCount: bucket.length,
        estimatedTokens: Math.ceil(partXml.length / 4),
        charCount: partXml.length,
      },
    });
  }

  return {
    xmlContent: parts[0]?.xmlContent ?? singleXml,
    parts,
    isChunked: true,
    stats: {
      fileCount: validFiles.length,
      estimatedTokens: Math.ceil(totalChars / 4),
      charCount: totalChars,
    },
  };
}

================
File: packages/edge-worker/src/index.ts
================
import type { WorkerEnv, HybridWorkerEnv, CommitRequest, MixRequest, WorkerPatchRequest } from './types';
import { utf8ToBase64 } from './utils/encoding';
import { resolveFileContent, handlePatchRequest } from './modules/patcher';
import { handleMixRequest } from './modules/mixer';
import { handleGithubWebhookRequest } from './modules/webhookHandler';
import { getBranchHeadOid, fetchFileContent, createCommitOnBranch, executeGraphQL, parseRepoOwner } from './utils/github';

const CORS_HEADERS: Record<string, string> = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type, Authorization, x-bridge-secret',
  'Access-Control-Max-Age': '86400',
};

export function jsonResponse(data: any, status = 200): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), {
    status,
    headers: {
      ...CORS_HEADERS,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json'
    }
  });
}

export function errorResponse(message: string, status = 400, details?: any): Response {
  return jsonResponse({
    success: false,
    error: message,
    ...(details ? { details } : {})
  }, status);
}

export function resolveSecret(env: WorkerEnv): string {
  return env.WORKER_SECRET || env.BRIDGE_SECRET || '';
}

export function resolveGithubToken(env: WorkerEnv): string {
  return env.GITHUB_PAT || env.GITHUB_TOKEN || '';
}

export function verifyAuth(request: Request, env: WorkerEnv): boolean {
  const secret = resolveSecret(env);
  if (!secret) {
    return false;
  }

  const customHeader = request.headers.get('x-bridge-secret');
  if (customHeader && customHeader === secret) {
    return true;
  }

  const authHeader = request.headers.get('Authorization');
  if (authHeader) {
    const token = authHeader.replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, '').trim();
    if (token === secret) {
      return true;
    }
  }

  return false;
}

export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: WorkerEnv, ctx?: any): Promise<Response> {
    // 1. Handle CORS Preflight
    if (request.method === 'OPTIONS') {
      return new Response(null, {
        status: 204,
        headers: CORS_HEADERS
      });
    }

    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const path = url.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';

    // 2. Health check endpoint (public)
    if (request.method === 'GET' && (path === '/' || path === '/health')) {
      return jsonResponse({
        status: 'ok',
        service: 'gemini-git-bridge',
        version: '2.0.0',
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        hasBridgeSecret: Boolean(resolveSecret(env)),
        hasGithubToken: Boolean(resolveGithubToken(env))
      });
    }

    // 2b. GitHub Webhook endpoint (authenticated via HMAC-SHA256 signature, not Bearer token)
    if (path === '/api/webhook/github') {
      return handleGithubWebhookRequest(request, env as HybridWorkerEnv, ctx);
    }

    // 3. Authenticate all protected endpoints
    if (!verifyAuth(request, env)) {
      return errorResponse('Unauthorized: Invalid or missing WORKER_SECRET / BRIDGE_SECRET in Authorization or x-bridge-secret header', 401);
    }

    const githubToken = resolveGithubToken(env);
    if (!githubToken) {
      return errorResponse('Server configuration error: GITHUB_PAT / GITHUB_TOKEN secret is not set in Worker environment.', 500);
    }

    try {
      // 4. V1 Commit / Push Relay Endpoint (Supports POST /push, /commit, /api/commit)
      if (request.method === 'POST' && (path === '/push' || path === '/commit' || path === '/api/commit' || path === '/')) {
        const body = (await request.json().catch(() => {
          throw new Error('Malformed JSON payload');
        })) as CommitRequest;

        let owner = '';
        let repo = '';
        try {
          const parsed = parseRepoOwner(body.repo || '', body.owner);
          owner = parsed.owner;
          repo = parsed.repo;
        } catch {
          return errorResponse('Missing required fields: Repository must be specified as "owner/repo" or separate "owner" and "repo".');
        }

        const commitMessage = body.commitMessage || body.message;
        if (!commitMessage) {
          return errorResponse('Missing required field: "commitMessage" (or "message") must be specified.');
        }

        const targetBranch = body.targetBranch || body.branch || 'main';
        const fileChanges = body.changes || body.files || [];
        const deletions = body.deletions || [];

        if (fileChanges.length === 0 && deletions.length === 0) {
          return errorResponse('Commit must include at least one entry in "changes" or "files" or "deletions".');
        }

        // 4a. Resolve HEAD Commit OID for target branch to prevent race conditions
        const { headOid, resolvedBranch } = await getBranchHeadOid(owner, repo, targetBranch, githubToken);

        // 4b. Resolve File Changes (Apply Search/Replace, Hard Guardrail, & encode to Base64)
        const additions: { path: string; contents: string }[] = [];

        for (const fileInput of fileChanges) {
          if (!fileInput.path) {
            throw new Error('Every file entry must contain a "path" property.');
          }

          const contentFetcher = async (filePath: string) => {
            return await fetchFileContent(owner, repo, resolvedBranch, filePath, githubToken);
          };

          const resolvedText = await resolveFileContent(fileInput, contentFetcher);
          const encodedBase64 = utf8ToBase64(resolvedText);

          additions.push({
            path: fileInput.path,
            contents: encodedBase64
          });
        }

        // 4c. Format Deletions
        const formattedDeletions: { path: string }[] = deletions.map((del) => {
          return typeof del === 'string' ? { path: del } : { path: del.path };
        });

        // 4d. Execute createCommitOnBranch via GitHub GraphQL
        const commitResult = await createCommitOnBranch(
          owner,
          repo,
          resolvedBranch,
          headOid,
          commitMessage,
          additions,
          formattedDeletions,
          githubToken
        );

        return jsonResponse({
          success: true,
          commit: commitResult,
          branch: resolvedBranch,
          repository: `${owner}/${repo}`,
          filesChanged: additions.length + formattedDeletions.length
        });
      }

      // 5. V2 Mix Engine Endpoint (Stage 1)
      if (request.method === 'POST' && path === '/api/mix') {
        const body = (await request.json().catch(() => {
          throw new Error('Malformed JSON payload');
        })) as MixRequest;

        const mixResult = await handleMixRequest(body, githubToken);
        return jsonResponse(mixResult, mixResult.status === 'OK' ? 200 : 400);
      }

      // 6. V2 In-Memory Patcher Endpoint (Stage 2 & Stage 4)
      if (request.method === 'POST' && path === '/api/patch') {
        const body = (await request.json().catch(() => {
          throw new Error('Malformed JSON payload');
        })) as WorkerPatchRequest;

        const patchResult = await handlePatchRequest(body, githubToken);
        return jsonResponse(patchResult, patchResult.status === 'ERROR' ? 400 : 200);
      }

      // 7. Raw GraphQL Proxy Endpoint
      if (request.method === 'POST' && path === '/graphql') {
        const body: any = await request.json().catch(() => {
          throw new Error('Malformed JSON payload');
        });

        if (!body.query) {
          return errorResponse('Missing required field: "query" in GraphQL request.');
        }

        const result = await executeGraphQL(body.query, body.variables || {}, githubToken);
        return jsonResponse({ success: true, data: result });
      }

      return errorResponse(`Endpoint not found: ${request.method} ${path}`, 404);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Request processing error:', err);
      return errorResponse(err.message || 'Internal server error', 500, {
        stack: err.stack
      });
    }
  }
};

================
File: packages/userscript-hud/src/dom/injector.ts

/**
 * DOM-Injector for Google Gemini and Google AI Studio
 */

export function findPromptInputElement(): HTMLElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | null {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return null;

  // 1. Gemini Web (gemini.google.com)
  const geminiSelectors = [
    'rich-textarea div[contenteditable="true"]',
    'div.ql-editor[contenteditable="true"]',
    'div[contenteditable="true"][role="textbox"]',
    'div[contenteditable="true"]',
  ];

  for (const sel of geminiSelectors) {
    const el = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(sel);
    if (el && el.offsetParent !== null) {
      return el;
    }
  }

  // 2. Google AI Studio (aistudio.google.com)
  const aiStudioSelectors = [
    'ms-autosize-textarea textarea',
    'textarea[aria-label*="prompt" i]',
    'textarea[placeholder*="prompt" i]',
    'textarea.prompt-textarea',
    'textarea',
  ];

  for (const sel of aiStudioSelectors) {
    const el = document.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>(sel);
    if (el && el.offsetParent !== null) {
      return el;
    }
  }

  // 3. Fallback
  return document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('textarea, div[contenteditable="true"]');
}

export interface MixFileItem {
  fileName: string;
  xmlContent: string;
}

/**
 * Injects multiple repo-mix parts as a virtual file paste into the chat prompt area.
 */
export function injectRepoMixParts(parts: MixFileItem[]): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof document === 'undefined') return false;
  if (!parts || parts.length === 0) return false;

  const inputEl = findPromptInputElement();
  const target = inputEl || document.activeElement || document.body;

  try {
    const dataTransfer = new DataTransfer();
    for (const part of parts) {
      const file = new File([part.xmlContent], part.fileName, { type: 'text/plain' });
      dataTransfer.items.add(file);
    }

    const pasteEvent = new ClipboardEvent('paste', {
      clipboardData: dataTransfer,
      bubbles: true,
      cancelable: true,
    });

    target.dispatchEvent(pasteEvent);
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[Gemini Bridge Injector] Failed to inject repo mix parts:', err);
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Injects repo-mix.txt as a virtual file paste into the chat prompt area.
 */
export function injectRepoMixFile(xmlContent: string, fileName = 'repo-mix.txt'): boolean {
  return injectRepoMixParts([{ fileName, xmlContent }]);
}

/**
 * Focuses the prompt input, types promptText and optionally clicks the send/run button.
 */
export function injectPromptText(promptText: string, autoSubmit = false): boolean {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return false;

  const inputEl = findPromptInputElement();
  if (!inputEl) {
    console.warn('[Gemini Bridge Injector] Prompt input element not found');
    return false;
  }

  inputEl.focus();

  let inserted = false;
  try {
    inserted = document.execCommand('insertText', false, promptText);
  } catch (e) {
    inserted = false;
  }

  if (!inserted) {
    if (inputEl instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement || 'value' in inputEl) {
      const textarea = inputEl as HTMLTextAreaElement;
      const start = textarea.selectionStart ?? textarea.value.length;
      const end = textarea.selectionEnd ?? textarea.value.length;
      textarea.value = textarea.value.slice(0, start) + promptText + textarea.value.slice(end);
      textarea.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
      textarea.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
    } else {
      inputEl.textContent = (inputEl.textContent || '') + promptText;
      inputEl.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent('input', { bubbles: true, inputType: 'insertText', data: promptText }));
    }
  }

  if (autoSubmit) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const sendButtonSelectors = [
        'button.send-button',
        'button[aria-label*="Send" i]',
        'button[aria-label*="Senden" i]',
        'button[aria-label*="Run" i]',
        'ms-run-button button',
        'button.run-button',
      ];

      for (const sel of sendButtonSelectors) {
        const btn = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(sel);
        if (btn && !btn.disabled) {
          btn.click();
          break;
        }
      }
    }, 300);
  }

  return true;
}

import type {
  HudConfig,
  MixRequest,
  MixResponse,
  WorkerPatchRequest,
  WorkerPatchResponse,
  CommitRequest,
} from '../types';

/**
 * CSP-Bypass Invariant (P1 Komponente 5):
 * All requests to the Cloudflare Worker MUST use GM_xmlhttpRequest (or GM.xmlHttpRequest)
 * to bypass the host site's strict connect-src Content Security Policy.
 */
export async function gmRequestJson<T>(
  url: string,
  method: 'GET' | 'POST',
  secret: string,
  body?: unknown
): Promise<T> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    'Accept': 'application/json',
    'Authorization': `Bearer ${secret}`,
    'x-bridge-secret': secret,
  };

  let payloadString: string | undefined;
  if (body !== undefined) {
    headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
    payloadString = typeof body === 'string' ? body : JSON.stringify(body);
  }

  // 1. Preferred Tampermonkey / Greasemonkey API
  if (typeof GM_xmlhttpRequest === 'function') {
    return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
      GM_xmlhttpRequest({
        method,
        url,
        headers,
        data: payloadString,
        onload: (res) => {
          try {
            const data = JSON.parse(res.responseText);
            if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 300) {
              resolve(data as T);
            } else {
              const errMsg =
                data?.error || data?.errorMessage || `HTTP ${res.status}: ${res.responseText}`;
              reject(new Error(errMsg));
            }
          } catch (err) {
            if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 300) {
              resolve(res.responseText as unknown as T);
            } else {
              reject(new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}: ${res.responseText}`));
            }
          }
        },
        onerror: (err) => {
          reject(new Error(`Network error calling Cloudflare Worker: ${err?.statusText || err || 'Unknown error'}`));
        },
        ontimeout: () => {
          reject(new Error('Request to Cloudflare Worker timed out'));
        },
      });
    });
  }

  // 2. Async GM.xmlHttpRequest API
  if (typeof GM !== 'undefined' && typeof GM.xmlHttpRequest === 'function') {
    const res = await GM.xmlHttpRequest({
      method,
      url,
      headers,
      data: payloadString,
    });
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(res.responseText);
      if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 300) {
        return data as T;
      }
      throw new Error(data?.error || data?.errorMessage || `HTTP ${res.status}: ${res.responseText}`);
    } catch (e: any) {
      if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 300) {
        return res.responseText as unknown as T;
      }
      throw e;
    }
  }

  // 3. Fallback to standard fetch (e.g. node / dev harness)
  if (typeof fetch === 'function') {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      method,
      headers,
      body: payloadString,
    });
    const text = await res.text();
    let data: any;
    try {
      data = JSON.parse(text);
    } catch {
      data = text;
    }
    if (res.ok) {
      return data as T;
    }
    throw new Error(data?.error || data?.errorMessage || `HTTP ${res.status}: ${text}`);
  }

  throw new Error('No supported HTTP client found (GM_xmlhttpRequest, GM.xmlHttpRequest, or fetch)');
}

export async function checkWorkerHealth(
  cfg: HudConfig
): Promise<{ status: string; version?: string; hasBridgeSecret?: boolean; hasGithubToken?: boolean }> {
  const url = `${cfg.workerUrl}/health`;
  return gmRequestJson(url, 'GET', cfg.workerSecret);
}

export async function requestRepoMix(
  cfg: HudConfig,
  req?: Partial<MixRequest>
): Promise<MixResponse> {
  const url = `${cfg.workerUrl}/api/mix`;
  const payload: MixRequest = {
    repo: req?.repo || cfg.repo,
    branch: req?.branch || cfg.branch,
    includePaths: req?.includePaths || (cfg.includePaths.length > 0 ? cfg.includePaths : undefined),
    persistToGitHub: req?.persistToGitHub ?? cfg.persistToGitHub,
    maxChunkChars: req?.maxChunkChars ?? cfg.maxChunkChars,
    maxChunkBytes: req?.maxChunkBytes,
    maxChunkTokens: req?.maxChunkTokens,
    splitOutput: req?.splitOutput,
    includeRecentCommits: req?.includeRecentCommits ?? cfg.includeRecentCommits,
  };
  return gmRequestJson<MixResponse>(url, 'POST', cfg.workerSecret, payload);
}

export async function requestWorkerPatch(
  cfg: HudConfig,
  req: WorkerPatchRequest
): Promise<WorkerPatchResponse> {
  const url = `${cfg.workerUrl}/api/patch`;
  return gmRequestJson<WorkerPatchResponse>(url, 'POST', cfg.workerSecret, req);
}

export async function requestLegacyCommit(
  cfg: HudConfig,
  req: CommitRequest
): Promise<any> {
  // Support both /push and /commit on the worker
  const url = `${cfg.workerUrl}/push`;
  return gmRequestJson<any>(url, 'POST', cfg.workerSecret, req);
}

## 1. Zusammenspiel der Einzelteile (End-to-End Datenfluss über alle 3 Schichten)

1. **Kontext-Laden (`Mix-Flow` – Schicht 1 -> 2 -> 3):**
   - `shadowHud.ts` (im isolierten `#gemini-edge-hud-root` Shadow DOM) liest Konfiguration via `storage.ts` (`sanitizeNoGithubPat`) und ruft über `workerClient.ts` (`GM_xmlhttpRequest` CSP-Bypass) `POST /api/mix` an `packages/edge-worker/src/index.ts` auf (inkl. optionalem Subpfad-Filter `includePaths` und `maxChunkChars`).
   - `index.ts` prüft Auth (`verifyAuth`) und delegiert an `mixer.ts` (`handleMixRequest`).
   - `mixer.ts` lädt den GitHub-Tarball in **1 Subrequest**, dekomprimiert ihn per `DecompressionStream('gzip')`, liest die 40-stellige `headOid` aus dem PAX Global Header (`typeflag === 'g'`, Fallback `getBranchHeadOid` in `github.ts`), verwirft Ignorierlisten & Dateien `> 512 KB` direkt am 512-Byte-Header (`shouldSkipTarEntry` via `tarUtils.ts`), wertet dynamisch `.repomixignore.cf` aus und übergibt die Inhalte an `mixChunker.ts`.
   - `mixChunker.ts` partitioniert den Code atomar ohne Zerschneiden von Dateien in Chunks (`part 1 von N`) mit 85k-Char-Budget (`DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_CHARS`), 100k Hard-Cutoff, max 10 Parts (`MAX_GEMINI_PARTS = 10`) und generiert standardmäßig `.txt`-Dateien mit MIME `text/plain` (zur Vermeidung von Gemini XML-Parsing-Bugs).
   - `shadowHud.ts` speichert die 40-char `headOid` im State und übergibt die Chunks an `injector.ts` (`injectRepoMixParts`), der die `.txt`-Dateien per synthetischem `ClipboardEvent('paste')` nebeneinander als saubere Datei-Chips in Gemini / AI Studio einfügt.
2. **Code-Ernte & Atomarer Patch-Commit (`Patch-Flow` – Schicht 1 -> 2 -> 3):**
   - `harvester.ts` überwacht per `MutationObserver` Chat-Codeblöcke sowie Gemini Canvas-Karten (`enhanceCanvasCards`) und extrahiert V2 `GeminiPatchPayload`-JSON (oder konvertiert V1 `<<<<<<< SEARCH ... >>>>>>> REPLACE`-Blöcke).
   - **Queue-Management (Stufe 6.3):** Die geernteten Patches werden im HUD in einer Aktionsleiste dargestellt (`⚡ Apply All (${patchCount})` und `🗑️ Clear Queue`). Bei Klick auf `Apply All` erfolgt eine sequenzielle FIFO-Abarbeitung (`[...state.harvestedPatches].reverse()`) mit Fail-Fast (sofortiger Abbruch bei Fehlern), atomarem State-Pruning erfolgreicher Patches und OID-Kettenfortschreibung.
   - Bei Ausführung sendet `workerClient.ts` `POST /api/patch` inkl. `expectedHeadOid` an `index.ts` -> `patcher.ts` (`handlePatchRequest`).
   - `patcher.ts` prüft `expectedHeadOid` gegen `getBranchHeadOid` (`GIT_CONFLICT`-Schutz), lädt alle Zieldateien in **1 Subrequest** (`fetchMultipleFilesViaGraphQL` in `github.ts`) und ruft `patchInMemory.ts` (`applyPatchesInMemory`) auf.
   - `patchInMemory.ts` wendet `applySearchReplace` (3-Stufen-Kaskade) an, prüft `checkPlaceholderErrors` (`validateNoPlaceholders`) und ruft den Standard-Hook `runPreflightLinter` in `linter.ts` auf.
   - `linter.ts` + `syntaxScanner.ts` prüfen alle geänderten Dateien **ohne Fail-Fast** auf AST-/Lexikal-Syntaxfehler (`validateSyntaxInWorker`, `stage: 'OXC_SYNTAX'`) und Custom Rules (`forbidPatterns`, `requireExports`, `validateNoPlaceholders`, `stage: 'CUSTOM_RULE'`).
   - **Bei `0` Fehlern:** Atomarer Commit via `createCommitOnBranch` (`github.ts`) auf den Zielbranch (inkl. automatischer Branch-Erstellung via `createBranchRef` an `expectedHeadOid`, falls `targetBranch` abweicht); `shadowHud.ts` aktualisiert `headOid` und zeigt Compare-/PR-Link an.
3. **Self-Healing Loop (`PREFLIGHT_FAILED` – Schicht 2 -> 1):**
   - Sind Fehler in `errors: LinterError[]` vorhanden, bricht der Worker vor dem Commit mit `status: 'PREFLIGHT_FAILED'` ab.
   - `selfHealing.ts` (`executeSelfHealingTurn`) baut aus allen gesammelten Fehlern einen präzisen `#solve`-Korrektur-Prompt (`buildSelfHealingPrompt`) und injiziert ihn bei aktivem Auto-Pilot bis zu `retryCount < 3` (`MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES = 3`) automatisch über `injector.ts` in den Chat.

## 3. Globale Invarianten, Host-Umgebung & Ausführungs-Sicherheit

1. **Path Awareness & Monorepo-Struktur:** Aktiver Code liegt ausschließlich in `packages/edge-worker/`, `packages/userscript-hud/`, `packages/local-cli/` und `shared/` (synchronisiert nach Root `gemini-bridge.user.js`). `_v1_backup/` und `cf-gemini-git-bridge/` sind strikt **Read-Only**.
2. **Dateigrößen-Gesetz (`< 500` Zeilen Pflicht):** **300–499 Zeilen** = Warnzone; **ab 500 Zeilen** = sofortige Pflicht-Dekomposition in Submodule (überprüft durch **Probe 10** in [worker-smoke.mjs](file:///y:/projekte2026/website/gitTrans/githubgeminibridge/scripts/patches_and_tests/worker-smoke.mjs) über alle 24 aktiven TypeScript-Quelldateien).
3. **Windows 11 / PowerShell & Single-Action Protocol (`/flash`):**
   - Nutze native Datei-Tools (`view_file`, `replace_file_content`, `write_to_file`); keine Unix-Only CLI-Befehle (`sed`, `awk`, `grep`, `cat`, `rm`, `cp`, `mv`).
   - Führe Shell-Befehle strikt einzeln nacheinander aus (**niemals `&&` oder `;` verketten**).
4. **Decision Gate (Quiet Zone vs. Hard Stops):**
   - *Quiet Zone (Direkt ausführen & berichten):* Read-Only-Analysen, geplante Schritte, `tsc --noEmit`, `worker-smoke.mjs` und lokale Git-Checkpoint-Commits.
   - *Hard Stops (Immer vorher User fragen):* `git push`, `npx wrangler deploy` auf den Live-Worker, History-Rewrites oder Datenlöschung.

File: packages/edge-worker/src/modules/mixer.ts
================
import type { MixRequest, MixResponse } from '../types';
import { parseRepoOwner, getBranchHeadOid, commitSingleFileViaGraphQL } from '../utils/github';
import {
  concatBuffers,
  parseAsciiString,
  parseOctal,
  parseIgnoreLines,
  matchesIgnorePattern,
  DEFAULT_IGNORED_DIR_NAMES,
  DEFAULT_IGNORED_FILES,
  DEFAULT_IGNORED_EXTENSIONS,
} from '../utils/tarUtils';
import { buildChunkedMixXml } from './mixChunker';
import { fetchRecentCommitsAndVolatileFiles } from './recentCommits';

/**
 * Escapes any occurrences of "]]>" inside file content as "]]]]><![CDATA[>"
 * to ensure that XML CDATA sections are never prematurely closed.
 */
export function escapeCdata(content: string): string {
  return content.replace(/\]\]>/g, ']]]]><![CDATA[>');
}

/**
 * Filter decision helper to avoid allocating buffer or string memory for ignored entries.
 * Evaluates TAR entry metadata at the 512-byte header level.
 */
export function shouldSkipTarEntry(
  relPath: string,
  size: number,
  typeflag: string,
  includePaths?: string[]
): { skip: boolean; reason?: 'DIR_OR_NON_FILE' | 'IGNORED_PATH' | 'OVERSIZED' | 'NOT_INCLUDED' } {
  // 1. Directories, extended headers or non-regular files
  // In POSIX/USTAR tar: '0', '', '\0' are regular files. '5' is directory, 'g' PAX global, 'x' PAX extended.
  if (
    typeflag === '5' ||
    typeflag === 'g' ||
    typeflag === 'x' ||
    relPath.endsWith('/') ||
    (typeflag !== '0' && typeflag !== '' && typeflag !== '\0')
  ) {
    return { skip: true, reason: 'DIR_OR_NON_FILE' };
  }

  // 2. Normalize relPath (strip leading / or ./)
  const normalized = relPath.replace(/^\.?\/+/, '');
  if (!normalized) {
    return { skip: true, reason: 'DIR_OR_NON_FILE' };
  }

  const segments = normalized.split('/');
  const filename = segments[segments.length - 1];

  // 3. Ignored directory paths (e.g. .venv, node_modules, dist, .git via DEFAULT_IGNORED_DIR_NAMES)
  for (let i = 0; i < segments.length - 1; i++) {
    const seg = segments[i];
    if (DEFAULT_IGNORED_DIR_NAMES.has(seg) || seg.startsWith('repomix_')) {
      return { skip: true, reason: 'IGNORED_PATH' };
    }
  }

  // 4. Ignored lockfiles, bundle outputs and temporary artifacts
  if (
    DEFAULT_IGNORED_FILES.has(filename) ||
    filename.endsWith('.bundle') ||
    filename.startsWith('repomix-output') ||
    /.*-bundle.*\.txt$/i.test(filename)
  ) {
    return { skip: true, reason: 'IGNORED_PATH' };
  }

  // 5. Ignored binary, media and archive extensions
  const extMatch = filename.match(/\.[a-zA-Z0-9]+$/);
  if (extMatch && DEFAULT_IGNORED_EXTENSIONS.has(extMatch[0].toLowerCase())) {
    return { skip: true, reason: 'IGNORED_PATH' };
  }

  // 6. Whitelist includePaths filter
  if (includePaths && includePaths.length > 0) {
    if (normalized !== '.repomixignore.cf' && normalized !== '.repomixignore') {
      const normalizedIncludes = includePaths.map((p) => p.replace(/^\.?\/+/, ''));
      const isIncluded = normalizedIncludes.some((prefix) => normalized.startsWith(prefix));
      if (!isIncluded) {
        return { skip: true, reason: 'NOT_INCLUDED' };
      }
    }
  }

  // 7. Maximum file size guardrail (512 KB)
  if (size > 524288) {
    return { skip: true, reason: 'OVERSIZED' };
  }

  return { skip: false };
}

/**
 * Extracts a 40-character lowercase hexadecimal SHA from a PAX header payload or comment field.
 */
export function extractHeadOidFromPaxHeader(paxText: string): string | null {
  const match = paxText.match(/(?:^|\n)(?:\d+\s+)?comment=([0-9a-fA-F]{40})(?:\r?\n|$)/) || paxText.match(/\b([0-9a-fA-F]{40})\b/);
  return match ? match[1].toLowerCase() : null;
}


/**
 * Handles POST /api/mix requests.
 * Fetches the repository tarball in a single HTTP subrequest, decompresses it on the fly,
 * filters files in Worker RAM without exceeding 128 MB, and outputs structured XML.
 */
export async function handleMixRequest(
  req: MixRequest,
  githubToken: string
): Promise<MixResponse> {
  let owner = '';
  let repo = '';
  try {
    const parsed = parseRepoOwner(req.repo);
    owner = parsed.owner;
    repo = parsed.repo;
  } catch (err: any) {
    return {
      status: 'ERROR',
      headOid: '',
      xmlContent: '',
      stats: { fileCount: 0, estimatedTokens: 0 },
      errorMessage: err.message || 'Invalid repository format'
    };
  }

  const resolvedBranch = (req.branch || 'main').trim();
  const tarUrl = `https://api.github.com/repos/${encodeURIComponent(owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo)}/tarball/${encodeURIComponent(resolvedBranch)}`;

  const res = await fetch(tarUrl, {
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${githubToken}`,
      Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json',
      'User-Agent': 'Cloudflare-Worker-gemini-git-bridge'
    }
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorBody = await res.text().catch(() => '');
    return {
      status: 'ERROR',
      headOid: '',
      xmlContent: '',
      stats: { fileCount: 0, estimatedTokens: 0 },
      errorMessage: `GitHub tarball fetch failed with HTTP ${res.status} (${res.statusText}): ${errorBody}`
    };
  }

  if (!res.body) {
    return {
      status: 'ERROR',
      headOid: '',
      xmlContent: '',
      stats: { fileCount: 0, estimatedTokens: 0 },
      errorMessage: 'GitHub tarball response body is empty'
    };
  }

  // Stream decompression
  const decompressedStream = res.body.pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('gzip'));
  const reader = decompressedStream.getReader();

  let buffer: Uint8Array<any> = new Uint8Array(0);
  let eof = false;
  let consecutiveZeroBlocks = 0;

  let headOid: string | null = null;
  let nextPaxPath: string | null = null;

  const fileTreeEntries: string[] = [];
  const files: { path: string; content: string }[] = [];
  const dynamicIgnorePatterns: string[] = [];
  const textDecoder = new TextDecoder();

  // Read next chunk into buffer if needed
  async function ensureBytes(count: number): Promise<boolean> {
    while (buffer.length < count) {
      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
      if (done || !value) {
        eof = true;
        return false;
      }
      buffer = concatBuffers(buffer, value);
    }
    return true;
  }

  // Discard bytes from stream without keeping in memory
  async function discardBytes(count: number): Promise<void> {
    if (buffer.length >= count) {
      buffer = buffer.subarray(count);
      return;
    }
    let remaining = count - buffer.length;
    buffer = new Uint8Array(0);
    while (remaining > 0) {
      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
      if (done || !value) {
        eof = true;
        return;
      }
      if (value.length <= remaining) {
        remaining -= value.length;
      } else {
        buffer = value.subarray(remaining);
        remaining = 0;
      }
    }
  }

  try {
    while (!eof) {
      const hasHeader = await ensureBytes(512);
      if (!hasHeader && buffer.length < 512) {
        break;
      }

      const header = buffer.subarray(0, 512);

      // Check for zero block (tar termination)
      let isZero = true;
      for (let i = 0; i < 512; i++) {
        if (header[i] !== 0) {
          isZero = false;
          break;
        }
      }

      if (isZero) {
        consecutiveZeroBlocks++;
        buffer = buffer.subarray(512);
        if (consecutiveZeroBlocks >= 2) {
          // TAR EOF reached
          break;
        }
        continue;
      }
      consecutiveZeroBlocks = 0;

      // Parse TAR Header
      const name = parseAsciiString(header, 0, 100);
      const size = parseOctal(header, 124, 12);
      const typeflagRaw = header[156];
      const typeflag = typeflagRaw === 0 || typeflagRaw === 32 ? '0' : String.fromCharCode(typeflagRaw);
      const prefix = parseAsciiString(header, 345, 155);

      const paddedSize = Math.ceil(size / 512) * 512;

      // Slice out the 512-byte header
      buffer = buffer.subarray(512);

      // 1. PAX Global Extended Header (contains head commit SHA in comment=...)
      if (typeflag === 'g') {
        await ensureBytes(paddedSize);
        const payloadBytes = buffer.subarray(0, size);
        buffer = buffer.subarray(paddedSize);
        const paxText = textDecoder.decode(payloadBytes);
        const extracted = extractHeadOidFromPaxHeader(paxText);
        if (extracted) {
          headOid = extracted;
        }
        continue;
      }

      // 2. PAX Extended Header (overrides path for the next entry)
      if (typeflag === 'x') {
        await ensureBytes(paddedSize);
        const payloadBytes = buffer.subarray(0, size);
        buffer = buffer.subarray(paddedSize);
        const paxText = textDecoder.decode(payloadBytes);
        const match = paxText.match(/(?:^|\n)\d+\s+path=(.+?)(?:\r?\n|$)/);
        if (match) {
          nextPaxPath = match[1].trim();
        }
        continue;
      }

      // Reconstruct entry path
      const rawEntryPath = nextPaxPath || (prefix ? `${prefix}/${name}` : name);
      nextPaxPath = null;

      // Strip top-level repository prefix directory created by GitHub (e.g. "owner-repo-<sha>/")
      const slashIndex = rawEntryPath.indexOf('/');
      if (slashIndex === -1) {
        // Root directory entry or top-level file
        await discardBytes(paddedSize);
        continue;
      }

      const rootPrefix = rawEntryPath.slice(0, slashIndex);
      const relPath = rawEntryPath.slice(slashIndex + 1);

      // Check if rootPrefix ends with 40-character hex SHA
      if (!headOid) {
        const rootMatch = rootPrefix.match(/-([0-9a-fA-F]{40})$/);
        if (rootMatch) {
          headOid = rootMatch[1].toLowerCase();
        }
      }

      if (!relPath || relPath.endsWith('/')) {
        // Directory entry
        await discardBytes(paddedSize);
        continue;
      }

      // Early skip if dynamic ignore patterns already matched
      if (
        dynamicIgnorePatterns.length > 0 &&
        relPath !== '.repomixignore.cf' &&
        relPath !== '.repomixignore' &&
        matchesIgnorePattern(relPath, dynamicIgnorePatterns)
      ) {
        await discardBytes(paddedSize);
        continue;
      }

      // Filter check at header level
      const filterDecision = shouldSkipTarEntry(relPath, size, typeflag, req.includePaths);

      if (filterDecision.skip) {
        if (filterDecision.reason === 'OVERSIZED') {
          fileTreeEntries.push(`${relPath} [SKIPPED: >512KB]`);
        }
        await discardBytes(paddedSize);
        continue;
      }

      // Read file content
      await ensureBytes(paddedSize);
      const fileBytes = buffer.subarray(0, size);
      buffer = buffer.subarray(paddedSize);

      const content = textDecoder.decode(fileBytes);

      // Skip binary files if null bytes are present
      if (content.includes('\0')) {
        continue;
      }

      // Dynamically load ignore rules from .repomixignore.cf or .repomixignore
      if (relPath === '.repomixignore.cf' || relPath === '.repomixignore') {
        const newPatterns = parseIgnoreLines(content);
        if (newPatterns.length > 0) {
          dynamicIgnorePatterns.push(...newPatterns);
          for (let i = files.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            if (matchesIgnorePattern(files[i].path, dynamicIgnorePatterns)) {
              files.splice(i, 1);
            }
          }
          for (let i = fileTreeEntries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const entryPath = fileTreeEntries[i].replace(/\s+\[SKIPPED:.*\]$/, '');
            if (matchesIgnorePattern(entryPath, dynamicIgnorePatterns)) {
              fileTreeEntries.splice(i, 1);
            }
          }
        }
      }

      if (dynamicIgnorePatterns.length > 0 && matchesIgnorePattern(relPath, dynamicIgnorePatterns)) {
        continue;
      }

      fileTreeEntries.push(relPath);
      files.push({ path: relPath, content });
    }
  } catch (err: any) {
    return {
      status: 'ERROR',
      headOid: headOid || '',
      xmlContent: '',
      stats: { fileCount: 0, estimatedTokens: 0 },
      errorMessage: `Error processing tarball stream: ${err.message}`
    };
  } finally {
    try {
      reader.releaseLock();
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }
  }

  // Guarantee 40-character headOid
  if (!headOid || !/^[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$/.test(headOid)) {
    try {
      const branchInfo = await getBranchHeadOid(owner, repo, resolvedBranch, githubToken);
      headOid = branchInfo.headOid;
    } catch (err: any) {
      return {
        status: 'ERROR',
        headOid: '',
        xmlContent: '',
        stats: { fileCount: 0, estimatedTokens: 0 },
        errorMessage: `Failed to resolve 40-character HEAD commit OID: ${err.message}`
      };
    }
  }

  // Fetch recent commits & volatile files if enabled (default: true)
  let recentCommitsText = '';
  let recentCommitsCount = 0;
  let volatileFilesCount = 0;
  if (req.includeRecentCommits !== false) {
    try {
      const commitsResult = await fetchRecentCommitsAndVolatileFiles(
        owner,
        repo,
        resolvedBranch,
        githubToken,
        req.recentCommitsCount || 3
      );
      recentCommitsText = commitsResult.xmlContent;
      recentCommitsCount = commitsResult.commits.length;
      volatileFilesCount = commitsResult.volatileFiles.length;
    } catch {
      // Graceful fallback
    }
  }

  // Partition files and build structured XML representation(s)
  const mixResult = buildChunkedMixXml({
    owner,
    repo,
    branch: resolvedBranch,
    headOid,
    fileTreeEntries,
    files,
    recentCommitsText,
    maxChunkChars: req.maxChunkChars,
    maxChunkBytes: req.maxChunkBytes,
    maxChunkTokens: req.maxChunkTokens,
    splitOutput: req.splitOutput,
  });

  let persistedCommitOid: string | undefined;

  // Persist to GitHub if requested
  if (req.persistToGitHub === true) {
    try {
      const commitRes = await commitSingleFileViaGraphQL({
        repo: `${owner}/${repo}`,
        branch: resolvedBranch,
        expectedHeadOid: headOid,
        path: '_context/repo-mix.xml',
        content: mixResult.xmlContent,
        commitMessage: 'chore(context): update repo-mix.xml snapshot',
        token: githubToken
      });
      persistedCommitOid = commitRes.oid;
      headOid = commitRes.oid;
    } catch (err: any) {
      return {
        status: 'ERROR',
        headOid,
        xmlContent: mixResult.xmlContent,
        parts: mixResult.parts,
        isChunked: mixResult.isChunked,
        stats: {
          ...mixResult.stats,
          ...(recentCommitsCount > 0 ? { recentCommitsCount, volatileFilesCount } : {})
        },
        errorMessage: `Failed to persist repo-mix.xml snapshot to GitHub: ${err.message}`
      };
    }
  }

  return {
    status: 'OK',
    headOid,
    xmlContent: mixResult.xmlContent,
    parts: mixResult.parts,
    isChunked: mixResult.isChunked,
    stats: {
      ...mixResult.stats,
      ...(recentCommitsCount > 0 ? { recentCommitsCount, volatileFilesCount } : {})
    },
    ...(persistedCommitOid ? { persistedCommitOid } : {})
  };
}

import type { HudConfig } from '../types';

const PAT_REGEX = /(?:ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|github_pat_[a-zA-Z0-9_]{30,}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|ghs_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,})/;

/**
 * Hard Guardrail (P1 Komponente 5):
 * Never allow storing a GitHub Personal Access Token in browser storage!
 */
export function sanitizeNoGithubPat(value: string, fieldName = 'value'): string {
  if (!value) return '';
  if (PAT_REGEX.test(value)) {
    throw new Error(
      `[Security Guardrail] Storing a GitHub Personal Access Token in browser storage (${fieldName}) is strictly forbidden!`
    );
  }
  return value.trim();
}

export function getStoredValue(key: string, defaultValue = ''): string {
  try {
    if (typeof GM_getValue === 'function') {
      const val = GM_getValue<string>(key);
      if (val !== undefined && val !== null && val !== '') {
        return sanitizeNoGithubPat(String(val), key);
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`[Gemini Bridge Storage] GM_getValue error for ${key}:`, err);
  }

  try {
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      const local = localStorage.getItem('gemini_bridge_' + key);
      if (local !== null && local !== undefined && local !== '') {
        return sanitizeNoGithubPat(local, key);
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`[Gemini Bridge Storage] localStorage.getItem error for ${key}:`, err);
  }

  return defaultValue;
}

export function sanitizeRepo(repoStr: string): string {
  let cleaned = (repoStr || '').trim();
  cleaned = cleaned.replace(/^https?:\/\/github\.com\//i, '');
  cleaned = cleaned.replace(/^git@github\.com:/i, '');
  cleaned = cleaned.replace(/https?:?$/i, '');
  cleaned = cleaned.replace(/\/+$/, '');
  return cleaned.trim();
}

export function setStoredValue(key: string, value: string): void {
  const safeVal = sanitizeNoGithubPat(value, key);
  try {
    if (typeof GM_setValue === 'function') {
      GM_setValue(key, safeVal);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`[Gemini Bridge Storage] GM_setValue error for ${key}:`, err);
  }

  try {
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.setItem('gemini_bridge_' + key, safeVal);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`[Gemini Bridge Storage] localStorage.setItem error for ${key}:`, err);
  }
}

export const DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG: HudConfig = {
  workerUrl: 'https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev',
  workerSecret: 'fc1bec3f781b59dee2e576650460f417269fd44d3b0e39d77ae18480c270524f',
  repo: 'alexanderpeschkaGit/Portfolio_VJ_Brave_V2_0',
  branch: 'preview',
  targetBranch: '',
  includePaths: [],
  autoPilot: false,
  persistToGitHub: false,
  maxChunkChars: 85000,
  includeRecentCommits: true,
};

export function loadHudConfig(): HudConfig {
  const workerUrl = getStoredValue('worker_url', DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.workerUrl);
  const workerSecret = getStoredValue('worker_secret', DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.workerSecret);
  const repo = sanitizeRepo(getStoredValue('owner_repo', DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.repo));
  const branch = getStoredValue('branch', DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.branch);
  const targetBranch = getStoredValue('target_branch', DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.targetBranch || '');
  const autoPilotStr = getStoredValue('auto_pilot', 'false');
  const persistStr = getStoredValue('persist_to_github', 'false');
  const includePathsStr = getStoredValue('include_paths', '');
  const includeRecentCommitsStr = getStoredValue('include_recent_commits', 'true');

  let includePaths: string[] = [];
  if (includePathsStr) {
    try {
      includePaths = JSON.parse(includePathsStr);
      if (!Array.isArray(includePaths)) includePaths = [];
    } catch {
      includePaths = includePathsStr.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    }
  }

  const maxChunkCharsStr = getStoredValue('max_chunk_chars', '85000');
  const parsedChars = parseInt(maxChunkCharsStr, 10);
  const maxChunkChars = Number.isFinite(parsedChars) && parsedChars >= 10000 && parsedChars <= 2000000 ? parsedChars : 85000;

  return {
    workerUrl: workerUrl.replace(/\/+$/, ''),
    workerSecret,
    repo,
    branch,
    targetBranch,
    includePaths,
    autoPilot: autoPilotStr === 'true',
    persistToGitHub: persistStr === 'true',
    maxChunkChars,
    includeRecentCommits: includeRecentCommitsStr === 'true',
  };
}

export function saveHudConfig(cfg: Partial<HudConfig>): HudConfig {
  const current = loadHudConfig();
  const updated: HudConfig = { ...current, ...cfg };

  if (cfg.workerUrl !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('worker_url', sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.workerUrl, 'workerUrl'));
  }
  if (cfg.workerSecret !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('worker_secret', sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.workerSecret, 'workerSecret'));
  }
  if (cfg.repo !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('owner_repo', sanitizeRepo(sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.repo, 'repo')));
  }
  if (cfg.branch !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('branch', sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.branch, 'branch'));
  }
  if (cfg.targetBranch !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('target_branch', sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.targetBranch || '', 'targetBranch'));
  }
  if (cfg.autoPilot !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('auto_pilot', updated.autoPilot ? 'true' : 'false');
  }
  if (cfg.persistToGitHub !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('persist_to_github', updated.persistToGitHub ? 'true' : 'false');
  }
  if (cfg.includePaths !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('include_paths', JSON.stringify(updated.includePaths));
  }
  if (cfg.maxChunkChars !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('max_chunk_chars', String(updated.maxChunkChars));
  }
  if (cfg.includeRecentCommits !== undefined) {
    setStoredValue('include_recent_commits', updated.includeRecentCommits ? 'true' : 'false');
  }

  return updated;
}

// ==========================================
// 1. WORKER ENVIRONMENT & AUTH (V2 + V1 Compatible)
// ==========================================
export interface WorkerEnv {
  GITHUB_PAT: string;       // Hinterlegt via `wrangler secret put GITHUB_PAT`
  GITHUB_TOKEN: string;     // V1 Legacy Alias für GITHUB_PAT
  WORKER_SECRET: string;    // Shared Bearer Secret zwischen Userscript und Worker
  BRIDGE_SECRET: string;    // V1 Legacy Alias für WORKER_SECRET
}

export type Env = WorkerEnv;

// ==========================================
// 2. UNTERTOOL A: MIXER (/api/mix)
// ==========================================
export interface MixPart {
  partIndex: number;          // 1-basierter Index (z.B. 1, 2, 3)
  totalParts: number;         // Gesamtanzahl der Chunks
  fileName: string;           // z.B. "repo-mix.part1.xml"
  xmlContent: string;         // Vollständiges, valides XML für diesen Part
  stats: {
    fileCount: number;
    estimatedTokens: number;
    charCount: number;
  };
}

export interface MixRequest {
  repo: string;              // Format: "owner/repository"
  branch: string;            // z.B. "main"
  includePaths?: string[];   // Optionaler Subpfad-Filter (z.B. ["src/", "shared/"])
  persistToGitHub?: boolean; // Speichert Mix optional nach /_context/repo-mix.xml
  maxChunkChars?: number;    // Maximale Zeichenanzahl pro Part (Standard: 85_000, unterhalb 100k Gemini-Limit)
  maxChunkBytes?: number;    // Maximale Byte-Größe pro Part (Standard: 512_000 ~500 KB)
  maxChunkTokens?: number;   // Maximale geschätzte Tokens pro Part (Standard: 125_000)
  splitOutput?: boolean;     // Ob Chunking aktiviert werden soll (Standard: true wenn Budget überschritten)
  includeRecentCommits?: boolean; // Whether to fetch & include recent commit metadata and volatile file summaries
  recentCommitsCount?: number;   // Number of recent commits to analyze (default: 3, max: 5)
}

export interface MixResponse {
  status: "OK" | "ERROR";
  headOid: string;           // Voller 40-stelliger Commit-SHA als Basis für Folgebefehle
  xmlContent: string;        // Strukturierter XML-Kontext (Part 1 oder Gesamtmix für Abwärtskompatibilität)
  parts?: MixPart[];         // Aufgeteilte Parts für Multi-File Ingestion bei großen Repos
  isChunked?: boolean;       // Zeigt an, ob der Output in mehrere Parts gesplittet wurde
  stats: {
    fileCount: number;
    estimatedTokens: number;
    charCount?: number;
    recentCommitsCount?: number;
    volatileFilesCount?: number;
  };
  persistedCommitOid?: string; // Falls persistToGitHub === true ausgeführt wurde
  errorMessage?: string;       // Fehlerbeschreibung bei status === "ERROR"
}

// ==========================================
// 3. UNTERTOOL B & C: PATCHER & LINTER (/api/patch)
// ==========================================
export interface PatchBlock {
  search: string;
  replace: string;
}

export interface FilePatchOperation {
  path: string;
  action: "modify" | "create" | "delete";
  blocks?: PatchBlock[];     // Pflicht bei action === "modify"
  fullContent?: string;      // Pflicht bei action === "create" (optional bei "modify")
}

export interface GeminiPatchPayload {
  commitMessage: string;
  preflight?: {
    forbidPatterns?: string[];                 // RegExp-Strings, z.B. ["console\\.log", ": any"]
    requireExports?: Record<string, string[]>; // z.B. { "src/auth.ts": ["useAuth"] }
  };
  patches: FilePatchOperation[];
}

export interface WorkerPatchRequest {
  repo: string;
  branch: string;
  expectedHeadOid: string;     // 40-stelliger Git-SHA für Optimistic Concurrency Control
  payload: GeminiPatchPayload;
  targetBranch?: string;          // Optional: abweichender Zielbranch (z.B. "feature/xyz" oder "gemini/patch-1")
  createBranchIfNeeded?: boolean; // Default true: legt targetBranch an, falls nicht vorhanden
}

export interface LinterError {
  file: string;
  stage: "SEARCH_REPLACE" | "OXC_SYNTAX" | "CUSTOM_RULE";
  line?: number;
  column?: number;
  message: string;
  codeSnippet?: string;
}

export interface WorkerPatchResponse {
  status: "COMMITTED" | "PREFLIGHT_FAILED" | "GIT_CONFLICT" | "NO_CHANGES_NEEDED" | "ERROR";
  newHeadOid?: string;         // Neuer 40-stelliger Commit-SHA nach erfolgreichem Commit
  commitUrl?: string;
  errors?: LinterError[];      // Aggregierte Fehlerliste für Auto-Retry in Gemini
  errorMessage?: string;
  targetBranch?: string;          // Der tatsächlich verwendete Zielbranch
  createdBranch?: boolean;        // True, falls der Branch frisch erzeugt wurde
  pullRequestUrl?: string;        // GitHub Compare/PR-Link (z.B. https://github.com/owner/repo/compare/base...target?expand=1)
}

// ==========================================
// 4. V1 LEGACY COMPATIBILITY INTERFACES (/push, /commit)
// ==========================================
export type PatchItem = PatchBlock;

export interface FileChangeInput {
  path: string;
  content?: string;
  search?: string;
  replace?: string;
  patches?: PatchItem[];
  diff?: string;
}

export interface FileDeletionInput {
  path: string;
}

export interface CommitRequest {
  owner?: string;
  repo: string; // e.g. "alexanderpeschkaGit/Portfolio_VJ_Brave_V2_0" or "Portfolio_VJ_Brave_V2_0"
  branch?: string;
  baseBranch?: string;
  targetBranch?: string;
  message?: string;
  commitMessage?: string;
  files?: FileChangeInput[];
  changes?: FileChangeInput[];
  deletions?: (string | FileDeletionInput)[];
}

export interface GitHubCommitResult {
  oid: string;
  url: string;
  messageHeadline: string;
}

// ==========================================
export * from './hybridTypes';

File: .agents/rules/userscript-shadow-hud.md
