trigger: model_decision
description: "Apply this rule whenever working on packages/userscript-hud/ or gemini-bridge.user.js (DOM injection, harvesting, Shadow DOM UI, Tampermonkey grants)."
---

# Tampermonkey Userscript & Shadow-DOM HUD (`packages/userscript-hud/`)

> **Historischer Kontext:** Verbindet die bewährten DOM-Scanner-, Diff-Parser- und `GM_*`-Storage-Mechanismen aus `_v1_backup/gemini-bridge.user.js` mit der neuen React Shadow-DOM HUD-Architektur (Stufe 3 bis 6.3).

---

## 1. Host-Kompatibilität & Sicherheits-Grenzen

1. **Dual-Target Support (`@match`):**
   - Das Userscript muss zwingend auf beiden Plattformen funktionieren:
     - `https://gemini.google.com/*`
     - `https://aistudio.google.com/*`
2. **Strikte Secret-Trennung:**
   - Im Userscript (`GM_setValue` / `localStorage`) darf **niemals** ein GitHub Personal Access Token (`GITHUB_PAT` / `GITHUB_TOKEN`) gespeichert werden.
   - Es werden ausschließlich die Worker-URL (`https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev`), das `WORKER_SECRET` (bzw. `BRIDGE_SECRET`), das Ziel-Repository (`owner/repo`) und der Ziel-Branch gespeichert.
3. **CSP-Bypass via `GM_xmlhttpRequest`:**
   - Da `gemini.google.com` und `aistudio.google.com` strikte Content Security Policies (`connect-src`) erzwingen, müssen sämtliche HTTP-Aufrufe zum Cloudflare Worker über `GM_xmlhttpRequest` (bzw. `GM.xmlHttpRequest`) mit `@connect *.workers.dev` erfolgen – niemals über ungekapseltes Browser-`fetch()`.

---

## 2. UI-Kapselung, Trusted-Types-Sicherheit & DOM-Interaktion

1. **Verpflichtendes Shadow DOM (`attachShadow`) & Trusted-Types-Konformität:**
   - Mounte niemals Komponenten oder globale Stylesheets direkt in `document.body` der Host-Seite (Gefahr von CSS-Leaks und Angular/Material-Konflikten in Gemini/AI Studio).
   - Erzeuge einen dedizierten Host-Container `#gemini-edge-hud-root` und mounte die gesamte HUD-UI + Styles innerhalb von `attachShadow({ mode: 'open' })`.
   - **Trusted-Types-Pflicht (`require-trusted-types-for 'script'`):** Da `gemini.google.com` und `aistudio.google.com` strikte Trusted-Types-Policies erzwingen, darf im Userscript niemals ungeschütztes `element.innerHTML = ...` verwendet werden. Nutze ausschließlich sichere DOM-APIs (`document.createElement`, `textContent`, `appendChild`) oder eine gekapselte `trustedTypes.createPolicy`-Brücke. Die Styles und Trusted-Types DOM-Bausteine sind modular in `src/ui/hudStylesAndDom.ts` gekapselt und werden von `src/ui/shadowHud.ts` gesteuert.
2. **DOM-Injector (`src/dom/injector.ts`):**
   - **Kontext-Upload (Multi-Part `.txt`-Chunks):** Konstruiere `File`-Objekte für Multi-Part `repo-mix.part1.txt`, `repo-mix.part2.txt`, … (MIME `text/plain` zur Vermeidung von XML-Parsing-Bugs) via `injectRepoMixParts`, verpacke sie gemeinsam in ein `DataTransfer`-Objekt und dispatche ein synthetisches `ClipboardEvent('paste', { clipboardData: dt, bubbles: true, cancelable: true })` auf das aktive Eingabefeld (`div[contenteditable="true"]` bzw. `textarea`). Gemini / AI Studio erzeugt dadurch saubere Datei-Chips nebeneinander.
   - **Scope-Filter & Chunk-Größe (`includePaths`, `maxChunkChars`):** Im HUD-Drawer (`hudStylesAndDom.ts`) können optional ein kommagetrennter Subpfad-Filter (`src/, packages/edge-worker/`) sowie das Zeichenbudget `maxChunkChars` (Standard: 85.000, unterhalb des 100k-Gemini-Limits) konfiguriert werden, die an `MixRequest` übergeben werden.
   - **Prompt-Text-Injektion:** Nutze `document.execCommand('insertText', false, promptText)` bzw. native `InputEvent`-Dispatches auf dem fokussierten Editor-Element, damit der interne State von Gemini/AI Studio die Eingabe zuverlässig registriert. Siehe auch [REPOMIX_INSTRUCTIONS.md](../../REPOMIX_INSTRUCTIONS.md) als Referenz für Prompt-Leitplanken.
3. **DOM-Harvester (`src/dom/harvester.ts`):**
   - Überwache neu generierte LLM-Antwortblöcke per `MutationObserver` (mit Debounce/Streaming-Completion-Erkennung).
   - Extrahiere sowohl strukturierte V2 `GeminiPatchPayload`-JSON-Codeblöcke als auch klassische `<<<<<<< SEARCH ... >>>>>>> REPLACE`-Blöcke (V1-Fallback aus `_v1_backup/gemini-bridge.user.js` inkl. automatischer Konvertierung in `GeminiPatchPayload`).
4. **Queue-Management & Sequenzieller FIFO Fail-Fast (Stufe 6.3):**
   - **Queue-Header:** `hudStylesAndDom.ts` rendert über der Patch-Kartenliste eine Flex-Leiste mit zwei Aktions-Buttons:
     - `⚡ Apply All (${patchCount})` (`hud-btn-primary`)
     - `🗑️ Clear` (`hud-btn-secondary`)
   - **FIFO-Abarbeitung:** Da neu geerntete Patches per `unshift` an den Anfang gelegt werden, kehrt `handleApplyAllPatches` die Liste chronologisch um (`[...state.harvestedPatches].reverse()`), um die Patches in der Reihenfolge ihrer Erzeugung anzuwenden.
   - **Fail-Fast & Concurrency-Schutz:** Bricht sofort ab, sobald ein Patch fehlschlägt (`PREFLIGHT_FAILED`, `GIT_CONFLICT` oder `ERROR`), damit Folge-Patches nicht auf inkonsistenten Commits aufbauen.
   - **State-Pruning:** Erfolgreich committete oder idempotente Patches (`COMMITTED`, `NO_CHANGES_NEEDED`) werden nach jedem Schritt automatisch aus `state.harvestedPatches` entfernt; die OID-Kette (`headOid`) wird atomar fortgeschrieben.
5. **Storage & Secret Guardrail (`src/services/storage.ts`):**
   - Striktes Verbot von GitHub PATs via `sanitizeNoGithubPat` (Hard Guardrail `P1`).
   - Abwärtskompatible V1-Schlüssel via `getStoredValue` / `setStoredValue` (`gemini_bridge_` Fallback).
   - Konfiguration über `loadHudConfig` und `saveHudConfig` (`HudConfig`), inklusive optionalem `targetBranch` für isoliertes Committen auf neue/abweichende Zielbranches.
6. **Worker Client & CSP-Bypass (`src/services/workerClient.ts`):**
   - Kapselung aller Worker-Aufrufe über `gmRequestJson` (`GM_xmlhttpRequest` / `GM.xmlHttpRequest` mit `fetch`-Fallback).
   - Endpunkte: `checkWorkerHealth` (`GET /health`), `requestRepoMix` (`POST /api/mix`), `requestWorkerPatch` (`POST /api/patch`), `requestLegacyCommit` (`POST /push`).
7. **Optimistic Concurrency (`headOid` State-Machine) & Self-Healing Loop (`src/services/selfHealing.ts`):**
   - Speichere die von `/api/mix` gelieferte 40-stellige `headOid` im HUD-State (`mountShadowHud`).
   - Nach jedem erfolgreichen `/api/patch`-Commit (`status === "COMMITTED"`) aktualisiere `headOid` (falls auf den aktiven Mix-Branch committet wurde) sofort auf `response.newHeadOid` und setze `retryCount = 0`. Zeige bei Commits auf abweichende Branches den Zielbranch und den GitHub Compare/PR-Link (`pullRequestUrl`) im Status an.
   - Bei `status === "PREFLIGHT_FAILED"` und aktiviertem Auto-Pilot injiziere automatisch einen strukturierten `#solve`-Korrektur-Prompt mit der aggregierten `errors: LinterError[]`-Liste (maximal `retryCount < 3`, danach automatischer Stopp und User-Hinweis).
8. **Build-Pipeline (`packages/userscript-hud/scripts/build-userscript.mjs`):**
   - Bündelt TypeScript-Quellcode (`src/index.ts`) via `esbuild` in ein valides Tampermonkey-Userscript mit allen Grants (`@grant GM_xmlhttpRequest`, `@grant GM_setValue`, etc.).
   - Erzeugt `packages/userscript-hud/dist/gemini-bridge.user.js` und synchronisiert automatisch mit Root `gemini-bridge.user.js`.

================
File: packages/userscript-hud/src/ui/hudStylesAndDom.ts
================

export const HUD_SHADOW_STYLES = HUD_STYLES;

export interface HudDomElements {
  host: HTMLDivElement;
  shadowRoot: ShadowRoot;
  trigger: HTMLDivElement;
  triggerBadge: HTMLSpanElement;
  panel: HTMLDivElement;
  closeBtn: HTMLButtonElement;
  repoInput: HTMLInputElement;
  branchInput: HTMLInputElement;
  targetBranchInput: HTMLInputElement;
  secretInput: HTMLInputElement;
  includePathsInput: HTMLInputElement;
  maxChunkCharsInput: HTMLInputElement;
  oidBadge: HTMLDivElement;
  mixBtn: HTMLButtonElement;
  autoPilotCheck: HTMLInputElement;
  recentCommitsCheck: HTMLInputElement;
  retryIndicator: HTMLSpanElement;
  applyAllBtn: HTMLButtonElement;
  clearQueueBtn: HTMLButtonElement;
  patchQueueEl: HTMLDivElement;
  emptyQueueMsg: HTMLDivElement;
  statusBox: HTMLDivElement;
}

export function createHudElement<K extends keyof HTMLElementTagNameMap>(
  tag: K,
  className?: string,
  textContent?: string
): HTMLElementTagNameMap[K] {
  const el = document.createElement(tag);
  if (className) el.className = className;
  if (textContent !== undefined) el.textContent = textContent;
  return el;
}

export function buildHudDomTree(config: HudConfig, state: HudState): HudDomElements {
  const existing = document.getElementById('gemini-edge-hud-root');
  if (existing) {
    existing.remove();
  }

  const host = createHudElement('div');
  host.id = 'gemini-edge-hud-root';
  host.style.cssText = 'all: initial; position: fixed; z-index: 2147483647;';
  document.body.appendChild(host);

  const shadowRoot = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });

  const styleEl = createHudElement('style');
  styleEl.textContent = HUD_STYLES;
  shadowRoot.appendChild(styleEl);

  // Trigger button
  const trigger = createHudElement('div', 'hud-trigger');
  const triggerIcon = createHudElement('span', undefined, '⚡');
  const triggerLabel = createHudElement('span', undefined, 'Gemini Edge HUD');
  const triggerBadge = createHudElement('span', 'hud-trigger-badge idle', 'IDLE');
  trigger.appendChild(triggerIcon);
  trigger.appendChild(triggerLabel);
  trigger.appendChild(triggerBadge);
  shadowRoot.appendChild(trigger);

  // Drawer Panel
  const panel = createHudElement('div', 'hud-panel');
  const header = createHudElement('div', 'hud-header');
  const title = createHudElement('div', 'hud-title', '⚡ Cloudflare Edge HUD (Stage 3)');
  const closeBtn = createHudElement('button', 'hud-close-btn', '×');
  closeBtn.title = 'Close Panel';
  header.appendChild(title);
  header.appendChild(closeBtn);
  panel.appendChild(header);

  // Body
  const body = createHudElement('div', 'hud-body');

  // Repo Row
  const repoRow = createHudElement('div', 'hud-form-row');
  const repoLabel = createHudElement('span', 'hud-label', 'GitHub Repository (owner/repo)');
  const repoInput = createHudElement('input', 'hud-input');
  repoInput.value = config.repo;
  repoRow.appendChild(repoLabel);
  repoRow.appendChild(repoInput);
  body.appendChild(repoRow);

  // Branch Row
  const branchRow = createHudElement('div', 'hud-form-row');
  const branchLabel = createHudElement('span', 'hud-label', 'Branch');
  const branchInput = createHudElement('input', 'hud-input');
  branchInput.value = config.branch;
  branchRow.appendChild(branchLabel);
  branchRow.appendChild(branchInput);
  body.appendChild(branchRow);

  // Target Branch Row (Optional, for writing to a different or new branch)
  const targetBranchRow = createHudElement('div', 'hud-form-row');
  const targetBranchLabel = createHudElement('span', 'hud-label', 'Target Branch (optional)');
  const targetBranchInput = createHudElement('input', 'hud-input');
  targetBranchInput.placeholder = 'Same as mix branch (or e.g. preview, feature/...)';
  targetBranchInput.value = config.targetBranch || '';
  targetBranchRow.appendChild(targetBranchLabel);
  targetBranchRow.appendChild(targetBranchInput);
  body.appendChild(targetBranchRow);

  // Include Paths Row (Scope Filter for large repos)
  const pathsRow = createHudElement('div', 'hud-form-row');
  const pathsLabel = createHudElement('span', 'hud-label', 'Include Paths (comma-separated, optional)');
  const includePathsInput = createHudElement('input', 'hud-input');
  includePathsInput.placeholder = 'e.g. src/, packages/edge-worker/';
  includePathsInput.value = (config.includePaths || []).join(', ');
  pathsRow.appendChild(pathsLabel);
  pathsRow.appendChild(includePathsInput);
  body.appendChild(pathsRow);

  // Max Chunk Chars Row (Gemini Chat 85k budget)
  const maxChunkRow = createHudElement('div', 'hud-form-row');
  const maxChunkLabel = createHudElement('span', 'hud-label', 'Max Chunk Chars (Default: 85000, Max 10 Parts)');
  const maxChunkCharsInput = createHudElement('input', 'hud-input');
  maxChunkCharsInput.type = 'number';
  maxChunkCharsInput.min = '10000';
  maxChunkCharsInput.max = '2000000';
  maxChunkCharsInput.value = String(config.maxChunkChars || 85000);
  maxChunkRow.appendChild(maxChunkLabel);
  maxChunkRow.appendChild(maxChunkCharsInput);
  body.appendChild(maxChunkRow);

  // Worker Secret Row
  const secretRow = createHudElement('div', 'hud-form-row');
  const secretLabel = createHudElement('span', 'hud-label', 'Worker Secret (WORKER_SECRET)');
  const secretInput = createHudElement('input', 'hud-input');
  secretInput.type = 'password';
  secretInput.value = config.workerSecret;
  secretRow.appendChild(secretLabel);
  secretRow.appendChild(secretInput);
  body.appendChild(secretRow);

  // Head OID Badge Row
  const oidRow = createHudElement('div', 'hud-form-row');
  const oidLabel = createHudElement('span', 'hud-label', 'Head OID (Optimistic Concurrency)');
  const oidBadge = createHudElement('div', 'hud-badge-oid', state.headOid || '(Not mixed yet)');
  oidRow.appendChild(oidLabel);
  oidRow.appendChild(oidBadge);
  body.appendChild(oidRow);

  // Action Buttons Row: Mix Repo
  const actionRow = createHudElement('div');
  actionRow.style.cssText = 'display: flex; gap: 8px;';
  const mixBtn = createHudElement('button', 'hud-btn hud-btn-primary', '📦 Load Repo Mix (/api/mix)');
  mixBtn.style.flex = '1';
  actionRow.appendChild(mixBtn);
  body.appendChild(actionRow);

  // Options Row: Auto-Pilot toggle
  const optRow = createHudElement('div');
  optRow.style.cssText = 'display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;';
  const autoPilotLabel = createHudElement('label', 'hud-toggle-label');
  const autoPilotCheck = createHudElement('input');
  autoPilotCheck.type = 'checkbox';
  autoPilotCheck.checked = config.autoPilot;
  const autoPilotText = createHudElement('span', undefined, '🤖 Auto-Pilot Self-Healing');
  autoPilotLabel.appendChild(autoPilotCheck);
  autoPilotLabel.appendChild(autoPilotText);

  const retryIndicator = createHudElement('span', undefined, `Retries: ${state.retryCount}/3`);
  retryIndicator.style.cssText = 'font-size: 11px; color: #94a3b8;';
  optRow.appendChild(autoPilotLabel);
  optRow.appendChild(retryIndicator);
  body.appendChild(optRow);

  // Commits & Volatile Files Row
  const commitsRow = createHudElement('div');
  commitsRow.style.cssText = 'display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; margin-top: 4px;';
  const recentCommitsLabel = createHudElement('label', 'hud-toggle-label');
  const recentCommitsCheck = createHudElement('input');
  recentCommitsCheck.type = 'checkbox';
  recentCommitsCheck.checked = config.includeRecentCommits !== false;
  const recentCommitsText = createHudElement('span', undefined, '🕒 Volatile Dateien & Letzte Commits');
  recentCommitsLabel.appendChild(recentCommitsCheck);
  recentCommitsLabel.appendChild(recentCommitsText);
  commitsRow.appendChild(recentCommitsLabel);
  body.appendChild(commitsRow);

  // Patch Queue Header Row
  const queueHeaderRow = createHudElement('div');
  queueHeaderRow.style.cssText = 'display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; margin-top: 4px;';
  const queueLabel = createHudElement('span', 'hud-label', 'Harvested Patches');

  const queueActions = createHudElement('div');
  queueActions.style.cssText = 'display: flex; gap: 6px;';

  const applyAllBtn = createHudElement('button', 'hud-btn hud-btn-primary', '⚡ Apply All');
  applyAllBtn.style.cssText = 'padding: 3px 8px; font-size: 11px;';

  const clearQueueBtn = createHudElement('button', 'hud-btn hud-btn-secondary', '🗑️ Clear');
  clearQueueBtn.style.cssText = 'padding: 3px 8px; font-size: 11px;';

  queueActions.appendChild(applyAllBtn);
  queueActions.appendChild(clearQueueBtn);
  queueHeaderRow.appendChild(queueLabel);
  queueHeaderRow.appendChild(queueActions);
  body.appendChild(queueHeaderRow);

  const patchQueueEl = createHudElement('div', 'hud-patch-queue');
  const emptyQueueMsg = createHudElement('div', undefined, 'No patches harvested from chat yet.');
  emptyQueueMsg.style.cssText = 'color: #64748b; font-size: 11px; text-align: center; padding: 6px;';
  patchQueueEl.appendChild(emptyQueueMsg);
  body.appendChild(patchQueueEl);

  // Status & Error Box
  const statusBox = createHudElement('div', 'hud-status-box idle', state.statusMessage);
  body.appendChild(statusBox);

  panel.appendChild(body);
  shadowRoot.appendChild(panel);

  return {
    host,
    shadowRoot,
    trigger,
    triggerBadge,
    panel,
    closeBtn,
    repoInput,
    branchInput,
    targetBranchInput,
    secretInput,
    includePathsInput,
    maxChunkCharsInput,
    oidBadge,
    mixBtn,
    autoPilotCheck,
    recentCommitsCheck,
    retryIndicator,
    applyAllBtn,
    clearQueueBtn,
    patchQueueEl,
    emptyQueueMsg,
    statusBox
  };
}

================
File: .agents/rules/edge-worker-architecture.md
================
---
trigger: model_decision
description: "Apply this rule whenever working on packages/edge-worker/, shared/types.ts, wrangler.jsonc, GitHub GraphQL/Tarball integration, or V1/V2 API endpoints."
---

# Cloudflare Edge-Worker Architecture (`packages/edge-worker/`)

> **Historischer Kontext:** Diese Domänen-Regel bündelt die ursprünglichen V1-Worker-Spezifikationen (`cf-gemini-git-bridge/` aus der früheren Root-`agents.md`) mit der neuen V2-Orchestrator-Architektur (`mixer.ts`, `patcher.ts`, `linter.ts`).
> **WICHTIG:** Der aktive Quellcode liegt ausschließlich in `packages/edge-worker/`. Das Verzeichnis `cf-gemini-git-bridge/` im Projekt-Root und `_v1_backup/` sind **Read-Only Legacy-Bestand** und dürfen nicht bearbeitet werden.

---

## 1. Runtime & Deployment Constraints

- **Worker Name & Wrangler Config:**
  - `packages/edge-worker/wrangler.jsonc` behält zwingend `"name": "gemini-git-bridge"` und `"compatibility_date": "2026-08-04"` (oder neuer), damit bestehende Cloudflare-Deployments und Secrets ohne Re-Provisionierung weiterlaufen.
- **Zero Local State & Pure Web Standards:**
  - Der Worker läuft zustandslos an der Cloudflare Edge. Keine Node.js-Dateisystem-Zugriffe (`fs`), keine SQLite-Datenbanken, kein persistenter In-Memory-State zwischen Requests.
  - Nutze native Web-Standard-APIs (`fetch`, `Request`, `Response`, `Headers`, `ReadableStream`, `DecompressionStream('gzip')`, `TextDecoder`, `TextEncoder`).
- **Dual Secret & Header Support (V1 + V2 Abwärtskompatibilität):**
  - **GitHub Token:** Unterstütze `env.GITHUB_PAT || env.GITHUB_TOKEN`.
  - **Worker Secret:** Unterstütze `env.WORKER_SECRET || env.BRIDGE_SECRET`.
  - **Request Auth-Header:** Akzeptiere sowohl `Authorization: Bearer <SECRET>` als auch den Legacy-Header `x-bridge-secret: <SECRET>`.

---

## 2. API Route & Synchronization Table (V1 Legacy + V2 Orchestrator)

Alle Routen in `packages/edge-worker/src/index.ts` müssen CORS-Header (`Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`, `Access-Control-Allow-Headers: Content-Type, Authorization, x-bridge-secret`) unterstützen und folgende Verträge strikt einhalten:

| Endpoint | Method | Auth | Ursprung | Modul / Handler | Request / Response Vertrag |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `/` oder `/health` | `GET` | Public | V1 + V2 | `index.ts` | Gibt `{ status: "ok", service: "gemini-git-bridge", version: "2.0.0", hasBridgeSecret: boolean, hasGithubToken: boolean }` zurück (niemals Klartext-Secrets!). |
| `/api/mix` | `POST` | Bearer / `x-bridge-secret` | **V2 (Stufe 1, 6–6.2)** | `modules/mixer.ts` + `modules/mixChunker.ts` | Body: `MixRequest` (`repo`, `branch`, `includePaths?`, `persistToGitHub?`, `maxChunkBytes?`, `maxChunkTokens?`, `splitOutput?`). Streamt GitHub-Tarball (1 Subrequest), filtert im RAM und liefert `MixResponse` (`headOid`, `xmlContent`, `parts?: MixPart[]` als `.txt` / `text/plain`, `isChunked?`, `stats`). |
| `/api/patch` | `POST` | Bearer / `x-bridge-secret` | **V2 (Stufe 2, 4 & 7)** | `modules/patcher.ts` + `modules/linter.ts` | Body: `WorkerPatchRequest` (`repo`, `branch`, `expectedHeadOid`, `payload: GeminiPatchPayload`, `targetBranch?`, `createBranchIfNeeded?`). Führt Concurrency-Check auf `branch` aus, batch-readet Dateien, prüft Search/Replace, Placeholder & AST/Syntax, erzeugt bei abweichendem `targetBranch` automatisch den Ref via `createBranchRef` an `expectedHeadOid` und committet atomar. Liefert `WorkerPatchResponse` (`COMMITTED` inkl. `targetBranch`, `createdBranch`, `pullRequestUrl`, `PREFLIGHT_FAILED`, `GIT_CONFLICT`, `NO_CHANGES_NEEDED`, `ERROR`). |
| `/push`, `/commit`, `/api/commit`, `/` | `POST` | Bearer / `x-bridge-secret` | **V1 Legacy** | `modules/patcher.ts` (`handleCommitRequest`) | Body: `CommitRequestPayload` (`repo`, `targetBranch`, `commitMessage`, `changes[]`, `deletions[]`, `expectedHeadOid?`). Muss zu 100 % abwärtskompatibel für bestehende V1-Clients bleiben! |
| `/graphql` | `POST` | Bearer / `x-bridge-secret` | **V1 + V2** | `index.ts` (`handleGraphQLProxy`) | Body: `{ query: string, variables?: Record<string, unknown> }`. Authentifizierter Proxy zur GitHub GraphQL API v4. |
| `/api/webhook/github` | `POST` | HMAC-SHA256 (`x-hub-signature-256`) | **V2 (Stufe 5)** | `modules/webhookHandler.ts` | Body: GitHub Webhook JSON. Validiert HMAC per Web Crypto API, filtert `@gemini`-Trigger, liefert sofort `202 Accepted` und führt `executeWorkerWorkflow` (Mixer + Gemini REST + Patcher + PR/Issue Comment) asynchron via `ctx.waitUntil` aus. |

---

## 3. Technische Modul-Gesetze (Stufe 1, 2, 4, 5 & 6)

### A. Untertool A: `src/modules/mixer.ts` (1-Subrequest Tarball-Stream + Repomix-Filterlogik)
1. **1-Subrequest Tarball Streaming (`uithub`-Pattern):**
   - Lade das Repository in einem einzigen HTTP-Subrequest via `GET https://api.github.com/repos/{owner}/{repo}/tarball/{branch}` und dekomprimiere es on-the-fly mit `response.body.pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('gzip'))`.
   - **Verboten:** Einzelabrufe über die GitHub Contents API in einer Schleife (sprengt das Limit von 50 Subrequests auf Cloudflare Workers).
2. **Exakte 40-stellige `headOid` Extraktion & Persist-Synchronisation:**
   - Lies im ersten 512-Byte-Block des TAR-Streams (`typeflag === 'g'`, PAX Global Extended Header) das Kommentarfeld `comment=<40-char-sha>` aus. Nutze das Hash-Suffix des Root-Ordnernamens als sekundären Pfad und führe bei verkürztem SHA (< 40 Zeichen) automatisch `getBranchHeadOid` als Fallback aus, damit `headOid` garantiert immer 40-stellig ist.
   - Wird `persistToGitHub === true` genutzt (`commitSingleFileViaGraphQL` nach `_context/repo-mix.xml`), wird `headOid` in der `MixResponse` auf die neue `persistedCommitOid` aktualisiert, damit nachfolgende `/api/patch`-Aufrufe keinen `GIT_CONFLICT` auslösen.
3. **USTAR & PAX Extended Path Support:**
   - Unterstütze Dateipfade > 100 Zeichen verlustfrei über das USTAR-`prefix`-Feld (Offset 345, Länge 155) sowie PAX Extended Header (`typeflag === 'x'`, `path=...`).
4. **128 MB RAM-Überlaufschutz & Repomix-Filter (Header-Level Stream-Skip):**
   - Überspringe folgende Pfade und Dateitypen **bereits anhand des 512-Byte-TAR-Headers** direkt im Stream (ohne Buffer-/String-Allokation) über `shouldSkipTarEntry`:
     - **Verzeichnisse:** `.git/`, `node_modules/`, `dist/`, `build/`, `.wrangler/`, `.turbo/`, `.next/`, `coverage/`, `_v1_backup/`, `repomix_*/`, `backup_unused/`, `.venv/`, `venv/`, `__pycache__/`.
     - **Lockfiles & Bundles:** `package-lock.json`, `yarn.lock`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `bun.lockb`, `*.bundle`, `repomix-output*`, `*-bundle*.txt`.
     - **Binär- & Medien-Endungen:** `.png`, `.jpg`, `.jpeg`, `.gif`, `.webp`, `.avif`, `.ico`, `.svg`, `.mp4`, `.webm`, `.mov`, `.mp3`, `.wav`, `.ogg`, `.woff`, `.woff2`, `.ttf`, `.eot`, `.pdf`, `.zip`, `.tar`, `.gz`, `.7z`, `.wasm`, `.exe`, `.dll`, `.so`, `.dylib`, `.pyc`.
     - **Dynamische Ignore-Regeln:** Unterstütze `.repomixignore.cf` bzw. `.repomixignore` direkt aus dem TAR-Stream via `tarUtils.ts`.
     - **Größen-Limit:** Einzeldateien `> 512 KB` (`524288` Bytes) werden im Stream übersprungen (und im `<file_tree>` mit `[SKIPPED: >512KB]` vermerkt).
5. **Format-Migration auf `.txt` (Repomix-kompatibel, MIME `text/plain`):**
   - Um XML-Parsing-Bugs und UI-Wrapping in Gemini / AI Studio zu verhindern, werden Chunks standardmäßig als `.txt`-Dateien mit MIME `text/plain` (`part-01.txt`, `part-02.txt` usw.) erzeugt.
   - Escape vorhandene `]]>`-Sequenzen im Dateiinhalt sicher als `]]]]><![CDATA[>` (`escapeCdata`), falls XML-Wrapping verwendet wird.
6. **RepoMix Atomic Chunking & Multi-Part Partitioning (`mixChunker.ts`):**
   - Wird `splitOutput: true` übergeben oder überschreitet das Repository das Budget (`maxChunkChars` Standard 85.000 Zeichen unterhalb des 100k-Gemini-Limits), partitioniert `buildChunkedMixFiles` die Dateien **atomar** in nummerierte Chunks (`part 1 von N`).
   - **Gemini Web UI 100k Cutoff-Schutz:** Jedes generierte `MixPart.textContent.length` bleibt strikt `<= 100_000` Zeichen (Target-Budget: `DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_CHARS = 85_000` Zeichen). Einzeldateien `> 100_000` Zeichen werden im Stream übersprungen und im `<file_tree>` mit `[SKIPPED: >100k chars for Gemini Chat limit]` markiert; Dateien zwischen 85k und 100k Zeichen erhalten einen isolierten Einzelpart.
   - **10-Parts-Guardrail:** Die Anzahl der Parts wird auf maximal 10 begrenzt (`MAX_GEMINI_PARTS = 10`), um Browser- und Clipboard-Überläufe zu verhindern.
   - Part 1 enthält den vollständigen Repository `<file_tree>` (bis max. 14k Zeichen gedeckelt), Folgeparts `2..N` einen kompakten Teilbaum nur für die im jeweiligen Part enthaltenen Dateien.
   - **Invariante:** Einzelne Dateien werden **niemals** mitten im Quellcode zerschnitten. Jeder Part ist ein in sich geschlossenes Dokument mit Metadaten und Filetree.
   - Low-Level TAR-Header-Parsing (`concatBuffers`, `parseAsciiString`, `parseOctal`) ist in `src/utils/tarUtils.ts` ausgelagert, um das `< 500`-Zeilen-Gesetz für `mixer.ts` strikt einzuhalten.

### B. Untertool B: `src/modules/patcher.ts` & `src/modules/patchInMemory.ts` & `src/utils/github.ts` (Stufe 2)
1. **1-Subrequest GraphQL Batch-Read:**
   - Nutze `fetchMultipleFilesViaGraphQL` in `src/utils/github.ts`, um alle zu patchenden Dateien über dynamische GraphQL-Aliases (`f0: object(expression: $exp_0) { ... on Blob { text isTruncated } }`) in **einem einzigen** Subrequest zu laden.
2. **Multi-Strategy Search/Replace (`applySearchReplace`):**
   - Behalte die 3-stufige Matching-Kaskade aus V1 bei:
     1. Exakter Substring-Match,
     2. Zeilenenden-normalisierter Match (`\r\n` <-> `\n`),
     3. Whitespace-toleranter Zeilen-Match (`trimEnd()` / `trim()`).
3. **Hard Guardrail (`validateNoPlaceholders`) & Non-Fail-Fast Aggregation:**
   - Prüfe jeden `replace`-Block und jeden `fullContent`-Block gegen `LLM_PLACEHOLDER_PATTERNS`. Bei Treffern oder nicht auflösbaren `search`-Blöcken wird **nicht** beim ersten Fehler abgebrochen, sondern über alle Dateien und Blöcke hinweg im Array `errors: LinterError[]` (`stage: "SEARCH_REPLACE"` bzw. `"CUSTOM_RULE"`) gesammelt.
4. **Idempotenz, Concurrency & `PreflightLinterHook`:**
   - Prüfe vorab `expectedHeadOid` gegen `getBranchHeadOid`; weicht der Branch-HEAD ab, gib sofort `status: "GIT_CONFLICT"` zurück.
   - Biete in `handlePatchRequest` einen typisierten Hook (`PreflightLinterHook`) sowie Basis-Prüfungen für `preflight.forbidPatterns` / `preflight.requireExports`, in den sich Stufe 4 (`linter.ts`) ohne Refactoring von `patcher.ts` einklinkt.
   - Die in-Memory-Verarbeitung und Preflight-Auswertung ist in `src/modules/patchInMemory.ts` gekapselt und bleibt über `patcher.ts` zu 100 % re-exportiert.
   - Ergibt das Patchen exakt denselben Inhalt wie auf GitHub (`additions.length === 0 && deletions.length === 0`), gib `status: "NO_CHANGES_NEEDED"` zurück.

5. **Target Branch & Atomic Branch Creation (`createBranchRef`, Stufe 7):**
   - Unterstützt optionalen abweichenden `targetBranch` in `WorkerPatchRequest`.
   - Concurrency Check erfolgt strikt auf dem Basisbranch (`req.branch`) gegen `req.expectedHeadOid`.
   - Existiert der `targetBranch` noch nicht auf GitHub und ist `createBranchIfNeeded !== false`, wird er atomar an `req.expectedHeadOid` via GraphQL `createRef` (oder REST-Fallback) erzeugt.
   - Der Commit wird auf `targetBranch` ausgeführt und liefert `WorkerPatchResponse` mit `targetBranch`, `createdBranch: boolean` und optionalem `pullRequestUrl` (GitHub Compare/PR-Link).

### C. Untertool C: `src/modules/linter.ts` & `src/modules/syntaxScanner.ts` (Stufe 4)
1. **Kein Fail-Fast (Fehler-Aggregation):**
   - Brich niemals beim ersten fehlgeschlagenen Search-Block oder Syntaxfehler ab. Sammle alle Fehler aus allen Dateien im Array `errors: LinterError[]` (`SEARCH_REPLACE`, `OXC_SYNTAX`, `CUSTOM_RULE`), damit Gemini alle Probleme in einem einzigen Korrektur-Turn beheben kann.
2. **Syntax- & AST-Prüfung (`validateSyntaxInWorker`):**
   - Deterministischer Lexer & Klammer-/Struktur-Verifier (< 5 ms Laufzeit im Worker-RAM) für `.ts`, `.tsx`, `.js`, `.jsx`, `.mjs`, `.cjs` sowie `JSON.parse` für `.json` (gekapselt in `src/modules/syntaxScanner.ts`).
   - Erkennt nicht geschlossene Strings/Comments/Templates, Bracket-Mismatches und verbliebene Git-Merge-Conflict-Marker (`<<<<<<<`, `=======`, `>>>>>>>`).
   - Unterstützt optionalen pluggbaren `registerOxcWasmParser` Hook.
3. **Automatischer Hook (`runPreflightLinter`):**
   - Fungiert als Standard-`preflightHook` in `applyPatchesInMemory` und `handlePatchRequest`, aggregiert Syntax- und Custom-Rules (`validateNoPlaceholders`, `forbidPatterns`, `requireExports`) und dedupliziert identische Fehler-Einträge.

### D. Untertool D: `src/modules/webhookHandler.ts` (Stufe 5 Hybrid Routing & Webhook)
1. **Zero Node.js Imports:**
   - Strikt reine Web-Standard-APIs (`crypto.subtle`, `fetch`, `Request`, `Response`, `TextEncoder`). Absolutes Verbot von Node.js-Modulen (`fs`, `child_process`, `simple-git`, `path`) im Worker-Kontext.
2. **Pure Web Crypto HMAC-SHA256 Signatur-Verifikation:**
   - Validiert den GitHub-Header `x-hub-signature-256` über `crypto.subtle.importKey` und `crypto.subtle.verify` (Timing-Attack-sicher).
3. **Sofortiges 202 Accepted & Hintergrund-Verarbeitung (`ctx.waitUntil`):**
   - Um den strikten 10-Sekunden-Timeout von GitHub-Webhooks einzuhalten, antwortet der Worker sofort mit `202 Accepted` und delegiert `executeWorkerWorkflow` (Mixer -> Gemini REST -> Patcher -> PR/Issue-Feedback-Kommentar) an `ctx.waitUntil`.

### E. Validierung vor Abschluss:
- Jede Änderung an `packages/edge-worker/` erfordert zwingend `tsc --noEmit` sowie einen erfolgreichen Lauf von `node scripts/patches_and_tests/worker-smoke.mjs` (inkl. aller 12 Probes mit harter `< 500`-Zeilen-Bedingung über alle aktiven TypeScript-Quelldateien).

================
File: packages/userscript-hud/src/ui/shadowHud.ts
================
import type {
  HudConfig,
  HudState,
  HarvestedPatch,
  WorkerPatchRequest,
} from '../types';
import {
  loadHudConfig,
  saveHudConfig,
  sanitizeNoGithubPat,
  getStoredValue,
  setStoredValue
} from '../services/storage';
import {
  requestRepoMix,
  requestWorkerPatch,
  requestLegacyCommit
} from '../services/workerClient';
import { injectRepoMixFile, injectRepoMixParts, injectPromptText } from '../dom/injector';
import {
  executeSelfHealingTurn,
  buildSelfHealingPrompt,
  MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES,
} from '../services/selfHealing';
import {
  HUD_STYLES,
  HUD_SHADOW_STYLES,
  createHudElement,
  buildHudDomTree,
  type HudDomElements
} from './hudStylesAndDom';

export {
  HUD_STYLES,
  HUD_SHADOW_STYLES,
  createHudElement,
  buildHudDomTree,
  type HudDomElements
};

export interface ShadowHudController {
  getState: () => HudState;
  updateState: (partial: Partial<HudState>) => void;
  openPanel: () => void;
  closePanel: () => void;
  handleApplyPatch: (patch: HarvestedPatch) => Promise<boolean>;
  addHarvestedPatch: (patch: HarvestedPatch) => void;
}

export function mountShadowHud(onApplyPatchCustom?: (patch: HarvestedPatch) => Promise<boolean>): ShadowHudController {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') {
    throw new Error('mountShadowHud requires a DOM environment');
  }

  const config = loadHudConfig();
  const state: HudState = {
    headOid: getStoredValue('head_oid', ''),
    lastMixStats: null,
    status: 'IDLE',
    statusMessage: 'Ready. Load Repo Mix or commit patches.',
    retryCount: 0,
    lastErrors: [],
    harvestedPatches: [],
  };

  const dom = buildHudDomTree(config, state);

  // Helper to re-render UI state
  function renderState() {
    dom.triggerBadge.className = `hud-trigger-badge ${state.status.toLowerCase()}`;
    dom.triggerBadge.textContent = state.status;

    if (state.headOid) {
      dom.oidBadge.textContent = state.headOid;
      dom.oidBadge.style.color = '#6ee7b7';
    } else {
      dom.oidBadge.textContent = '(Not mixed yet)';
      dom.oidBadge.style.color = '#94a3b8';
    }

    dom.retryIndicator.textContent = `Retries: ${state.retryCount}/3`;

    const statusClasses: Record<string, string> = {
      ERROR: 'error', PREFLIGHT_FAILED: 'error', GIT_CONFLICT: 'error',
      COMMITTED: 'success', MIXING: 'info', PATCHING: 'info'
    };
    dom.statusBox.className = `hud-status-box ${statusClasses[state.status] || 'idle'}`;

    // Render status and any linter errors cleanly without innerHTML
    dom.statusBox.textContent = '';
    const mainMsg = createHudElement('div', undefined, state.statusMessage);
    dom.statusBox.appendChild(mainMsg);

    if (state.lastErrors && state.lastErrors.length > 0) {
      const errList = createHudElement('ul');
      errList.style.cssText = 'margin: 6px 0 0 16px; padding: 0; font-size: 11px;';
      state.lastErrors.forEach((err) => {
        const li = createHudElement('li', undefined, `[${err.stage}] ${err.file}: ${err.message}`);
        errList.appendChild(li);
      });
      dom.statusBox.appendChild(errList);

      const healBtn = createHudElement('button', 'hud-btn hud-btn-secondary', '🔧 Send Self-Healing Prompt to Chat');
      healBtn.style.cssText =
        'margin-top: 8px; width: 100%; font-size: 11px; padding: 5px 8px; border-color: #38bdf8; color: #38bdf8;';
      healBtn.onclick = () => {
        const nextRetry = (state.retryCount || 0) + 1;
        const prompt = buildSelfHealingPrompt(
          state.lastErrors,
          config.repo,
          config.branch,
          state.headOid,
          nextRetry
        );
        injectPromptText(prompt, false);
        controller.updateState({
          retryCount: nextRetry,
          statusMessage: `🔧 Injected self-healing prompt (Turn ${nextRetry}/${MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES}) into chat.`,
        });
      };
      dom.statusBox.appendChild(healBtn);
    }

    // Update Apply All and Clear buttons
    const patchCount = state.harvestedPatches.length;
    const isBusy = state.status === 'PATCHING' || state.status === 'MIXING';
    dom.applyAllBtn.disabled = patchCount === 0 || isBusy;
    dom.applyAllBtn.textContent = patchCount > 0 ? `⚡ Apply All (${patchCount})` : '⚡ Apply All';
    dom.clearQueueBtn.disabled = patchCount === 0 || isBusy;

    // Render Patch Queue
    dom.patchQueueEl.textContent = '';
    if (state.harvestedPatches.length === 0) {
      dom.patchQueueEl.appendChild(dom.emptyQueueMsg);
    } else {
      state.harvestedPatches.forEach((patch) => {
        const item = createHudElement('div', 'hud-patch-item');
        const itemTitle = createHudElement(
          'div',
          'hud-patch-item-title',
          `${patch.mode === 'v2_json' ? '⚡' : '📝'} ${patch.commitMessage}`
        );
        itemTitle.title = patch.commitMessage;

        const applyBtn = createHudElement('button', 'hud-btn hud-btn-primary', 'Apply');
        applyBtn.style.cssText = 'padding: 3px 8px; font-size: 11px;';
        applyBtn.disabled = isBusy;
        applyBtn.onclick = () => {
          controller.handleApplyPatch(patch);
        };

        item.appendChild(itemTitle);
        item.appendChild(applyBtn);
        dom.patchQueueEl.appendChild(item);
      });
    }
  }

  // Toggle Panel
  const openPanel = () => {
    dom.panel.classList.add('open');
  };
  const closePanel = () => {
    dom.panel.classList.remove('open');
  };

  dom.trigger.onclick = () => {
    if (dom.panel.classList.contains('open')) {
      closePanel();
    } else {
      openPanel();
    }
  };
  dom.closeBtn.onclick = closePanel;

  // Input persistence
  dom.repoInput.onchange = () => {
    try {
      const sanitized = sanitizeNoGithubPat(dom.repoInput.value.trim(), 'repo');
      config.repo = sanitized;
      saveHudConfig({ repo: sanitized });
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e.message);
      dom.repoInput.value = config.repo;
    }
  };

  dom.branchInput.onchange = () => {
    try {
      const sanitized = sanitizeNoGithubPat(dom.branchInput.value.trim(), 'branch');
      config.branch = sanitized;
      saveHudConfig({ branch: sanitized });
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e.message);
      dom.branchInput.value = config.branch;
    }
  };

  dom.targetBranchInput.onchange = () => {
    try {
      const sanitized = sanitizeNoGithubPat(dom.targetBranchInput.value.trim(), 'targetBranch');
      config.targetBranch = sanitized;
      saveHudConfig({ targetBranch: sanitized });
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e.message);
      dom.targetBranchInput.value = config.targetBranch || '';
    }
  };

  dom.includePathsInput.onchange = () => {
    try {
      const raw = dom.includePathsInput.value.trim();
      const paths = raw.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      config.includePaths = paths;
      saveHudConfig({ includePaths: paths });
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e.message);
      dom.includePathsInput.value = (config.includePaths || []).join(', ');
    }
  };

  dom.maxChunkCharsInput.onchange = () => {
    try {
      const val = parseInt(dom.maxChunkCharsInput.value, 10);
      const safe = Math.max(10000, Math.min(2000000, Number.isFinite(val) ? val : 85000));
      config.maxChunkChars = safe;
      dom.maxChunkCharsInput.value = String(safe);
      saveHudConfig({ maxChunkChars: safe });
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e.message);
      dom.maxChunkCharsInput.value = String(config.maxChunkChars || 85000);
    }
  };

  dom.secretInput.onchange = () => {
    try {
      const sanitized = sanitizeNoGithubPat(dom.secretInput.value.trim(), 'workerSecret');
      config.workerSecret = sanitized;
      saveHudConfig({ workerSecret: sanitized });
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e.message);
      dom.secretInput.value = config.workerSecret;
    }
  };

  dom.autoPilotCheck.onchange = () => {
    config.autoPilot = dom.autoPilotCheck.checked;
    saveHudConfig({ autoPilot: config.autoPilot });
  };

  dom.recentCommitsCheck.onchange = () => {
    config.includeRecentCommits = dom.recentCommitsCheck.checked;
    saveHudConfig({ includeRecentCommits: config.includeRecentCommits });
  };

  // Mix Repo Action
  dom.mixBtn.onclick = async () => {
    try {
      dom.mixBtn.disabled = true;
      dom.mixBtn.style.opacity = '0.6';
      controller.updateState({
        status: 'MIXING',
        statusMessage: `⏳ Streaming repo mix for ${config.repo} (${config.branch})...`,
      });

      const res = await requestRepoMix(config);
      if (res.status === 'OK' && (res.parts?.length || res.xmlContent)) {
        state.headOid = res.headOid;
        state.lastMixStats = res.stats;
        setStoredValue('head_oid', res.headOid);

        let injected = false;
        const metaCommits = res.stats.recentCommitsCount
          ? ` (incl. ${res.stats.recentCommitsCount} recent commits, ${res.stats.volatileFilesCount ?? 0} volatile files)`
          : '';

        if (res.parts && res.parts.length > 1) {
          const partBreakdown = res.parts
            .map((p) => `P${p.partIndex}: ${(p.stats.charCount ?? p.xmlContent.length).toLocaleString()} chars`)
            .join(', ');
          injected = injectRepoMixParts(
            res.parts.map((p) => ({ fileName: p.fileName, xmlContent: p.xmlContent }))
          );
          controller.updateState({
            status: 'IDLE',
            statusMessage: `✅ Loaded ${res.parts.length} parts (${res.stats.fileCount} files${metaCommits}, ~${res.stats.estimatedTokens} tokens) [${partBreakdown}]. Head: ${res.headOid.slice(0, 7)}${injected ? ' (Injected into chat prompt)' : ''}`,
          });
        } else {
          injected = injectRepoMixFile(res.xmlContent);
          const charLen = res.stats.charCount ?? res.xmlContent.length;
          controller.updateState({
            status: 'IDLE',
            statusMessage: `✅ Loaded ${res.stats.fileCount} files${metaCommits} (${charLen.toLocaleString()} chars, ~${res.stats.estimatedTokens} tokens). Head: ${res.headOid.slice(0, 7)}${injected ? ' (Injected into chat prompt)' : ''}`,
          });
        }
      } else {
        controller.updateState({
          status: 'ERROR',
          statusMessage: `❌ Mix error: ${res.errorMessage || 'Unknown error'}`,
        });
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      controller.updateState({
        status: 'ERROR',
        statusMessage: `❌ Mix error: ${err.message || String(err)}`,
      });
    } finally {
      dom.mixBtn.disabled = false;
      dom.mixBtn.style.opacity = '1';
    }
  };

  // Apply Patch Action
  const handleApplyPatch = async (patch: HarvestedPatch): Promise<boolean> => {
    if (onApplyPatchCustom) {
      return onApplyPatchCustom(patch);
    }

    controller.openPanel();
    controller.updateState({
      status: 'PATCHING',
      statusMessage: `⏳ Applying patch: "${patch.commitMessage}"...`,
    });

    try {
      if (state.headOid) {
        const patchReq: WorkerPatchRequest = {
          repo: config.repo,
          branch: config.branch,
          expectedHeadOid: state.headOid,
          payload: patch.payload,
          targetBranch: config.targetBranch?.trim() || undefined,
        };

        const res = await requestWorkerPatch(config, patchReq);
        if (res.status === 'COMMITTED') {
          state.harvestedPatches = state.harvestedPatches.filter((p) => p.id !== patch.id);
          const branchLabel = res.targetBranch || config.branch;
          if (res.newHeadOid && (!res.targetBranch || res.targetBranch === config.branch)) {
            state.headOid = res.newHeadOid;
            setStoredValue('head_oid', res.newHeadOid);
          }
          const prMsg = res.pullRequestUrl ? ` | 🔗 PR: ${res.pullRequestUrl}` : '';
          controller.updateState({
            status: 'COMMITTED',
            statusMessage: `✅ Committed to ${branchLabel}: ${res.newHeadOid ? res.newHeadOid.slice(0, 7) : 'OK'} (${patch.commitMessage})${prMsg}`,
            retryCount: 0,
            lastErrors: [],
          });
          return true;
        }

        if (res.status === 'PREFLIGHT_FAILED') {
          const selfHealingResult = executeSelfHealingTurn(res, config, state, true);
          if (selfHealingResult.triggered) {
            controller.updateState({
              status: 'PREFLIGHT_FAILED',
              statusMessage: `⚠️ Preflight failed. 🤖 Auto-Pilot self-healing prompt injected (Turn ${selfHealingResult.nextRetryCount}/${MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES}).`,
              retryCount: selfHealingResult.nextRetryCount,
              lastErrors: res.errors || [],
            });
          } else {
            const reasonMsg =
              selfHealingResult.reason === 'MAX_RETRIES_EXCEEDED'
                ? `Max retries (${MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES}) reached.`
                : 'Auto-pilot disabled.';
            controller.updateState({
              status: 'PREFLIGHT_FAILED',
              statusMessage: `❌ Preflight failed. ${reasonMsg} Review errors below or trigger manual retry.`,
              retryCount: selfHealingResult.nextRetryCount,
              lastErrors: res.errors || [],
            });
          }
          return false;
        }

        if (res.status === 'GIT_CONFLICT' || res.status === 'ERROR') {
          controller.updateState({
            status: res.status,
            statusMessage: `❌ ${res.status}: ${res.errorMessage || 'Preflight / Concurrency check failed'}`,
            lastErrors: res.errors || [],
          });
          return false;
        }

        if (res.status === 'NO_CHANGES_NEEDED') {
          state.harvestedPatches = state.harvestedPatches.filter((p) => p.id !== patch.id);
          controller.updateState({
            status: 'IDLE',
            statusMessage: `ℹ️ No changes needed (idempotent patch).`,
          });
          return true;
        }
      }

      if (patch.legacyPayload) {
        controller.updateState({
          status: 'PATCHING',
          statusMessage: `⏳ Submitting legacy commit to /push...`,
        });
        const [owner, repoName] = config.repo.split('/');
        const legacyReq = {
          ...patch.legacyPayload,
          owner: owner || undefined,
          repo: repoName || config.repo,
          branch: config.branch,
        };
        const res = await requestLegacyCommit(config, legacyReq);
        state.harvestedPatches = state.harvestedPatches.filter((p) => p.id !== patch.id);
        controller.updateState({
          status: 'COMMITTED',
          statusMessage: `✅ Committed via legacy bridge: ${res?.commit?.oid ? res.commit.oid.slice(0, 7) : 'OK'}`,
          retryCount: 0,
          lastErrors: [],
        });
        return true;
      }

      throw new Error('No headOid set and no legacy payload available. Please click "Load Repo Mix" first.');
    } catch (err: any) {
      controller.updateState({
        status: 'ERROR',
        statusMessage: `❌ Patch error: ${err.message || String(err)}`,
      });
      return false;
    }
  };

  dom.clearQueueBtn.onclick = () => {
    state.harvestedPatches = [];
    renderState();
  };

  dom.applyAllBtn.onclick = async () => {
    if (state.harvestedPatches.length === 0 || state.status === 'PATCHING' || state.status === 'MIXING') return;

    // FIFO: Reverse queue so oldest (chronologically first) is applied first!
    const queue = [...state.harvestedPatches].reverse();
    const total = queue.length;
    let successCount = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
      const patch = queue[i];
      controller.updateState({
        status: 'PATCHING',
        statusMessage: `⏳ [${i + 1}/${total}] Applying patch: "${patch.commitMessage}"...`,
      });

      const success = await handleApplyPatch(patch);
      if (!success) {
        // Fail-Fast: Hard stop on first error!
        return;
      }
      successCount++;
    }

    controller.updateState({
      status: 'COMMITTED',
      statusMessage: `🎉 All ${successCount} patches successfully applied and committed!`,
    });
  };

  const controller: ShadowHudController = {
    getState: () => ({ ...state }),
    updateState: (partial: Partial<HudState>) => {
      Object.assign(state, partial);
      renderState();
    },
    openPanel,
    closePanel,
    handleApplyPatch,
    addHarvestedPatch: (patch: HarvestedPatch) => {
      if (!state.harvestedPatches.some((p) => p.id === patch.id)) {
        state.harvestedPatches.unshift(patch);
        if (state.harvestedPatches.length > 20) {
          state.harvestedPatches.pop();
        }
        renderState();
      }
    },
  };

  renderState();
  return controller;
}

// --- Trusted Types (TrustedHTML) compatibility for Google Gemini & AI Studio ---
if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.trustedTypes && typeof window.trustedTypes.createPolicy === 'function') {
  try {
    window.trustedTypes.createPolicy('default', {
      createHTML: (string) => string,
      createScript: (string) => string,
      createScriptURL: (string) => string,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    try {
      window.trustedTypes.createPolicy('geminiBridgePolicy', {
        createHTML: (string) => string,
      });
    } catch (e2) {
      // Ignored if policy already exists
    }
  }
}

"use strict";
(() => {
  // packages/userscript-hud/src/services/storage.ts
  var PAT_REGEX = /(?:ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|github_pat_[a-zA-Z0-9_]{30,}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|ghs_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,})/;
  function sanitizeNoGithubPat(value, fieldName = "value") {
    if (!value) return "";
    if (PAT_REGEX.test(value)) {
      throw new Error(
        `[Security Guardrail] Storing a GitHub Personal Access Token in browser storage (${fieldName}) is strictly forbidden!`
      );
    }
    return value.trim();
  }
  function getStoredValue(key, defaultValue = "") {
    try {
      if (typeof GM_getValue === "function") {
        const val = GM_getValue(key);
        if (val !== void 0 && val !== null && val !== "") {
          return sanitizeNoGithubPat(String(val), key);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`[Gemini Bridge Storage] GM_getValue error for ${key}:`, err);
    }
    try {
      if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") {
        const local = localStorage.getItem("gemini_bridge_" + key);
        if (local !== null && local !== void 0 && local !== "") {
          return sanitizeNoGithubPat(local, key);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`[Gemini Bridge Storage] localStorage.getItem error for ${key}:`, err);
    }
    return defaultValue;
  }
  function sanitizeRepo(repoStr) {
    let cleaned = (repoStr || "").trim();
    cleaned = cleaned.replace(/^https?:\/\/github\.com\//i, "");
    cleaned = cleaned.replace(/^git@github\.com:/i, "");
    cleaned = cleaned.replace(/https?:?$/i, "");
    cleaned = cleaned.replace(/\/+$/, "");
    return cleaned.trim();
  }
  function setStoredValue(key, value) {
    const safeVal = sanitizeNoGithubPat(value, key);
    try {
      if (typeof GM_setValue === "function") {
        GM_setValue(key, safeVal);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`[Gemini Bridge Storage] GM_setValue error for ${key}:`, err);
    }
    try {
      if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") {
        localStorage.setItem("gemini_bridge_" + key, safeVal);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`[Gemini Bridge Storage] localStorage.setItem error for ${key}:`, err);
    }
  }
  var DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG = {
    workerUrl: "https://gemini-git-bridge.sikanda.workers.dev",
    workerSecret: "fc1bec3f781b59dee2e576650460f417269fd44d3b0e39d77ae18480c270524f",
    repo: "alexanderpeschkaGit/Portfolio_VJ_Brave_V2_0",
    branch: "preview",
    targetBranch: "",
    includePaths: [],
    autoPilot: false,
    persistToGitHub: false,
    maxChunkChars: 85e3,
    includeRecentCommits: true
  };
  function loadHudConfig() {
    const workerUrl = getStoredValue("worker_url", DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.workerUrl);
    const workerSecret = getStoredValue("worker_secret", DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.workerSecret);
    const repo = sanitizeRepo(getStoredValue("owner_repo", DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.repo));
    const branch = getStoredValue("branch", DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.branch);
    const targetBranch = getStoredValue("target_branch", DEFAULT_HUD_CONFIG.targetBranch || "");
    const autoPilotStr = getStoredValue("auto_pilot", "false");
    const persistStr = getStoredValue("persist_to_github", "false");
    const includePathsStr = getStoredValue("include_paths", "");
    const includeRecentCommitsStr = getStoredValue("include_recent_commits", "true");
    let includePaths = [];
    if (includePathsStr) {
      try {
        includePaths = JSON.parse(includePathsStr);
        if (!Array.isArray(includePaths)) includePaths = [];
      } catch {
        includePaths = includePathsStr.split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      }
    }
    const maxChunkCharsStr = getStoredValue("max_chunk_chars", "85000");
    const parsedChars = parseInt(maxChunkCharsStr, 10);
    const maxChunkChars = Number.isFinite(parsedChars) && parsedChars >= 1e4 && parsedChars <= 2e6 ? parsedChars : 85e3;
    return {
      workerUrl: workerUrl.replace(/\/+$/, ""),
      workerSecret,
      repo,
      branch,
      targetBranch,
      includePaths,
      autoPilot: autoPilotStr === "true",
      persistToGitHub: persistStr === "true",
      maxChunkChars,
      includeRecentCommits: includeRecentCommitsStr === "true"
    };
  }
  function saveHudConfig(cfg) {
    const current = loadHudConfig();
    const updated = { ...current, ...cfg };
    if (cfg.workerUrl !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("worker_url", sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.workerUrl, "workerUrl"));
    }
    if (cfg.workerSecret !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("worker_secret", sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.workerSecret, "workerSecret"));
    }
    if (cfg.repo !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("owner_repo", sanitizeRepo(sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.repo, "repo")));
    }
    if (cfg.branch !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("branch", sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.branch, "branch"));
    }
    if (cfg.targetBranch !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("target_branch", sanitizeNoGithubPat(updated.targetBranch || "", "targetBranch"));
    }
    if (cfg.autoPilot !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("auto_pilot", updated.autoPilot ? "true" : "false");
    }
    if (cfg.persistToGitHub !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("persist_to_github", updated.persistToGitHub ? "true" : "false");
    }
    if (cfg.includePaths !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("include_paths", JSON.stringify(updated.includePaths));
    }
    if (cfg.maxChunkChars !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("max_chunk_chars", String(updated.maxChunkChars));
    }
    if (cfg.includeRecentCommits !== void 0) {
      setStoredValue("include_recent_commits", updated.includeRecentCommits ? "true" : "false");
    }
    return updated;
  }

  // packages/userscript-hud/src/services/workerClient.ts
  async function gmRequestJson(url, method, secret, body) {
    const headers = {
      "Accept": "application/json",
      "Authorization": `Bearer ${secret}`,
      "x-bridge-secret": secret
    };
    let payloadString;
    if (body !== void 0) {
      headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
      payloadString = typeof body === "string" ? body : JSON.stringify(body);
    }
    if (typeof GM_xmlhttpRequest === "function") {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        GM_xmlhttpRequest({
          method,
          url,
          headers,
          data: payloadString,
          onload: (res) => {
            try {
              const data = JSON.parse(res.responseText);
              if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 300) {
                resolve(data);
              } else {
                const errMsg = data?.error || data?.errorMessage || `HTTP ${res.status}: ${res.responseText}`;
                reject(new Error(errMsg));
              }
            } catch (err) {
              if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 300) {
                resolve(res.responseText);
              } else {
                reject(new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}: ${res.responseText}`));
              }
            }
          },
          onerror: (err) => {
            reject(new Error(`Network error calling Cloudflare Worker: ${err?.statusText || err || "Unknown error"}`));
          },
          ontimeout: () => {
            reject(new Error("Request to Cloudflare Worker timed out"));
          }
        });
      });
    }
    if (typeof GM !== "undefined" && typeof GM.xmlHttpRequest === "function") {
      const res = await GM.xmlHttpRequest({
        method,
        url,
        headers,
        data: payloadString
      });
      try {
        const data = JSON.parse(res.responseText);
        if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 300) {
          return data;
        }
        throw new Error(data?.error || data?.errorMessage || `HTTP ${res.status}: ${res.responseText}`);
      } catch (e) {
        if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 300) {
          return res.responseText;
        }
        throw e;
      }
    }
    if (typeof fetch === "function") {
      const res = await fetch(url, {
        method,
        headers,
        body: payloadString
      });
      const text = await res.text();
      let data;
      try {
        data = JSON.parse(text);
      } catch {
        data = text;
      }
      if (res.ok) {
        return data;
      }
      throw new Error(data?.error || data?.errorMessage || `HTTP ${res.status}: ${text}`);
    }
    throw new Error("No supported HTTP client found (GM_xmlhttpRequest, GM.xmlHttpRequest, or fetch)");
  }
  async function checkWorkerHealth(cfg) {
    const url = `${cfg.workerUrl}/health`;
    return gmRequestJson(url, "GET", cfg.workerSecret);
  }
  async function requestRepoMix(cfg, req) {
    const url = `${cfg.workerUrl}/api/mix`;
    const payload = {
      repo: req?.repo || cfg.repo,
      branch: req?.branch || cfg.branch,
      includePaths: req?.includePaths || (cfg.includePaths.length > 0 ? cfg.includePaths : void 0),
      persistToGitHub: req?.persistToGitHub ?? cfg.persistToGitHub,
      maxChunkChars: req?.maxChunkChars ?? cfg.maxChunkChars,
      maxChunkBytes: req?.maxChunkBytes,
      maxChunkTokens: req?.maxChunkTokens,
      splitOutput: req?.splitOutput,
      includeRecentCommits: req?.includeRecentCommits ?? cfg.includeRecentCommits
    };
    return gmRequestJson(url, "POST", cfg.workerSecret, payload);
  }
  async function requestWorkerPatch(cfg, req) {
    const url = `${cfg.workerUrl}/api/patch`;
    return gmRequestJson(url, "POST", cfg.workerSecret, req);
  }
  async function requestLegacyCommit(cfg, req) {
    const url = `${cfg.workerUrl}/push`;
    return gmRequestJson(url, "POST", cfg.workerSecret, req);
  }

  // packages/userscript-hud/src/dom/injector.ts
  function findPromptInputElement() {
    if (typeof document === "undefined") return null;
    const geminiSelectors = [
      'rich-textarea div[contenteditable="true"]',
      'div.ql-editor[contenteditable="true"]',
      'div[contenteditable="true"][role="textbox"]',
      'div[contenteditable="true"]'
    ];
    for (const sel of geminiSelectors) {
      const el = document.querySelector(sel);
      if (el && el.offsetParent !== null) {
        return el;
      }
    }
    const aiStudioSelectors = [
      "ms-autosize-textarea textarea",
      'textarea[aria-label*="prompt" i]',
      'textarea[placeholder*="prompt" i]',
      "textarea.prompt-textarea",
      "textarea"
    ];
    for (const sel of aiStudioSelectors) {
      const el = document.querySelector(sel);
      if (el && el.offsetParent !== null) {
        return el;
      }
    }
    return document.querySelector('textarea, div[contenteditable="true"]');
  }
  function injectRepoMixParts(parts) {
    if (typeof window === "undefined" || typeof document === "undefined") return false;
    if (!parts || parts.length === 0) return false;
    const inputEl = findPromptInputElement();
    const target = inputEl || document.activeElement || document.body;
    try {
      const dataTransfer = new DataTransfer();
      for (const part of parts) {
        const file = new File([part.xmlContent], part.fileName, { type: "text/plain" });
        dataTransfer.items.add(file);
      }
      const pasteEvent = new ClipboardEvent("paste", {
        clipboardData: dataTransfer,
        bubbles: true,
        cancelable: true
      });
      target.dispatchEvent(pasteEvent);
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[Gemini Bridge Injector] Failed to inject repo mix parts:", err);
      return false;
    }
  }
  function injectRepoMixFile(xmlContent, fileName = "repo-mix.txt") {
    return injectRepoMixParts([{ fileName, xmlContent }]);
  }
  function injectPromptText(promptText, autoSubmit = false) {
    if (typeof document === "undefined") return false;
    const inputEl = findPromptInputElement();
    if (!inputEl) {
      console.warn("[Gemini Bridge Injector] Prompt input element not found");
      return false;
    }
    inputEl.focus();
    let inserted = false;
    try {
      inserted = document.execCommand("insertText", false, promptText);
    } catch (e) {
      inserted = false;
    }
    if (!inserted) {
      if (inputEl instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement || "value" in inputEl) {
        const textarea = inputEl;
        const start = textarea.selectionStart ?? textarea.value.length;
        const end = textarea.selectionEnd ?? textarea.value.length;
        textarea.value = textarea.value.slice(0, start) + promptText + textarea.value.slice(end);
        textarea.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
        textarea.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
      } else {
        inputEl.textContent = (inputEl.textContent || "") + promptText;
        inputEl.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent("input", { bubbles: true, inputType: "insertText", data: promptText }));
      }
    }
    if (autoSubmit) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        const sendButtonSelectors = [
          "button.send-button",
          'button[aria-label*="Send" i]',
          'button[aria-label*="Senden" i]',
          'button[aria-label*="Run" i]',
          "ms-run-button button",
          "button.run-button"
        ];
        for (const sel of sendButtonSelectors) {
          const btn = document.querySelector(sel);
          if (btn && !btn.disabled) {
            btn.click();
            break;
          }
        }
      }, 300);
    }
    return true;
  }

  // packages/userscript-hud/src/services/selfHealing.ts
  var MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES = 3;
  function buildSelfHealingPrompt(errors, repo, branch, headOid, retryAttempt) {
    const formattedErrors = errors.map((err, idx) => {
      const loc = err.line ? err.column ? `:${err.line}:${err.column}` : `:${err.line}` : "";
      const snippet = err.codeSnippet ? `
   > ${err.codeSnippet}` : "";
      return `${idx + 1}. [Stage: ${err.stage}] ${err.file}${loc} \u2014 ${err.message}${snippet}`;
    }).join("\n");
    return `#solve Linter & Preflight Self-Healing Loop (Retry ${retryAttempt}/${MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES})

The previous patch for repository "${repo}" on branch "${branch}" was rejected during Edge-Worker preflight validation.
Expected HEAD commit: "${headOid}".

Aggregated Preflight Errors (${errors.length}):
${formattedErrors}

Fix Instructions:
1. Fix ALL listed syntax and rule errors in a single cohesive response.
2. Hard Guardrail: NEVER use placeholder comments like "// ... existing code ...", "/* remaining code */", or "# ... existing code ...". Always provide complete search/replace blocks or full file content.
3. Provide your correction as a single valid JSON codeblock matching the GeminiPatchPayload schema:
\`\`\`json
{
  "commitMessage": "fix: resolve preflight linter errors (retry ${retryAttempt})",
  "patches": [
    {
      "path": "path/to/file.ext",
      "action": "modify",
      "blocks": [
        {
          "search": "exact string to replace",
          "replace": "replacement string"
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
\`\`\`
`;
  }
  function shouldTriggerSelfHealing(response, autoPilot, currentRetryCount) {
    if (response.status === "COMMITTED") {
      return {
        trigger: false,
        nextRetryCount: 0,
        reason: "COMMITTED_SUCCESS"
      };
    }
    if (response.status !== "PREFLIGHT_FAILED") {
      return {
        trigger: false,
        nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount,
        reason: "STATUS_NOT_PREFLIGHT_FAILED"
      };
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(response.errors) || response.errors.length === 0) {
      return {
        trigger: false,
        nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount,
        reason: "NO_ERRORS_REPORTED"
      };
    }
    if (currentRetryCount >= MAX_SELF_HEALING_RETRIES) {
      return {
        trigger: false,
        nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount,
        reason: "MAX_RETRIES_EXCEEDED"
      };
    }
    if (!autoPilot) {
      return {
        trigger: false,
        nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount,
        reason: "AUTOPILOT_DISABLED"
      };
    }
    return {
      trigger: true,
      nextRetryCount: currentRetryCount + 1,
      reason: "PREFLIGHT_FAILED_AUTO_RETRY"
    };
  }
  function executeSelfHealingTurn(response, cfg, state, autoSubmit = true) {
    const evalResult = shouldTriggerSelfHealing(response, cfg.autoPilot, state.retryCount);
    if (!evalResult.trigger) {
      return {
        triggered: false,
        nextRetryCount: evalResult.nextRetryCount,
        reason: evalResult.reason
      };
    }
    const prompt = buildSelfHealingPrompt(
      response.errors || [],
      cfg.repo,
      cfg.branch,
      state.headOid,
      evalResult.nextRetryCount
    );
    injectPromptText(prompt, autoSubmit);
    return {
      triggered: true,
      nextRetryCount: evalResult.nextRetryCount,
      prompt,
      reason: evalResult.reason
    };
  }

  var HUD_SHADOW_STYLES = HUD_STYLES;
  function createHudElement(tag, className, textContent) {
    const el = document.createElement(tag);
    if (className) el.className = className;
    if (textContent !== void 0) el.textContent = textContent;
    return el;
  }
  function buildHudDomTree(config, state) {
    const existing = document.getElementById("gemini-edge-hud-root");
    if (existing) {
      existing.remove();
    }
    const host = createHudElement("div");
    host.id = "gemini-edge-hud-root";
    host.style.cssText = "all: initial; position: fixed; z-index: 2147483647;";
    document.body.appendChild(host);
    const shadowRoot = host.attachShadow({ mode: "open" });
    const styleEl = createHudElement("style");
    styleEl.textContent = HUD_STYLES;
    shadowRoot.appendChild(styleEl);
    const trigger = createHudElement("div", "hud-trigger");
    const triggerIcon = createHudElement("span", void 0, "\u26A1");
    const triggerLabel = createHudElement("span", void 0, "Gemini Edge HUD");
    const triggerBadge = createHudElement("span", "hud-trigger-badge idle", "IDLE");
    trigger.appendChild(triggerIcon);
    trigger.appendChild(triggerLabel);
    trigger.appendChild(triggerBadge);
    shadowRoot.appendChild(trigger);
    const panel = createHudElement("div", "hud-panel");
    const header = createHudElement("div", "hud-header");
    const title = createHudElement("div", "hud-title", "\u26A1 Cloudflare Edge HUD (Stage 3)");
    const closeBtn = createHudElement("button", "hud-close-btn", "\xD7");
    closeBtn.title = "Close Panel";
    header.appendChild(title);
    header.appendChild(closeBtn);
    panel.appendChild(header);
    const body = createHudElement("div", "hud-body");
    const repoRow = createHudElement("div", "hud-form-row");
    const repoLabel = createHudElement("span", "hud-label", "GitHub Repository (owner/repo)");

    repoRow.appendChild(repoLabel);
